# YuNet偶发超限：独立代码与证据审查任务

你是一名负责嵌入式Linux、Python/C++并发、VART/DPU推理和性能诊断的代码审查工程师。请对附件中的AURA/KV260视觉运行链进行独立、全面审查，重点解释YuNet偶发超过30ms的原因，检查保护/恢复机制和测量是否可靠，并提出最小、可验证的改进方案。

这是一项审查任务。请先阅读实际源码与数值证据，再形成结论；不要顺着此前分析认定GIL、硬件或USB就是根因。如果无法访问附件或其中缺少必要文件，明确说明缺少什么，不要假装读过本地路径、运行过程序或连接过板卡。可给出补丁建议和实验方案，不直接操作生产服务。

## 一、背景与最新状态

- 平台：AMD/Xilinx KV260，当前DPU fingerprint为0x101000056010407。历史板端检查为Linux5.15.36、Python3.9.9/aarch64；确切VART/XIR二进制版本、绑定源码和编译选项尚未在本审查包中完整核验，不要假定与任意网上版本一致。
- 业务：单一相机采集，YOLOX-Nano人体检测约10Hz，YuNet人脸检测和SFace身份特征共享同一个DPU owner与进程内调度器；GUI/音频是其他进程。请区分同进程线程的GIL竞争与其他进程造成的CPU/内存竞争。
- 当前正式版本unit SHA256：a29082bae1d382d7a43505d5a1d3a51fa2203081423755900e9d72836385e343。三个视觉模型均已正式使用板端DPU，身份后端DPU_SFACE_INT8；“SFace还在Windows”的旧资料已过期。
- 源码与证据状态基于2026-10-23 UTC08:12～08:19附近的只读记录，不能理解为审查当下的在线实读。
- 本版YuNet累计10678次native完成，2次硬占用超限、native_failed为0；恢复faults2/attempts2/recoveries2，当前RUNNING且disabled=false。YOLO约9.94Hz，SFace1528次完成/超限0；逐发布TTL gap累计0。主周期超限累计1次，是接受阶段已有计数，后续该记录未增加。
- 一次约3秒背景CPU采样：整板62.61%，相机进程组19.46%，GUI进程组36.35%，Xserver组1.92%，四核整板100%为分母。这是剖析线索，不是严格性能对照。另一个历史15秒三模型窗口DPU任务busy约6.28%，不是MAC利用率或PL静态资源占用率。

## 二、“超限”在本项目中的精确定义

请根据源码重新核实以下定义，发现与实际不符时指出。

1. DpuVisionSchedule.optional()准入后设active并记started；退出时以monotonic墙钟计算elapsed。elapsed>0.030秒为hardcap_exceeded；超过next_primary-guard的结束期限为guard_exceeded。guard当前5ms，初始估计预算20ms，观测后可提高到最多30ms。
2. 30ms是项目软件对可选任务持有调度执行权的占用保护，不是已证明的DPU硬件计算时间，也不是VART可以在30ms处被中断的硬件超时。它是在调用返回后检查；不可据此推断在途作业已取消。
3. budget_exceeded（估计偏差）、hardcap/guard故障、未获得时隙、提交前取消、native非零状态、源帧TTL过期和相机断帧，是不同事件，必须分别统计。
4. 该占用区间可能包含Python包装、诊断采样、Condition/Lock、调度停顿、native调用与wait。请特别核对“记录的elapsed结束”与“真正active=None/notify_all释放”是否是同一时间，finish诊断是否延长未记录的持有区间。

## 三、两个真实故障，必须分别解释

原始时间戳与全部诊断字段在evidence/latest_yunet_faults.json；derived_fault_phases_ms是从单调时钟端点重算的结果，不能当新增观测。

| 字段 | 故障A，event6326 | 故障B，event10528 |
|---|---:|---:|
| scheduler_occupancy_ms | 30.692017 | 31.162401 |
| optional_wall_ms，另一计时边界 | 30.202292 | 31.206052 |
| optional调用线程CPU ms | 2.245852 | 2.090762 |
| optional trace起点到execute_start | 22.709727 | 0.281563 |
| execute_wall_ms | 4.099281 | 30.220712 |
| execute调用线程CPU ms | 1.000611 | 1.122652 |
| execute返回到wait开始 | 2.998700 | 0.356684 |
| wait_wall_ms | 0.160092 | 0.095521 |
| wait结束到optional trace结束 | 0.234492 | 0.251572 |
| native_status / completed | 0 / 1 | 0 / 1 |
| guard_exceeded / waiting_primary | 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 |

两次均完成推理、未越guard、故障记录当时无等待中的YOLO，之后都自动恢复。相同“30ms超限”对应两种明显不同的时间分布：A主要停在execute前，B主要停在execute墙钟区间。请不要将它们合并成“模型本身需要31ms”。也不能因当时waiting_primary=0就认为所有端到端期限已获证明。

当前线程CPU不包括其他native/runtime/内核线程；wall减去该线程CPU不能直接当作GIL等待、runqueue时间或硬件执行时间。当前观测主要为getrusage上下文切换计数，没有runqueue等待时长。两次故障对应的新USB内核窗口未在本次最新探针中采集；历史另一次31.340ms故障曾确认其窗口匹配USB告警0，不能把历史结论套到本次两条故障。

## 四、已改正的问题与仍需审查的边界

- 已撤回的首版通知候选曾在提交前抢相机共享状态锁，抢不到就AdmissionCancelled('busy')。468次尝试中355次busy取消，仅113次发布、14段TTL gap、最长3751ms，但DPU hardcap超限0。当前用独立取消Event检查close/source TTL/deadline，图像/跟踪发布仍走原共享锁；当前busy0/gap0。请检查该修复的并发和关闭语义，但不要把旧缺陷当成当前仍未修。
- 原重试存在25ms退让、最多2次重试、200ms轮次期限、失败后50ms/成功后200ms等待。当前另有最多100ms的时隙通知等待，在DPU关键区外等待并在醒来后重新准入；不要把等待100ms直接说成占用DPU100ms。
- YuNet已实现：仅对明确native完成/status0、仅hardcap而未越guard的事件，冷却10秒后有条件探测，10分钟最多2个探测周期，连续3次正常完成恢复。故障计数保留。检查恢复资格、原子性、错误路径及状态机，不能以“恢复成功”代替“故障根因消除”。
- SFace尚未发生本版真实超限，但源码只给YuNet配了恢复策略；SFace disabled后可能持续defer，而顶层ready主要反映初始化状态。这是待审查的可用性缺口，不能说已经发生SFace失效。禁止在单次向量级回退到Windows FP32旧模板空间；应另行评估明确停用、恢复或整套配置/库协调回退。
- 历史出现过Camera stopped producing frames、MCP01 USB播放告警；当前三服务active/零重启。已做协调关闭PCM再切相机，仍未定位这些历史故障根因，不要将它们自动等同本次YuNet长尾。
- 历史摘要有两类统计误读已纠正：354次raw native_failed对应提交前撤回，非354次硬件执行失败；某60秒基线gap累计6→6，新增是0而非6。请主动审查同类“准入次数/真正提交/完成/发布”“累计/窗口增量”混淆。

## 五、请逐项审查

### A. 调用链、计时和观测自身影响

重建完整调用链与锁/执行权持有关系：FaceTargetService→DpuYuNet→encode/pre_execute→PinnedRunner→调度optional→execute_async/wait→native_completed→诊断finish→输出反量化/解码→发布。YOLO和SFace也纳入同一个时序图。

逐一标出scheduler started、diagnostic begin、execute_start/return、wait_start/end、native_completed、occupancy计算、diagnostic finish、active释放端点；解释两种optional墙钟为何不同，是否存在漏计、重计、端点漂移或锁等待归属不清。

核查诊断开始/结束、thread_probe.sample、健康snapshot复制/排序是否在关键路径上持锁；是否可能在计时器之外增加真实占用。当前观测曾有平均约0.264ms合成额外开销合格，不能据均值证明故障时开销很小。提出低干扰、可关闭、成本可校验的补充测量，而非增加大量同步日志。

### B. 并发、优先级与调度

审查Condition/Lock/RLock顺序、GIL边界、状态共享和snapshot反向依赖，寻找具体死锁/长持锁/竞态路径，并给出可复现交错，不笼统说“有锁就慢”。

检查next_primary在什么时机更新；YOLO的started是否先于等待新图像，真实唤醒/截止/睡眠是否一致；optional预算估计只上升而不下降是否影响准入及饥饿。检查通知等待的唤醒、抢占窗口、时隙重新准入、源TTL/关闭信号、轮次deadline和YuNet/SFace公平性。保留YOLO优先这一业务目标。

检查共享最新帧、在途输入/输出缓冲生命周期、runner跨线程使用、owner独占、异常和close路径；预后处理/跟踪是否不必要地持有DPU执行权。对VART绑定是否释放GIL、execute_async是否立即返回，只能基于匹配版本的代码/资料或实测下结论，不能靠函数名猜测。

### C. 保护、故障恢复和健康可见性

审查30ms硬占用/5ms guard与真实业务期限的关系：它们分别防什么、当前故障造成了什么可观测影响。可以评价策略是否合理，但不能把简单放宽阈值、删保护或换计时边界当成根因修复。

审查YuNet的冷却、探测额度、native完成证明、状态恢复、主任务异常、未知错误、提交前取消和重启计数。检查CPU fallback与CPU实际负载、结果时效。单独审查SFace disabled/defer/health/ready传播和身份空间完整性。

### D. 故障假设与最小区分实验

对至少这些候选机制分别给出支持证据、反证/缺失证据、可否解释A/B、置信度和最小区分实验：Python/GIL执行竞争；Linux唤醒/抢占与CPU排队；诊断/健康接口锁竞争；VART调用内部同步；驱动/设备执行等待；CPU/内存带宽及音视频并发；过宽的调度执行权范围。

优先设计约1～3分钟能反馈的单变量实验，长期证据随正常运行积累，不让每轮开发都等两小时。固定模型、输入、频率、UI状态、音频状态和观测配置；发生超限先留证，恢复不得清故障计数。对稀有事件未复现，结论只能是该窗口未复现。

评价几类候选：减小观测/锁临界区、复用缓冲、原生execute/wait最小对照、单独DPU执行进程、GIL间隔/CPU亲和性。先列证据门槛与代价，再选最小方案；不要默认C++或独立进程一定有效，也不要同时修改多项后无法归因。生产owner与测试owner不得同时争抢DPU。

CPU优化必须先找GUI和相机函数/阶段热点。GUI独立进程高CPU是调度/内存竞争线索，不直接证明与相机共享同一个GIL。利用率、有效吞吐、源帧龄、准确率、CPU核秒应同时报告，不用空推理制造DPU忙碌率。

## 六、资料范围与可信度

1. current_source/：16份当前部署回读源码，均与正式ACTIVE_RELEASE中的active_source_files逐字节SHA匹配。
2. release_pinned_source/：上述回读没有覆盖、但正式candidate_pins明确固定的其他源码；与发布PIN核验匹配，区别于“本次重新从实板读取”。
3. supporting_source/：继承阶段的pipeline_metrics/thread_sched_metrics及相关业务源码，逐份列出来源和SHA；没有本次完整实板PIN，不能无条件认定为当前部署字节。若结论取决于这些文件，明确要求补齐版本证据。
4. evidence/：仅保留调度、故障、CPU和状态的过滤数值；当前故障原始单调时间戳完整保留。source_index.json、SHA256SUMS.json记录来源与包内哈希。
5. tests_reference/：既有故障注入/集成/计时测试供审查，不代表本审查包可独立部署，环境与缺失依赖需另核实。不要将测试用例存在当作已经执行，更不能当作板端实测。
6. 审查包不含VART安装二进制/绑定源码、完整相机/GUI运行环境、模型权重、真人图像/音频、特征或身份库。无法从附件证明的结论保持待验证，列出最小补充清单。

## 七、输出格式

请用中文，先给最重要结论，再按以下顺序输出：

1. 一段普通用户能理解的解释：本项目的“YuNet偶发超限”究竟是什么，已知影响与未知原因分别是什么。
2. 两个故障A/B的时间线和计时边界解释；哪些归因已可排除、哪些仅证据不足。
3. 审查发现表：优先级、文件/函数/行号、具体触发交错、后果、源码/证据依据、置信度、已修/仍存在/仅风险。不要为凑数量罗列泛化建议。
4. 根因假设表，明确支持与反证/缺失证据；独立核验此前“锁/GIL/原生改写”判断，指出其中可能错误的地方。
5. 最优先的三个实验：改什么、保持什么、测什么、预期两种结果如何区分假设、失败如何恢复。给出真正可用的伪代码/补丁轮廓、故障注入测试和所需环境；未知参数留待核实。
6. 最小修复方案、替代架构代价及验收清单；分别验收可靠性、业务时效、CPU收益与身份准确率。不可清计数、仅放宽阈值或移走计时边界来伪造通过。
7. 最多10项关键缺失信息，按价值排序，具体到文件/版本/字段/采样边界。最后列出前一轮分析中需纠正的事实或统计口径。

如果你引用Linux、CPython、VART的外部实现，请给匹配版本的官方文档/源码位置，并区分“附件直接证明”“外部资料支持”“推断”“待实验验证”。不要将本任务提示词本身作为证明结论的证据。


以下为附件源码与数值证据。源文件行号从1开始；身份/模型数据未附。

# DeepSeek YuNet全面审查资料

用途：将本目录的完整提示词和源码/过滤故障资料交给DeepSeek，独立审查YuNet偶发30ms占用超限。资料依据UTC2026-10-23最新三模型DPU正式版整理，早期SFace仍Windows的状态已更新。

使用方式：上传`DeepSeek_YuNet审查资料.txt`，并发送“请按文件开头的完整任务提示词，阅读后面的源码与证据，完成独立审查”。如果客户端支持解压读取附件，也可上传`DeepSeek_YuNet审查包.zip`和[完整提示词](给DeepSeek的完整提示词.md)。若模型无法读取ZIP，使用合并TXT；不要假定仅提供本机路径即可让对方访问。

`source_index.json`区分当前实板回读源码、发布PIN固定源码、未经本次实板PIN确认的辅助源码与测试参考。所有包内文件的SHA见`SHA256SUMS.json`。只读代码审查，不执行或部署包内入口。

本包包含16份与现役导出SHA匹配的源码、7份其他发布PIN固定源码、6份明确标注未有本轮实板PIN的辅助源码和7份测试参考；36份Python仅做AST语法核验，没有运行所附业务测试。两条真实故障的时间分段已从端点重算。合并TXT约455KiB，ZIP约130KiB；具体检查结果见`CHECKS.json`。附件内容较长时，先读提示词、故障JSON和调度/runner/指标源码，再继续关联身份及参考测试。

本次只准备本地提示词和审查资料，没有向DeepSeek或其他外部服务发送内容，没有连接板卡、执行新推理或改变生产配置。资料不含模型权重、现场图像/音频、身份特征、数据库、密钥或原始UART。

0057:         from board.identity.sface_producer import producer_configuration
0058:         producer_configuration(config)
0059:     return config

0061: 
0062: def face_for_target(faces, tracks, key, *, minimum_score=.9, minimum_pixels=48):
0063:     """Reject overlapping ownership/multiple faces instead of choosing nearest."""
0064:     candidates = []
0065:     selected = key['track_id'], key['track_generation']
0066:     for face in faces:
0067:         if len(face) != 15 or not all(math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in face):
0068:             continue
0069:         x, y, w, h = map(float, face[:4])
0070:         if min(w, h) < minimum_pixels or face[14] < minimum_score:
0071:             continue
0072:         owners = []
0073:         for t in tracks:
0074:             if not t['visible']:
0075:                 continue
0076:             tx, ty, tw, th = map(float, t['box'])
0077:             intersection = max(0, min(x+w, tx+tw)-max(x, tx))*max(0, min(y+h, ty+th)-max(y, ty))
0078:             if tw > 0 and th > 0 and tx <= x+w/2 <= tx+tw and ty <= y+h/2 <= ty+.65*th and intersection/(w*h) >= .9:
0079:                 owners.append((t['track_id'], t['generation']))
0080:         if selected in owners:
0081:             if len(owners) != 1:
0082:                 return None, '人脸对应多个人体，请分开站立后重试'
0083:             candidates.append(face)
0084:     if len(candidates) != 1:
0085:         return None, '当前目标有多张人脸，请重新选择' if candidates else '未检测到合格人脸，请靠近并正对摄像头'
0086:     return candidates[0], ''
0087:

0137:     def extract(self, image, observation, key):
0138:         self.preview_png = None
0139:         self.enrollment_quality = None
0140:         if observation.get('target_kind')=='face':
0141:             face=observation.get('face');reason='等待当前目标的清晰正面人脸'
0142:             if (observation.get('face_target')!=key or face is None or len(face)!=15
0143:                     or not all(math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in face)
0144:                     or min(face[2:4])<self.config['minimum_face_pixels'] or face[14]<self.config['minimum_face_score']):face=None
0145:         else:
0146:             if self.detector is None:return None,'人脸目标来源不正确'
0147:             face, reason = face_for_target(self.detector.detect(image), observation['tracks'], key,
0148:                 minimum_score=self.config['minimum_face_score'], minimum_pixels=self.config['minimum_face_pixels'])
0149:         if face is None:
0150:             return None, reason
0151:         crop = self.align_crop(image, face)
0152:         variance = float(self.cv2.Laplacian(self.cv2.cvtColor(crop, self.cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY), self.cv2.CV_64F).var())
0153:         if not math.isfinite(variance) or variance < self.config['minimum_blur_variance']:
0154:             return None, '人脸较模糊，请保持正对摄像头'
0155:         from board.identity.sampling import pose_quality
0156:         self.enrollment_quality = pose_quality(face, variance)
0157:         shadow_without_reference = (self.shadow is not None and
0158:             not (self.config.get('dpu_sface_shadow') or {}).get('compare_cosine', True))
0159:         if shadow_without_reference and not self.producer_enabled:
0160:             # Independent compute evidence does not require Windows online.
0161:             # The primary request/error still follows the original path below.
0162:             self._offer_shadow(crop, observation, key, None)
0163:         ok, encoded = self.cv2.imencode('.png', crop)
0164:         if ok and encoded.nbytes <= 65536:
0165:             self.preview_png = encoded.tobytes()
0166:         if self.local_dpu:
0167:             # The store contract accepts ordinary finite Python scalars.
0168:             # The DPU decoder returns numpy.float32 values.
0169:             vector = tuple(float(v) for v in self.local_dpu.feature(
0170:                 crop, source_mono_ms=observation['captured_mono_ms']))
0171:         elif self.remote:
0172:             if not self.preview_png:raise ValueError('Could not encode face-only input')
0173:             vector=self.remote.feature(self.preview_png,key,observation['frame_id'],observation['captured_mono_ms'])
0174:         else:vector=tuple(float(v) for v in self.embedder.feature(crop))
0175:         if self.shadow is not None and not shadow_without_reference and not self.producer_enabled:
0176:             self._offer_shadow(crop, observation, key, vector)
0177:         return vector, ''
0178: 
0179:     def _offer_shadow(self, crop, observation, key, reference):
0180:         try:
0181:             quality = {'quality':self.enrollment_quality} if self.calibration is not None else {}
0182:             self.shadow.offer(crop, observation['frame_id'], observation['captured_mono_ms'], key, reference, **quality)
0183:         except Exception as exc:
0184:             self.shadow_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]

0186:     def close(self):
0187:         producer_stopped = True
0188:         if self.producer is not None:
0189:             try:
0190:                 producer_stopped = self.producer.close(timeout=2)
0191:             except Exception as exc:
0192:                 producer_stopped = False
0193:                 self.shadow_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]
0194:         if self.shadow is not None:
0195:             try:
0196:                 self.shadow_shutdown_timeout = not self.shadow.close(timeout=2)
0197:             except Exception as exc:
0198:                 self.shadow_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]
0199:                 self.shadow_shutdown_timeout = True
0200:         if self.local_dpu:
0201:             self.local_dpu.close()
0202:         self.shadow_shutdown_timeout = self.shadow_shutdown_timeout or not producer_stopped
0203:         return not self.shadow_shutdown_timeout
0204: 
0205:     def shutdown_ready(self):
0206:         return ((self.producer is None or self.producer.stopped.is_set())
0207:                 and (self.shadow is None or self.shadow.stopped.is_set()))

0209:     def health(self):
0210:         if self.shadow is not None or self.shadow_error:
0211:             try:
0212:                 if self.calibration is not None and self.shadow is not None:
0213:                     self.calibration.observe_target(self.shadow.current_target())
0214:                 shadow = self.shadow.health() if self.shadow is not None else {'enabled':True,'ready':False}
0215:                 return {'shadow':shadow,'auxiliary_error':self.shadow_error,
0216:                         'producer':self.producer.health() if self.producer is not None else {'enabled':False},
0217:                         'shadow_shutdown_timeout':self.shadow_shutdown_timeout,
0218:                         'calibration':self.calibration.health() if self.calibration is not None else {'enabled':False}}
0219:             except Exception as exc:
0220:                 return {'shadow':{'enabled':True,'ready':False},'auxiliary_error':type(exc).__name__[:64],
0221:                         'shadow_shutdown_timeout':self.shadow_shutdown_timeout}
0222:         return self.local_dpu.health() if self.local_dpu else None

0001: """Bounded count/source-age recorder. Per-attempt wall/CPU durations are omitted."""
0002: import threading
0003: from collections import deque
0004: 
0005: SCHEMA = 'aura-face-freshness-light-3'
0006: GAP_RECORD_CAPACITY = 32
0007: GAP_COUNTERS = ('offers', 'attempts', 'slot_unavailable', 'cancelled_busy',
0008:                 'cancelled_deadline', 'cancelled_expired')
0009: LIMIT = (1 << 63) - 1
0010: COUNTERS = ('offers', 'offers_accepted', 'offers_closed', 'latest_replaced', 'attempts',
0011:     'attempts_finished', 'active_attempts', 'slot_unavailable', 'input_ttl_discarded',
0012:     'publish_ttl_discarded', 'closed_after_detect_discarded', 'published',
0013:     'published_positive', 'published_negative', 'published_multiface', 'process_errors',
0014:     'callback_errors', 'consecutive_slot_rejections', 'maximum_consecutive_slot_rejections',
0015:     'invalid_source_ages', 'source_clock_pairs_rejected', 'definite_clipped_gap_intervals',
0016:     'observed_gap_intervals', 'post_commit_at_or_over_ttl', 'events_evicted', 'counter_saturation',
0017:     'cancelled', 'cancelled_closed', 'cancelled_expired', 'cancelled_deadline',
0018:     'cancelled_disabled', 'cancelled_busy', 'cancelled_other')
0019: EVENT_CODES = {'slot_unavailable': 1, 'input_ttl': 2, 'publish_ttl': 3,
0020:     'closed_after_detect': 4, 'gap': 5, 'invalid_source': 6, 'process_error': 7, 'closed': 8,
0021:     'cancelled': 9}
0022: CANCEL_REASON_CODES = {'other':0, 'closed':1, 'expired':2, 'deadline':3, 'disabled':4, 'busy':5}

0025: def integer(value):
0026:     return type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= LIMIT

0029: class FaceFreshnessMetrics:
0030:     def __init__(self, *, ttl_ms, event_capacity=16):
0031:         if not integer(ttl_ms) or not 1 <= ttl_ms <= 60000:
0032:             raise ValueError('Invalid TTL')
0033:         if type(event_capacity) is not int or not 1 <= event_capacity <= 64:
0034:             raise ValueError('Invalid event capacity')
0035:         self.ttl_ms, self.capacity = ttl_ms, event_capacity
0036:         self.lock = threading.Lock()
0037:         self.counters = dict.fromkeys(COUNTERS, 0)
0038:         self.closed = False
0039:         self.previous = None
0040:         self.events = deque(maxlen=event_capacity)
0041:         # Preserve rare freshness gaps independently from the high-volume
0042:         # mixed event ring. These records reuse timestamps already sampled
0043:         # by the business path; they add no per-attempt clock reads.
0044:         self.gap_records = []
0045:         self.gap_records_dropped = 0
0046:         self.summaries = {name: {'count': 0, 'total_ms': 0, 'min_ms': None, 'max_ms': None, 'last_ms': None}
0047:             for name in ('input_source_age_ms', 'pre_commit_source_age_ms',
0048:                          'post_commit_source_age_ms', 'post_commit_interval_ms',
0049:                          'clipped_gap_lower_bound_ms', 'clipped_gap_upper_bound_ms')}
0050: 
0051:     def inc(self, name, amount=1):
0052:         total = self.counters[name] + amount
0053:         if total > LIMIT:
0054:             total = LIMIT
0055:             if name != 'counter_saturation':
0056:                 self.counters['counter_saturation'] = min(LIMIT, self.counters['counter_saturation'] + 1)
0057:         self.counters[name] = total
0058: 
0059:     def event(self, kind, now=None, **values):
0060:         if len(self.events) == self.capacity:
0061:             self.inc('events_evicted')
0062:         self.events.append(dict(event_code=EVENT_CODES[kind], source_observed_ms=now, **values))
0063: 
0064:     def summary(self, name, value):
0065:         if not integer(value):
0066:             self.inc('invalid_source_ages')
0067:             return
0068:         row = self.summaries[name]
0069:         if row['count'] == LIMIT or row['total_ms'] > LIMIT - value:
0070:             self.inc('counter_saturation')
0071:             return
0072:         row['count'] += 1
0073:         row['total_ms'] += value
0074:         row['min_ms'] = value if row['min_ms'] is None else min(row['min_ms'], value)
0075:         row['max_ms'] = value if row['max_ms'] is None else max(row['max_ms'], value)
0076:         row['last_ms'] = value
0077: 
0078:     def mark(self):
0079:         # The existing service hook calls mark; no clock or lock is needed.
0080:         return None
0081: 
0082:     def offer(self, *, accepted, replaced):
0083:         with self.lock:
0084:             if self.closed:
0085:                 return
0086:             self.inc('offers')
0087:             self.inc('offers_accepted' if accepted else 'offers_closed')
0088:             if accepted and replaced:
0089:                 self.inc('latest_replaced')
0090: 
0091:     def begin(self, stamp_ms):
0092:         with self.lock:
0093:             if self.closed:
0094:                 return None
0095:             self.inc('attempts')
0096:             self.inc('active_attempts')
0097:         return {'stamp_ms': stamp_ms, 'input_now_ms': None, 'pre_commit_check_now_ms': None,
0098:                 'outcome_code': 0, 'finished': False, 'publication_observed': False,
0099:                 'callback_started': False, 'callback_completed': False}
0100: 
0101:     def publish(self, token, source_now_ms, face_count):
0102:         if type(token) is not dict or type(face_count) is not int or not 0 <= face_count <= 16:
0103:             raise ValueError('Invalid numeric observation')
0104:         with self.lock:
0105:             if self.closed or token['publication_observed']:
0106:                 return
0107:             token['publication_observed'] = True
0108:             self.inc('published_positive' if face_count else 'published_negative')
0109:             if face_count > 1:
0110:                 self.inc('published_multiface')
0111:             stamp, checked = token.get('stamp_ms'), token.get('pre_commit_check_now_ms')
0112:             if not (integer(stamp) and integer(checked) and integer(source_now_ms)
0113:                     and stamp <= checked <= source_now_ms):
0114:                 self.inc('invalid_source_ages')
0115:                 self.event('invalid_source')
0116:                 self.previous = None
0117:                 return
0118:             self.summary('pre_commit_source_age_ms', checked - stamp)
0119:             self.summary('post_commit_source_age_ms', source_now_ms - stamp)
0120:             if source_now_ms - stamp >= self.ttl_ms:
0121:                 self.inc('post_commit_at_or_over_ttl')
0122:             previous = self.previous
0123:             if previous is not None:
0124:                 old_stamp, old_observed, old_counts = previous
0125:                 if old_observed <= checked <= source_now_ms and old_stamp <= stamp:
0126:                     self.summary('post_commit_interval_ms', source_now_ms - old_observed)
0127:                     anchor = max(old_stamp + self.ttl_ms, old_observed)
0128:                     lower, upper = max(0, checked - anchor), max(0, source_now_ms - anchor)
0129:                     self.summary('clipped_gap_lower_bound_ms', lower)
0130:                     self.summary('clipped_gap_upper_bound_ms', upper)
0131:                     if lower:
0132:                         self.inc('definite_clipped_gap_intervals')
0133:                     if upper:
0134:                         self.inc('observed_gap_intervals')
0135:                         self.event('gap', source_now_ms, lower_ms=lower, upper_ms=upper)
0136:                         if len(self.gap_records) < GAP_RECORD_CAPACITY:
0137:                             record = {'previous_source_stamp_ms': old_stamp,
0138:                                 'previous_observed_ms': old_observed, 'current_source_stamp_ms': stamp,
0139:                                 'pre_commit_age_ms': checked - stamp,
0140:                                 'post_commit_age_ms': source_now_ms - stamp,
0141:                                 'interval_ms': source_now_ms - old_observed,
0142:                                 'lower_ms': lower, 'upper_ms': upper}
0143:                             record.update((name + '_since_previous', self.counters[name] - old_counts[index])
0144:                                           for index, name in enumerate(GAP_COUNTERS))
0145:                             self.gap_records.append(record)
0146:                         elif self.gap_records_dropped < LIMIT:
0147:                             self.gap_records_dropped += 1
0148:                 else:
0149:                     self.inc('source_clock_pairs_rejected')
0150:             self.previous = (stamp, source_now_ms, tuple(self.counters[name] for name in GAP_COUNTERS))
0151: 
0152:     def finish(self, token):
0153:         if type(token) is not dict:
0154:             return
0155:         with self.lock:
0156:             if self.closed or token['finished']:
0157:                 return
0158:             token['finished'] = True
0159:             self.inc('attempts_finished')
0160:             self.counters['active_attempts'] = max(0, self.counters['active_attempts'] - 1)
0161:             now, stamp = token.get('input_now_ms'), token.get('stamp_ms')
0162:             if integer(now) and integer(stamp) and now >= stamp:
0163:                 self.summary('input_source_age_ms', now - stamp)
0164:             else:
0165:                 self.inc('invalid_source_ages')
0166:             code = token.get('outcome_code')
0167:             reason = {1: ('input_ttl_discarded', 'input_ttl'), 2: ('slot_unavailable', 'slot_unavailable'),
0168:                 3: ('publish_ttl_discarded', 'publish_ttl'), 4: ('closed_after_detect_discarded', 'closed_after_detect'),
0169:                 5: ('published', None), 6: ('cancelled', 'cancelled')}.get(code, ('process_errors', 'process_error'))
0170:             self.inc(reason[0])
0171:             if code == 6:
0172:                 cancellation = token.get('cancel_reason_code', 0)
0173:                 name = next((name for name,value in CANCEL_REASON_CODES.items()
0174:                     if type(cancellation) is int and value == cancellation), 'other')
0175:                 self.inc('cancelled_'+name)
0176:                 self.event('cancelled', now if integer(now) else None, cancel_reason_code=CANCEL_REASON_CODES[name])
0177:             elif reason[1]:
0178:                 self.event(reason[1], now if integer(now) else None)
0179:             if token.get('callback_started') and not token.get('callback_completed'):
0180:                 self.inc('callback_errors')
0181:             if code == 2:
0182:                 self.inc('consecutive_slot_rejections')
0183:                 self.counters['maximum_consecutive_slot_rejections'] = max(
0184:                     self.counters['maximum_consecutive_slot_rejections'], self.counters['consecutive_slot_rejections'])
0185:             else:
0186:                 self.counters['consecutive_slot_rejections'] = 0
0187: 
0188:     def close(self):
0189:         with self.lock:
0190:             if not self.closed:
0191:                 self.closed = True
0192:                 self.event('closed')
0193: 
0194:     def snapshot(self):
0195:         with self.lock:
0196:             summaries = {name: dict(row, mean_ms=row['total_ms']/row['count'] if row['count'] else None)
0197:                 for name, row in self.summaries.items()}
0198:             return {'schema_version': SCHEMA, 'enabled': True, 'collecting': not self.closed,
0199:                 'closed': self.closed, 'mode': 'COUNTS_AND_SOURCE_AGE_ONLY', 'ttl_ms': self.ttl_ms,
0200:                 'per_attempt_wall_or_thread_cpu_collected': False,
0201:                 'clock_scope': 'existing service source clock; assumes monotonic service millis; no independent clock comparison',
0202:                 'gap_scope': 'pre-check/post-commit bounds clipped after previous post-commit observation; no head/tail extrapolation',
0203:                 'post_commit_ttl_scope': 'observation age reaching TTL is not proof of accepting stale input at the original gate',
0204:                 'snapshot_scope': 'recorder only; enclosing health caller may hold the shared service RLock',
0205:                 'event_capacity': self.capacity, 'event_codes': dict(EVENT_CODES),
0206:                 'cancel_reason_codes': dict(CANCEL_REASON_CODES),
0207:                 'events': [dict(row) for row in self.events],
0208:                 'gap_record_capacity': GAP_RECORD_CAPACITY,
0209:                 'gap_records_dropped': self.gap_records_dropped,
0210:                 'gap_records': [dict(row) for row in self.gap_records],
0211:                 'counters': dict(self.counters), 'summaries': summaries}

0235:     def retry_health(self):
0236:         """Numeric opt-in experiment counters; no images or identity fields."""
0237:         with self.lock:
0238:             value=dict(self.retry_stats)
0239:             if self.independent_admission:value['independent_admission']=True
0240:             return value

0243:     def _process_with_retry(self, packet, detector):
0244:         from board.vision.dpu_vision_runtime import SlotAdmissionDenied,AdmissionCancelled
0245:         began=self.retry_clock();deadline=None;retries=0
0246:         with self.lock:self.retry_stats['rounds']+=1
0247:         try:
0248:             while True:
0249:                 stamp=packet[2]
0250:                 def check():
0251:                     if self.independent_admission:
0252:                         if self._admission_cancelled.is_set():raise AdmissionCancelled('closed')
0253:                         if not 0<=self.clock()-stamp<self.ttl:raise AdmissionCancelled('expired')
0254:                         if deadline is not None and self.retry_clock()>=deadline:
0255:                             raise AdmissionCancelled('deadline')
0256:                         return
0257:                     # This callback also runs inside the DPU slot. Never wait
0258:                     # for the camera-shared lock while owning that slot.
0259:                     if not self.lock.acquire(False):raise AdmissionCancelled('busy')
0260:                     try:
0261:                         if self.closed:raise AdmissionCancelled('closed')
0262:                         if not 0<=self.clock()-stamp<self.ttl:raise AdmissionCancelled('expired')
0263:                         if deadline is not None and self.retry_clock()>=deadline:
0264:                             raise AdmissionCancelled('deadline')
0265:                     finally:self.lock.release()
0266:                 # An adapter waiting outside the DPU slot must not extend an
0267:                 # already established 200ms round admission deadline.
0268:                 check.deadline_monotonic=deadline
0269:                 rejected=self.process(packet,detector,admission_check=check)
0270:                 if type(rejected) is not SlotAdmissionDenied:return
0271:                 with self.lock:self.retry_stats['denied']+=1
0272:                 if deadline is None:deadline=rejected.rejected_at+.200
0273:                 if retries>=2:
0274:                     with self.lock:self.retry_stats['exhausted']+=1
0275:                     return
0276:                 # Deadline is conservative admission-start +200ms. Backoff is
0277:                 # separately anchored after the rejection bookkeeping returns.
0278:                 not_before=self.retry_clock()+.025
0279:                 with self.condition:
0280:                     while True:
0281:                         now=self.retry_clock()
0282:                         if self.closed:
0283:                             self.retry_stats['closed']+=1;return
0284:                         if now>=deadline:
0285:                             self.retry_stats['deadline']+=1;return
0286:                         if self.pending is not None and now>=not_before:
0287:                             latest,self.pending=self.pending,None
0288:                             # Re-offering an old frame cannot spend another
0289:                             # CPU preparation. Keep only a new, live packet.
0290:                             if (latest[1]<=packet[1] or latest[2]<packet[2]
0291:                                     or not 0<=self.clock()-latest[2]<self.ttl):
0292:                                 self.discarded+=1
0293:                                 continue
0294:                             packet=latest;retries+=1;self.retry_stats['retries']+=1
0295:                             break
0296:                         delay=deadline-now
0297:                         if self.pending is not None and now<not_before:
0298:                             delay=min(delay,not_before-now)
0299:                         # Condition.wait releases the camera-shared lock.
0300:                         # No DPU slot is owned here; no image copy or polling.
0301:                         self.condition.wait(delay)
0302:         finally:
0303:             ended=self.retry_clock()
0304:             with self.lock:
0305:                 self.retry_stats['maximum_round_ms']=max(
0306:                     self.retry_stats['maximum_round_ms'],(ended-began)*1000.)
0307:                 if deadline is not None:
0308:                     self.retry_stats['maximum_deadline_overrun_ms']=max(
0309:                         self.retry_stats['maximum_deadline_overrun_ms'],max(0.,ended-deadline)*1000.)
0310: 
0311: 
0312:     def work(self):
0313:         detector=None
0314:         try:
0315:             detector=self.factory()
0316:             with self.lock:self.ready=True
0317:             next_at=0
0318:             while True:
0319:                 with self.condition:
0320:                     self.condition.wait_for(lambda:self.closed or self.pending is not None)
0321:                     if self.closed:return
0322:                     delay=next_at-time.monotonic()
0323:                     if delay>0:self.condition.wait(delay);continue
0324:                     packet,self.pending=self.pending,None
0325:                     processed_before=self.processed
0326:                 if self.slot_retry:self._process_with_retry(packet,detector)
0327:                 else:self.process(packet,detector)
0328:                 wait_ms=self.interval
0329:                 if self.retry_recovery_ms is not None:
0330:                     with self.lock:
0331:                         if not self.closed and self.processed==processed_before:
0332:                             wait_ms=self.retry_recovery_ms
0333:                             self.retry_stats['recovery_waits']+=1
0334:                 next_at=time.monotonic()+wait_ms/1000
0335:         except Exception as exc:
0336:             with self.lock:self.error='人脸目标检测失败：'+str(exc)[:100];self.key=None;self.pending=None
0337:         finally:
0338:             if detector and hasattr(detector,'close'):detector.close()
0339: 
0340:     def close(self):
0341:         if self._admission_cancelled is not None:self._admission_cancelled.set()
0342:         with self.condition:self.closed=True;self.pending=None;self.key=None;self.condition.notify_all()
0343:         try:
0344:             if self.thread:
0345:                 self.thread.join(5)
0346:                 if self.thread.is_alive():raise RuntimeError('Face target worker did not stop')
0347:         finally:
0348:             if self._freshness is not None:self._freshness_call('close')

0001: """Selected-target identity, with one CPU worker and no camera ownership.
0002: 
0003: Embedding candidates stay in RAM; opted-in enrollment photos have a separate
0004: face-only archive. Only explicit enrollment writes a model-specific template.
0005: The owner supplies the camera lock and target check
0006: so a changed target cannot race a transaction or resurrect a late result.
0007: """

0051:             if self.result['match'].state!='UNKNOWN':raise ValueError('当前身份已识别或有歧义，请先核对')
0052:             if self.sampling.stage not in ('idle','failed'):
0053:                 raise ValueError('请先完成或取消当前采样')
0054:             replace=params.get('replace_person_id','')
0055:             if replace and (params.get('db_version')!=self.store.revision or
0056:                     replace not in [r['person_id'] for r in self.store.people()]):
0057:                 raise ValueError('待更新身份已变化，请重新核对')
0058:             self.sampling.start(key,self.store.revision,replace,anchor=self.result['embedding'])
0059:             return '已开始人脸采样，请正对镜头'

0061:     def sample_cancel(self, params, command_id):

0063:             if self.sampling and self.sampling.session_id==params['session_id']:
0064:                 self.sampling.reset()

0067:     def _sampling_check(self):
0068:         if self.sampling:

0070:                     and self.sampling.stage not in ('idle','failed')):
0071:                 self.sampling.fail('检测到多张人脸，已停止采样，请只保留登记者')
0072:             self.sampling.check(self.current_target(),self.store.revision)

0074:     def start(self):

0076:             if self.thread is not None or self.closed:
0077:                 raise RuntimeError('Identity worker cannot be started twice')
0078:             self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.work, name='camera-identity', daemon=True)
0079:             self.thread.start()

0081:     def offer(self, image, fid, stamp, observation, camera_epoch):
0082:         """Latest-only slot, sharing the immutable capture frame with detection."""
0083:         with self.condition:
0084:             selected = observation.get('target_id')
0085:             track = next((t for t in observation['tracks'] if t['track_id'] == selected
0086:                           and t['visible'] and t['confirmed']), None)
0087:             if track is None:
0088:                 self.pending = self.result = None
0089:                 return
0090:             key = {'camera_epoch': camera_epoch, 'track_id': selected, 'track_generation': track['generation']}
0091:             self.pending = (image, fid, stamp, copy.deepcopy(observation), key)
0092:             self.condition.notify()

0094:     def offer_face(self,image,face,fid,stamp,key):
0095:         # FaceTargetService invokes this after releasing state.lock. The
0096:         # extractor is published only after its auxiliary objects are bound.
0097:         extractor = self.extractor
0098:         producer = getattr(extractor, 'producer', None) if extractor is not None else None
0099:         if producer is not None:
0100:             try:
0101:                 producer.offer_face(image,face,fid,stamp,key)
0102:             except Exception:
0103:                 # Auxiliary delivery must not change Windows pending/results.
0104:                 pass
0105:         with self.condition:
0106:             if face is None or key is None:
0107:                 self.pending=self.result=None;return
0108:             observation={'target_kind':'face','face':tuple(face),'face_target':copy.deepcopy(key),
0109:                          'frame_id':fid,'captured_mono_ms':stamp}
0110:             self.pending=(image,fid,stamp,observation,copy.deepcopy(key));self.condition.notify()

0112:     def _fresh(self, result):
0113:         return bool(result and result['target'] == self.current_target()
0114:                     and 0 <= self.clock()-result['stamp'] < self.ttl
0115:                     and result['revision'] == self.store.revision)

0117:     def view(self):
0118:         with self.lock:
0119:             people = self.store.people()

0131:                 elif not current:value['detail']='请正对摄像头，等待人脸目标确认'

0136:             if fresh:

0156:                     and self.sampling.stage=='ready'

0158:                     and self.sampling.usable(result['embedding'],self.store.best_other_score(result['embedding'],self.sampling.replace_id)))

0160:                 'identity_sample_start':bool(self.sampling and can_sample and self.sampling.stage in ('idle','failed')),
0161:                 'identity_sample_cancel':bool(self.sampling and self.sampling.session_id)}

0166:             result = self.result

0172:     def enroll(self, params, command_id):
0173:         with self.lock:
0174:             key = target_key(params['target'])
0175:             name = params['display_name'].strip()
0176:             if params.get('confirmed') is not True or not text(name, 48, False):
0177:                 raise ValueError('登记需要明确确认和有效名称')
0178:             result = self.result
0179:             if not self.ready or self.error or not self._fresh(result) or result['target'] != key:
0180:                 raise ValueError('目标或合格人脸已变化，请重新确认后登记')
0181:             if result['match'].state != 'UNKNOWN':
0182:                 raise ValueError('当前对象已登记或匹配有歧义，请先核对身份')
0183:             vector=result['embedding']
0184:             replace=params.get('replace_person_id','')

0187:                     not any(r['person_id']==replace and r['display_name'].casefold()==name.casefold() for r in people)):

0189:             if self.sampling:

0195:                         self.store.best_other_score(vector,replace))):
0196:                     raise ValueError('人脸采样或独立新画面验证尚未通过')
0197:                 vector=self.sampling.candidate['embedding']

0199:                 if match.state!='UNKNOWN' and match.identity_id!=replace:raise ValueError('候选模板已对应现有身份，请先核对')
0200:             if any(p['display_name'].casefold() == name.casefold() and p['person_id']!=replace for p in people):
0201:                 raise ValueError('该称呼已存在，请使用不同称呼')
0202:             self.store.enroll(replace or uuid.uuid4().hex, name, vector, consent=True,
0203:                               expected_revision=result['revision'], max_people=self.max_people)
0204:             result['revision'] = self.store.revision
0205:             result['match'] = self.store.match(result['embedding'])

0208:                                    'state': 'COMMITTED', 'detail': detail, 'db_version': self.store.revision}

0212:     def delete(self, params, command_id):
0213:         with self.lock:
0214:             if params.get('confirmed') is not True:
0215:                 raise ValueError('删除需要明确确认')
0216:             if not self.store.delete(params['person_id'], expected_revision=params['db_version']):
0217:                 raise ValueError('该身份已删除，请刷新状态')
0218:             # Invalidate every cached template/result, including an in-flight job.
0219:             self.result = self.pending = None
0220:             if self.sampling:self.sampling.reset()
0221:             self.reason = '身份已删除，等待重新识别'
0222:             self.last_operation = {'command_id': command_id, 'action': 'identity_delete',
0223:                                    'state': 'COMMITTED', 'detail': '身份已删除', 'db_version': self.store.revision}
0224:             return '身份已删除'
0225: 
0226:     def process(self, packet, extractor):
0227:         image, fid, stamp, observation, key = packet
0228:         with self.lock:
0229:             if key != self.current_target() or not 0 <= self.clock()-stamp < self.ttl:
0230:                 self.discarded += 1
0231:                 return
0232:             revision = self.store.revision
0233:         started = time.monotonic()
0234:         try:
0235:             embedding, reason = extractor.extract(image, observation, key)
0236:             vector = None if embedding is None else normalize(embedding, self.store.dimension)
0237:             reason = str(reason)[:120]
0238:         except DeferredIdentity:
0239:             with self.lock:
0240:                 self.last_ms = round((time.monotonic()-started)*1000, 2)
0241:                 self.discarded += 1
0242:                 self.deferred += 1
0243:             # No recognition decision was computed. Keep the previous result
0244:             # and sampling state; view()/enroll() still enforce its original TTL.
0245:             return
0246:         except Exception as exc:
0247:             vector, reason = None, '身份识别失败：'+str(exc)[:100]
0248:         with self.lock:
0249:             self.last_ms = round((time.monotonic()-started)*1000, 2)
0250:             if (self.closed or key != self.current_target() or not 0 <= self.clock()-stamp < self.ttl
0251:                     or revision != self.store.revision):
0252:                 self.discarded += 1
0253:                 return
0254:             self.processed += 1
0255:             self.reason, self.result = reason, None
0256:             if vector is not None:
0257:                 preview = getattr(extractor, 'preview_png', None)
0258:                 if not isinstance(preview, bytes) or not preview.startswith(b'\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n') or len(preview)>65536:
0259:                     preview = None
0260:                 self.result = {'target': key, 'fid': fid, 'stamp': stamp, 'embedding': vector,
0261:                                'revision': revision, 'match': self.store.match(vector), 'face_png':preview}
0262:                 self.match_counts[self.result['match'].state]+=1
0263:                 if self.sampling:
0264:                     self.sampling.offer(self.result,getattr(extractor,'enrollment_quality',None),
0265:                         self.store.best_other_score(vector,self.sampling.replace_id))
0266: 
0267:     def work(self):
0268:         extractor = None
0269:         try:
0270:             extractor = self.extractor_factory()
0271:             with self.lock:
0272:                 self.extractor = extractor
0273:                 self.ready = True
0274:             next_at = 0
0275:             while True:
0276:                 with self.condition:
0277:                     self.condition.wait_for(lambda: self.closed or self.pending is not None)
0278:                     if self.closed:
0279:                         return
0280:                     delay = max(0, next_at-time.monotonic())
0281:                     if delay:
0282:                         self.condition.wait(delay)
0283:                         continue
0284:                     packet, self.pending = self.pending, None
0285:                 self.process(packet, extractor)
0286:                 next_at = time.monotonic()+self.interval/1000
0287:         except Exception as exc:
0288:             with self.lock:
0289:                 self.error = '身份服务不可用：'+str(exc)[:100]
0290:                 self.result = self.pending = None
0291:         finally:
0292:             if extractor is not None and callable(getattr(extractor, 'close', None)):
0293:                 try:
0294:                     if extractor.close() is False:
0295:                         with self.lock:self.shutdown_incomplete = True
0296:                 except Exception as exc:
0297:                     with self.lock:
0298:                         self.shutdown_incomplete = True
0299:                         self.error = '身份模型关闭失败：'+str(exc)[:100]
0300: 
0301:     def health(self):
0302:         with self.lock:
0303:             face=self.face_targets.health() if self.face_targets else None
0304:             return {'ready': self.ready and not self.error and (not face or face['ready']),
0305:                     'error': self.error or (face['error'] if face else ''), 'face_targets':face,
0306:                     'backend': self.backend, 'processed': self.processed,
0307:                     'shutdown_incomplete':self.shutdown_incomplete,
0308:                     'feature_inference': self.extractor.health() if self.extractor is not None
0309:                         and callable(getattr(self.extractor, 'health', None)) else None,
0310:                     'enrollment_photos':self.photo_archive.health() if self.photo_archive else {'enabled':False},
0311:                     'sampling_diagnostics':None if not self.sampling else {
0312:                         'stage':self.sampling.stage,'pool_frames':len(self.sampling.training),
0313:                         'template_policy':self.sampling.policy,
0314:                         'last_failure':copy.deepcopy(self.sampling.last_failure),'metrics':copy.deepcopy(self.sampling.metrics)},
0315:                     'discarded': self.discarded, 'deferred': self.deferred,
0316:                     'model_id': self.store.model_id, 'last_inference_ms': self.last_ms,
0317:                     'match_diagnostics':{'counts':dict(self.match_counts),
0318:                         'last_state':self.result['match'].state if self.result else None,
0319:                         'last_score':float(self.result['match'].score) if self.result else None},
0320:                     'pending_frames': int(self.pending is not None)}
0321: 
0322:     def close(self):
0323:         if self.face_targets:self.face_targets.close()
0324:         with self.condition:
0325:             self.closed = True
0326:             if self.sampling:self.sampling.reset()
0327:             self.pending = self.result = None
0328:             self.condition.notify_all()
0329:         if self.thread:
0330:             self.thread.join(5)
0331:             if self.thread.is_alive():
0332:                 raise RuntimeError('Identity worker did not stop; database remains open')
0333:         with self.lock:
0334:             if self.shutdown_incomplete:
0335:                 ready = getattr(self.extractor, 'shutdown_ready', None)
0336:                 if not callable(ready) or not ready():
0337:                     raise RuntimeError('Shadow inference has not stopped; keep the shared DPU owner open')
0338:                 self.shutdown_incomplete = False
0339:             self.store.close()

0017: def validate_dpu_configuration(config):
0018:     backend = config.get('feature_backend')
0019:     required = {'kind', 'model', 'manifest', 'model_sha256', 'manifest_sha256',
0020:                 'fallback_config', 'max_source_age_ms'}
0021:     if (not isinstance(backend, dict) or set(backend) != required or backend['kind'] != 'dpu_sface'
0022:             or config.get('model_id') != MODEL_ID or backend['model_sha256'] != MODEL_SHA256):
0023:         raise ValueError('DPU SFace requires its exact versioned model configuration')
0024:     if (type(backend['max_source_age_ms']) is not int
0025:             or not 100 <= backend['max_source_age_ms'] <= min(1500, config['result_ttl_ms'])):
0026:         raise ValueError('Invalid DPU SFace source lifetime')
0027:     digest = backend['manifest_sha256']
0028:     if not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in digest):
0029:         raise ValueError('An explicit SFace compiler manifest SHA256 is required')
0030:     for key in ('model', 'manifest', 'fallback_config'):
0031:         if not isinstance(backend[key], str) or not Path(backend[key]).is_absolute():
0032:             raise ValueError('DPU SFace paths must be explicit absolute paths')
0033:     database = Path(config['database']).expanduser()
0034:     if not database.is_absolute() or MODEL_ID not in database.name:
0035:         raise ValueError('DPU SFace requires a separate version-named database')
0036:     fallback = json.loads(Path(backend['fallback_config']).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
0037:     remote = fallback.get('feature_backend')
0038:     if (fallback.get('schema_version') != 'aura-camera-identity-1'
0039:             or fallback.get('model_id') != WINDOWS_MODEL_ID
0040:             or not isinstance(remote, dict) or remote.get('kind') != 'windows_sface'
0041:             or remote.get('host') != '192.168.50.1' or remote.get('port') != 8792):
0042:         raise ValueError('Fallback must preserve the original Windows FP32 configuration')
0043:     legacy = Path(fallback['database']).expanduser()
0044:     if not legacy.is_absolute() or database.resolve() == legacy.resolve():
0045:         raise ValueError('INT8 and Windows identity databases must be separate')
0046:     if database.exists() and legacy.exists() and os.path.samefile(str(database), str(legacy)):
0047:         raise ValueError('INT8 database aliases the Windows database')
0048:     photos = config.get('enrollment_photos')
0049:     if photos and photos.get('enabled'):
0050:         archive = Path(photos['directory']).expanduser()
0051:         old_photos = fallback.get('enrollment_photos')

0053:             raise ValueError('INT8 enrollment photos require a separate version-named archive')

0055:             raise ValueError('INT8 and Windows enrollment archives must be separate')

0059: def check_artifact_configuration(config):
0060:     """File/configuration check only; no XIR, VART, owner, device, or DB write."""

0062:     validate_dpu_configuration(config)

0066:             raise ValueError('Pinned SFace artifact mismatch: '+key)

0069:     if (manifest['sha256'] != MODEL_SHA256 or len(dpu) != 1 or dpu[0]['fingerprint'] != FINGERPRINT
0070:             or len(dpu[0]['inputs']) != 1 or len(dpu[0]['outputs']) != 1):

0073:     if (source['dims'] != [1,112,112,3] or source['dtype'] != 'xint8' or source['fix_point'] != 7

0078:     margin = config.get('ambiguity_margin')

0080:                             and 0 <= threshold <= 1 and type(margin) in (int, float)
0081:                             and math.isfinite(margin) and 0 <= margin <= 1)

0089: def validate_existing_database(path):
0090:     """Reject a renamed, mixed, or unbound existing database before any write."""
0091:     path = Path(path)
0092:     # exists() is false for a dangling symlink. Reject it explicitly so SQLite
0093:     # cannot create a database through an unreviewed filesystem alias.
0094:     if path.is_symlink():
0095:         raise ValueError('INT8 identity database must be a real file, not a symlink')
0096:     if not path.exists():
0097:         return
0098:     if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size == 0:
0099:         raise ValueError('Existing INT8 database must have a valid model binding')
0100:     connection = sqlite3.connect(path.resolve().as_uri() + '?mode=ro', uri=True)
0101:     try:
0102:         binding = connection.execute('SELECT model_id, dimension FROM identity_model_binding').fetchall()
0103:         if binding != [(MODEL_ID, 128)]:
0104:             raise ValueError('INT8 identity database model binding mismatch')
0105:         foreign = connection.execute('SELECT DISTINCT model_id, dimension FROM templates').fetchall()
0106:         if any(row != (MODEL_ID, 128) for row in foreign):
0107:             raise ValueError('Foreign templates in INT8 identity database')
0108:     except sqlite3.Error as exc:
0109:         raise ValueError('Existing database is not a bound INT8 identity database') from exc
0110:     finally:
0111:         connection.close()
0112: 
0113: 
0114: def open_dpu_store(config):
0115:     """Called only after the user explicitly selects the DPU service config."""
0116:     from board.identity.store import IdentityStore
0117:     validate_dpu_configuration(config)
0118:     path = Path(config['database'])
0119:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
0120:     created = False
0121:     try:
0122:         fd = os.open(str(path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
0123:     except FileExistsError:
0124:         # Revalidate after file creation races; never adopt an unbound file.
0125:         validate_existing_database(path)
0126:     else:
0127:         os.close(fd)
0128:         created = True
0129:     store = None
0130:     try:
0131:         store = IdentityStore(path, MODEL_ID, 128, config['threshold'], config['ambiguity_margin'],
0132:                               allow_cross_thread=True)
0133:         with store.db:
0134:             if created:
0135:                 store.db.execute('CREATE TABLE identity_model_binding '
0136:                                  '(model_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, dimension INTEGER NOT NULL)')
0137:                 store.db.execute('INSERT INTO identity_model_binding VALUES (?, ?)', (MODEL_ID, 128))
0138:         validate_existing_database(path)
0139:         return store
0140:     except Exception:
0141:         if store is not None:
0142:             store.close()
0143:         # Retain an incomplete new file for explicit inspection; never delete
0144:         # or overwrite any database during initialization failure.
0145:         raise

0001: """SQLite identities: no automatic enrollment; templates are model-specific."""
0002: from dataclasses import dataclass
0003: import json
0004: import math
0005: from pathlib import Path
0006: import sqlite3
0007: from common.contracts import finite, integer
0008: 
0009: 
0010: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0011: class IdentityMatch:
0012:     state: str
0013:     identity_id: str = ""
0014:     name: str = ""
0015:     score: float = 0.0
0016:     revision: int = 0
0017: 
0018: 
0019: def normalize(embedding, dimension):
0020:     values = tuple(embedding)
0021:     if len(values) != dimension or not all(finite(v) for v in values):
0022:         raise ValueError("Embedding dimension/non-finite mismatch")
0023:     norm = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in values))
0024:     if norm <= 1e-12:
0025:         raise ValueError("Zero embedding")
0026:     return tuple(v / norm for v in values)
0027: 
0028: 
0029: class IdentityStore:
0030:     def __init__(self, path, model_id, dimension, threshold, ambiguity_margin, *, allow_cross_thread=False):
0031:         if not model_id or not integer(dimension, 1) or not -1 <= threshold <= 1 or not 0 <= ambiguity_margin <= 2:
0032:             raise ValueError("Invalid identity model/threshold")
0033:         if str(path) != ":memory:":
0034:             Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
0035:         self.model_id, self.dimension = model_id, dimension
0036:         self.threshold, self.ambiguity_margin = threshold, ambiguity_margin
0037:         # A cross-thread connection requires its single owning service's lock.
0038:         self.db = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=not allow_cross_thread)
0039:         self.db.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
0040:         self.db.executescript("""
0041:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS identities (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL);
0042:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS templates (
0043:                 identity_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES identities(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
0044:                 model_id TEXT NOT NULL, dimension INTEGER NOT NULL, embedding TEXT NOT NULL,
0045:                 PRIMARY KEY(identity_id, model_id));
0046:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value INTEGER NOT NULL);
0047:             INSERT OR IGNORE INTO metadata VALUES ('revision', 0);
0048:         """)
0049:         self.db.commit()
0050: 
0051:     @property
0052:     def revision(self):
0053:         return self.db.execute("SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE key='revision'").fetchone()[0]
0054: 
0055:     def people(self):
0056:         return [{'person_id': key, 'display_name': name} for key, name in self.db.execute(
0057:             'SELECT i.id, i.name FROM identities i JOIN templates t ON i.id=t.identity_id '
0058:             'WHERE t.model_id=? AND t.dimension=? ORDER BY i.name, i.id', (self.model_id, self.dimension))]
0059: 
0060:     def enroll(self, identity_id, name, embedding, *, consent=False, expected_revision=None, max_people=None):
0061:         if consent is not True or not identity_id or not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
0062:             raise ValueError("Explicit enrollment with a name and consent required")
0063:         vector = normalize(embedding, self.dimension)
0064:         with self.db:
0065:             self.db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
0066:             if expected_revision is not None and self.revision != expected_revision:
0067:                 raise ValueError('身份库版本已变化，请刷新后重试')
0068:             people=self.people()
0069:             if max_people is not None and len(people) >= max_people and identity_id not in [r['person_id'] for r in people]:
0070:                 raise ValueError('身份库已满，请先删除不再使用的身份')
0071:             self.db.execute("INSERT INTO identities VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET name=excluded.name",
0072:                             (identity_id, name.strip()))
0073:             self.db.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO templates VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
0074:                             (identity_id, self.model_id, self.dimension, json.dumps(vector)))
0075:             self.db.execute("UPDATE metadata SET value=value+1 WHERE key='revision'")
0076: 
0077:     def delete(self, identity_id, *, expected_revision=None):
0078:         with self.db:
0079:             self.db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
0080:             if expected_revision is not None and self.revision != expected_revision:
0081:                 raise ValueError('身份库版本已变化，请刷新后重试')
0082:             count = self.db.execute("DELETE FROM identities WHERE id=?", (identity_id,)).rowcount
0083:             if count:
0084:                 self.db.execute("UPDATE metadata SET value=value+1 WHERE key='revision'")
0085:         return bool(count)
0086: 
0087:     def match(self, embedding):
0088:         vector = normalize(embedding, self.dimension)
0089:         rows = self.db.execute("""SELECT i.id, i.name, t.embedding FROM identities i
0090:             JOIN templates t ON i.id=t.identity_id WHERE t.model_id=? AND t.dimension=?""",
0091:             (self.model_id, self.dimension))
0092:         scored = sorted(((sum(a * b for a, b in zip(vector, normalize(json.loads(raw), self.dimension))), key, name)
0093:                          for key, name, raw in rows), reverse=True)
0094:         rev = self.revision
0095:         if not scored or scored[0][0] < self.threshold:
0096:             return IdentityMatch("UNKNOWN", score=scored[0][0] if scored else 0.0, revision=rev)
0097:         score, key, name = scored[0]
0098:         if len(scored) > 1 and score - scored[1][0] < self.ambiguity_margin:
0099:             return IdentityMatch("AMBIGUOUS", score=score, revision=rev)
0100:         return IdentityMatch("KNOWN", key, name, score, rev)
0101: 
0102:     def best_other_score(self, embedding, exclude_id=''):
0103:         """Future-gallery check for an explicitly selected replacement."""
0104:         vector=normalize(embedding,self.dimension)
0105:         rows=self.db.execute('SELECT identity_id,embedding FROM templates WHERE model_id=? AND dimension=?',
0106:                              (self.model_id,self.dimension))
0107:         scores=[sum(a*b for a,b in zip(vector,normalize(json.loads(raw),self.dimension)))
0108:                 for identity_id,raw in rows if identity_id!=exclude_id]
0109:         return max(scores) if scores else None
0110: 
0111:     def close(self):
0112:         self.db.close()

FILE: current_source/aura/board/vision/dpu_recovery_policy.py
SHA256: b00d19aacdf211f93bd192ebcbca20d72782bcc1bacae09736f6edaf37f17d62

0001: """Bounded recovery decisions; the scheduler holds its condition for every call."""
0002: from collections import deque
0003: 
0004: 
0005: class YuNetRecoveryPolicy:
0006:     def __init__(self):
0007:         self.phase = 'RUNNING'
0008:         self.reason = None
0009:         self.cooldown_s = 10.
0010:         self.window_s = 600.
0011:         self.maximum_attempts = 2
0012:         self.required_successes = 3
0013:         self.primary_max_age_s = .5
0014:         self.attempt_times = deque(maxlen=self.maximum_attempts)
0015:         self.cooldown_until = 0.
0016:         self.fault_primary_calls = self.fault_primary_overruns = 0
0017:         self.probe_successes = self.faults = self.attempts = self.recoveries = 0
0018: 
0019:     def fault(self, now, *, completed, body_failed, hardcap, guard,
0020:               primary_calls, primary_overruns):
0021:         self.faults += 1
0022:         self.probe_successes = 0
0023:         self.fault_primary_calls = primary_calls
0024:         self.fault_primary_overruns = primary_overruns
0025:         if not completed or body_failed or guard or not hardcap:
0026:             self.phase, self.reason = 'LATCHED', 'unsafe_or_unknown_completion'
0027:         elif len(self.attempt_times) == self.maximum_attempts and now - self.attempt_times[0] < self.window_s:
0028:             self.phase, self.reason = 'LATCHED', 'recovery_budget_exhausted'
0029:         else:
0030:             self.phase, self.reason = 'COOLDOWN', 'completed_hardcap_only'
0031:             self.cooldown_until = now + self.cooldown_s
0032: 
0033:     def due(self, now, *, primary_calls, primary_overruns, last_primary):
0034:         if self.phase != 'COOLDOWN' or now < self.cooldown_until:
0035:             return False
0036:         if primary_overruns != self.fault_primary_overruns:
0037:             self.phase, self.reason = 'LATCHED', 'primary_overrun_after_fault'
0038:             return False
0039:         if (primary_calls <= self.fault_primary_calls or last_primary is None
0040:                 or not 0 <= now - last_primary <= self.primary_max_age_s):
0041:             return False
0042:         while self.attempt_times and now - self.attempt_times[0] >= self.window_s:
0043:             self.attempt_times.popleft()
0044:         if len(self.attempt_times) >= self.maximum_attempts:
0045:             self.phase, self.reason = 'LATCHED', 'recovery_budget_exhausted'
0046:             return False
0047:         return True
0048: 
0049:     def admitted(self, now):
0050:         # Called only after atomic ordinary primary/deadline admission.
0051:         self.attempt_times.append(now)
0052:         self.attempts += 1
0053:         self.phase, self.reason = 'PROBATION', None
0054:         self.probe_successes = 0
0055: 
0056:     def success(self):
0057:         if self.phase == 'PROBATION':
0058:             self.probe_successes += 1
0059:             if self.probe_successes >= self.required_successes:
0060:                 self.phase, self.reason = 'RUNNING', None
0061:                 self.recoveries += 1
0062: 
0063:     def primary_failed(self):
0064:         if self.phase == 'PROBATION':
0065:             self.phase, self.reason = 'LATCHED', 'primary_overrun_during_probation'
0066:             return True
0067:         return False
0068: 
0069:     def snapshot(self):
0070:         return {'enabled': True, 'phase': self.phase, 'reason': self.reason,
0071:                 'cooldown_s': self.cooldown_s, 'window_s': self.window_s,
0072:                 'maximum_attempts': self.maximum_attempts,
0073:                 'required_successes': self.required_successes,
0074:                 'probe_successes': self.probe_successes, 'faults': self.faults,
0075:                 'attempts': self.attempts, 'recoveries': self.recoveries,
0076:                 'cooldown_until_monotonic_s': self.cooldown_until,
0077:                 'attempts_in_window': len(self.attempt_times)}

FILE: current_source/aura/board/vision/dpu_sface.py
SHA256: 6bc690e94cc72283495fc7020e0590fae00f46aff59e484b978a3bfc36e6c8f0

0001: """Optional local SFace inference under the camera's existing DPU owner.
0002: 
0003: There is deliberately no per-vector FP32 fallback: those vectors belong to a
0004: different template space. Rollback selects the preserved Windows config/store.
0005: """
0006: from contextlib import contextmanager
0007: import hashlib
0008: from pathlib import Path
0009: import time
0010: 
0011: from board.identity.deferred import DeferredIdentity
0012: from board.identity.model_store import MODEL_ID, MODEL_SHA256
0013: from board.vision.dpu_vision_runtime import PinnedRunner, encode, sface_feature
0014: 
0015: 
0016: class DpuSFace:
0017:     kind = 'DPU_SFACE_INT8'
0018:     model_id = MODEL_ID
0019: 
0020:     def __init__(self, config, schedule, owner, clock):
0021:         if schedule is None or owner is None or not callable(clock):
0022:             raise ValueError('DPU SFace requires the camera DPU owner and scheduler')
0023:         backend = config['feature_backend']
0024:         if config['model_id'] != MODEL_ID or backend['model_sha256'] != MODEL_SHA256:
0025:             raise ValueError('DPU SFace model version mismatch')
0026:         for key in ('model', 'manifest'):
0027:             if hashlib.sha256(Path(backend[key]).read_bytes()).hexdigest() != backend[key + '_sha256']:
0028:                 raise ValueError('Pinned SFace artifact mismatch: ' + key)
0029:         self.schedule, self.clock = schedule, clock
0030:         self.source_ttl = backend['max_source_age_ms']
0031:         self.requests = self.execution_attempts = self.completed = self.features_returned = 0
0032:         self.deferred = self.slot_denials = self.expired_after_admission = 0
0033:         self.runner = PinnedRunner(backend['model'], backend['manifest'], owner)
0034:         if (self.runner.input_fix != 7 or list(self.runner.inputs[0].dims) != [1, 112, 112, 3]
0035:                 or len(self.runner.outputs) != 1 or list(self.runner.outputs[0].dims) != [1, 128]
0036:                 or self.runner.outputs[0].get_attr('fix_point') != 5):
0037:             self.runner.close()
0038:             raise ValueError('Unexpected SFace INT8 tensor contract')
0039: 
0040:     def _fresh(self, stamp):
0041:         return type(stamp) is int and 0 <= self.clock() - stamp < self.source_ttl
0042: 
0043:     @contextmanager
0044:     def _slot(self, stamp):
0045:         wait_ms=getattr(self.schedule,'sface_slot_wait_ms',0)
0046:         until=time.monotonic()+wait_ms/1000.
0047:         while True:
0048:             with self.schedule.optional('sface') as acquired:
0049:                 fresh=self._fresh(stamp)
0050:                 if acquired:
0051:                     if not fresh:self.expired_after_admission+=1
0052:                     else:self.execution_attempts+=1
0053:                     yield fresh
0054:                     return
0055:                 self.slot_denials+=1
0056:             # No slot or state lock is held while waiting for the same owner.
0057:             if (not wait_ms or not fresh or self.schedule.disabled('sface')
0058:                     or time.monotonic()>=until
0059:                     or not self.schedule.wait_for_optional('sface',until)):
0060:                 yield False
0061:                 return
0062: 
0063:     def _defer(self, message, *, retryable=False):
0064:         self.deferred += 1
0065:         raise DeferredIdentity(message,retryable=retryable)
0066: 
0067:     def feature(self, crop, *, source_mono_ms):
0068:         self.requests += 1
0069:         if not self._fresh(source_mono_ms) or self.schedule.disabled('sface'):
0070:             self._defer('SFace source expired or optional task disabled')
0071: 
0072:         attempts_before=self.execution_attempts
0073:         def scheduled(normalized):
0074:             source = encode(normalized, self.runner.input_fix)
0075:             if not self._fresh(source_mono_ms):
0076:                 return None
0077:             # Encoding and runner output allocation/dequantization are outside
0078:             # the scheduling lock. Only execute_async/wait hold the DPU slot.
0079:             result = self.runner.execute_encoded(source, slot=self._slot(source_mono_ms))
0080:             if result is not None:
0081:                 self.completed += 1
0082:             return result
0083: 
0084:         result = sface_feature(crop, self.runner, execute=scheduled)
0085:         if result is None or not self._fresh(source_mono_ms):
0086:             self._defer('SFace optional slot unavailable or result expired',
0087:                 retryable=self.execution_attempts==attempts_before and self._fresh(source_mono_ms)
0088:                     and not self.schedule.disabled('sface'))
0089:         self.features_returned += 1
0090:         return result
0091: 
0092:     def health(self):
0093:         return {'kind': self.kind, 'model_id': self.model_id,
0094:                 'requests': self.requests, 'execution_attempts': self.execution_attempts,
0095:                 'completed': self.completed, 'features_returned': self.features_returned,
0096:                 'deferred': self.deferred, 'slot_denials': self.slot_denials,
0097:                 'expired_after_admission': self.expired_after_admission,
0098:                 'last_dpu_wait_ms': self.runner.last_ms,
0099:                 'scheduler_calls_mean_admissions': True}
0100: 
0101:     def close(self):
0102:         self.runner.close()

FILE: current_source/aura/board/vision/dpu_vision_runtime.py
SHA256: e3b2d11140838fdf0eb548f8def033da5930ec46dfa72ae27f098c03e801c9d3
