0001: """Pinned candidate runners; caller supplies the single DPU owner and scheduler.
0002: 
0003: No camera, network, service control, identity database or FPGA loading here.
0004: The four YuNet heads are matched by geometry, never VART's output ordering.
0005: """
0006: import hashlib
0007: import json
0008: from pathlib import Path
0009: import time
0010: from contextlib import contextmanager
0011: import numpy as np
0012: 
0013: FINGERPRINT = '0x101000056010407'
0014: 
0015: 
0016: @contextmanager
0017: def unrestricted_slot():
0018:     yield True
0019: 
0020: 
0021: def encode(values, fix_point):
0022:     values = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float32)
0023:     if not np.isfinite(values).all():
0024:         raise ValueError('Non-finite DPU input')
0025:     return np.ascontiguousarray(np.clip(np.floor(values * (2. ** fix_point) + .5), -128, 127).astype(np.int8))
0026: 
0027: 
0028: def tensor_meta(t):
0029:     return {'name': t.name, 'dims': list(t.dims), 'dtype': str(t.dtype),
0030:             'fix_point': t.get_attr('fix_point') if t.has_attr('fix_point') else None}
0031: 
0032: 
0033: class PinnedRunner:
0034:     def __init__(self, model_path, manifest_path, owner):
0035:         # Production should pass its DpuYoloX instance; a fixed-input bench can
0036:         # supply an equivalent owner holding the SAME project lock in this PID.
0037:         if owner.fingerprint != FINGERPRINT or owner.ownership is None:
0038:             raise RuntimeError('An active matching DPU owner is required')
0039:         owner.ownership.fileno()
0040:         self.owner = owner
0041:         path = Path(model_path)
0042:         manifest = json.loads(Path(manifest_path).read_text())
0043:         if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != manifest['sha256']:
0044:             raise ValueError('Candidate xmodel SHA256 mismatch')
0045:         expected = [s for s in manifest['partitions'] if s['device'] == 'DPU']
0046:         if len(expected) != 1 or expected[0]['fingerprint'] != FINGERPRINT:
0047:             raise ValueError('Candidate manifest DPU mismatch')
0048:         import xir
0049:         import vart
0050:         self.graph = xir.Graph.deserialize(str(path))
0051:         subs = [s for s in self.graph.get_root_subgraph().toposort_child_subgraph()
0052:                 if s.has_attr('device') and s.get_attr('device') == 'DPU']
0053:         if len(subs) != 1 or hex(int(subs[0].get_attr('dpu_fingerprint'))) != FINGERPRINT:
0054:             raise ValueError('Compiled DPU fingerprint mismatch')
0055:         # XIR exposes an unordered tensor set. VART's port order is the ABI for
0056:         # execute_async; using XIR order can overrun a smaller output buffer.
0057:         self.runner = vart.Runner.create_runner(subs[0], 'run')
0058:         self.inputs = list(self.runner.get_input_tensors())
0059:         self.outputs = list(self.runner.get_output_tensors())
0060:         if ({t.name: tensor_meta(t) for t in self.inputs} != {t['name']: t for t in expected[0]['inputs']} or
0061:                 {t.name: tensor_meta(t) for t in self.outputs} != {t['name']: t for t in expected[0]['outputs']}):
0062:             raise ValueError('Compiled tensor interface differs from manifest')
0063:         if len(self.inputs) != 1 or any(str(t.dtype) != 'xint8' for t in self.inputs + self.outputs):
0064:             raise ValueError('Expected INT8 tensors and exactly one input')
0065:         self.input_fix = self.inputs[0].get_attr('fix_point')
0066:         from board.vision.pipeline_metrics import PipelineMetrics
0067:         self.metrics=getattr(owner,'pipeline_metrics',None) or PipelineMetrics()
0068:         self.stage_kind='sface' if list(self.inputs[0].dims)==[1,112,112,3] else 'yunet'
0069:         self.last_ms = None
0070: 
0071:     def execute(self, normalized_nhwc):
0072:         return self.execute_encoded(encode(normalized_nhwc, self.input_fix))
0073: 
0074:     def _diagnostic_mark(self, phase, status=None):
0075:         diagnostics = getattr(self.metrics,'optional_diagnostics',None)
0076:         if diagnostics is not None and diagnostics.enabled:
0077:             try:diagnostics.native_mark(self.stage_kind,phase,status)
0078:             except Exception:diagnostics.error()
0079: 
0080:     def execute_encoded(self, source, *, slot=None, pre_execute=None):
0081:         if self.runner is None:
0082:             raise RuntimeError('DPU runner is closed')
0083:         self.owner.ownership.fileno()
0084:         if (not isinstance(source, np.ndarray) or source.dtype != np.int8
0085:                 or not source.flags.c_contiguous or list(source.shape) != list(self.inputs[0].dims)):
0086:             raise ValueError('DPU input shape mismatch')
0087:         allocate_started=time.monotonic_ns()
0088:         outputs = [np.empty(tuple(t.dims), dtype=np.int8) for t in self.outputs]
0089:         self.metrics.elapsed(self.stage_kind+'.allocate',allocate_started)
0090:         slot_started=time.monotonic_ns()
0091:         with (slot if slot is not None else unrestricted_slot()) as acquired:
0092:             slot_wait_ms=(time.monotonic_ns()-slot_started)/1e6
0093:             if not acquired:
0094:                 self.metrics.value(self.stage_kind+'.slot_wait',slot_wait_ms)
0095:                 return None
0096:             started = time.monotonic()
0097:             self._diagnostic_mark('execute_start')
0098:             try:
0099:                 # After all preparation/admission/diagnostic overhead, directly
0100:                 # before VART. Raising releases the original slot and submits
0101:                 # no native job. The default path has no guard invocation.
0102:                 if pre_execute is not None:
0103:                     try:pre_execute()
0104:                     except AdmissionCancelled as exc:
0105:                         exc.native_not_submitted=True
0106:                         raise
0107:                 job = self.runner.execute_async([source], outputs)
0108:                 self._diagnostic_mark('execute_return')
0109:                 self._diagnostic_mark('wait_start')
0110:                 status = self.runner.wait(job)
0111:                 self._diagnostic_mark('wait_end',status)
0112:             except BaseException:
0113:                 self._diagnostic_mark('exception')
0114:                 raise
0115:             self.last_ms = (time.monotonic() - started) * 1000
0116:             if status != 0:
0117:                 raise RuntimeError('VART execution failed: ' + str(status))
0118:         self.metrics.value(self.stage_kind+'.slot_wait',slot_wait_ms)
0119:         self.metrics.value(self.stage_kind+'.execute_wait',self.last_ms)
0120:         # Output conversion is CPU work; the next YOLO job can own the DPU now.
0121:         dequantize_started=time.monotonic_ns()
0122:         result={t.name: a.astype(np.float32) * (2. ** -t.get_attr('fix_point'))
0123:                 for t, a in zip(self.outputs, outputs)}
0124:         self.metrics.elapsed(self.stage_kind+'.dequantize',dequantize_started)
0125:         return result
0126: 
0127:     def close(self):
0128:         self.runner = self.graph = None
0129: 
0130: 
0131: def yunet_outputs(values):
0132:     shapes = {(40, 40, 51), (20, 20, 34), (10, 10, 34), (5, 5, 51)}
0133:     by_shape = {tuple(x.shape[1:]): x for x in values.values()}
0134:     if len(values) != 4 or set(by_shape) != shapes:
0135:         raise ValueError('YuNet must have four unique 320px output heads')
0136:     raw = np.concatenate([by_shape[s].reshape(1, -1) for s in sorted(shapes, reverse=True)], axis=1).reshape(-1, 17)
0137:     logits = raw[:, 14:16]
0138:     e = np.exp(logits - logits.max(axis=1, keepdims=True))
0139:     return raw[:, :14].copy(), e / e.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True), raw[:, 16:17].copy()
0140: 
0141: 
0142: class YuNetNetwork:
0143:     def __init__(self, runner, execute=None):
0144:         self.runner = runner
0145:         # The camera integration supplies a scheduling callback. The isolated
0146:         # benchmark calls sequentially under its exclusive process owner.
0147:         self.execute = execute or runner.execute
0148: 
0149:     def setInput(self, nchw_bgr):
0150:         self.source = np.ascontiguousarray(nchw_bgr.transpose(0, 2, 3, 1) / 256.)
0151: 
0152:     def forward(self, names):
0153:         if names != ['loc', 'conf', 'iou']:
0154:             raise ValueError('Unsupported YuNet CPU outputs')
0155:         result = self.execute(self.source)
0156:         if result is None:
0157:             # A skipped scheduling slot must not look like "no face" and break
0158:             # a live identity binding. Integration must discard this packet.
0159:             raise SlotUnavailable('Face detection skipped for a primary DPU deadline')
0160:         return yunet_outputs(result)
0161: 
0162: 
0163: class SlotUnavailable(RuntimeError):
0164:     pass
0165: 
0166: 
0167: class SlotAdmissionDenied(SlotUnavailable):
0168:     """Enabled optional admission denied; rejected_at precedes its bookkeeping.
0169: 
0170:     The timestamp is the conservative admission-attempt start, not the exact
0171:     scheduler decision. Only this exact exception is eligible for retry.
0172:     """
0173:     def __init__(self, rejected_at):
0174:         super().__init__('YuNet optional slot admission denied')
0175:         self.rejected_at = rejected_at
0176: 
0177: 
0178: class AdmissionCancelled(RuntimeError):
0179:     """The caller closed, its source expired, or its retry round expired."""
0180:     def __init__(self, reason):
0181:         super().__init__('YuNet admission cancelled: ' + reason)
0182:         self.reason = reason
0183: 
0184: 
0185: class OptionalJobDisabled(RuntimeError):
0186:     """Overrun protection disabled this job; it is never a retry signal."""
0187: 
0188: 
0189: def make_yunet(reference_model, runner, *, threshold=.9, execute=None):
0190:     from board.vision.reference_faces import YuNetCompat
0191:     result = YuNetCompat(reference_model, score_threshold=threshold, input_size=320)
0192:     result.net = YuNetNetwork(runner, execute)
0193:     return result
0194: 
0195: 
0196: def sface_feature(crop, runner, execute=None):
0197:     if not isinstance(crop, np.ndarray) or crop.dtype != np.uint8 or crop.shape != (112, 112, 3):
0198:         raise ValueError('SFace needs an aligned uint8 112x112 BGR crop')
0199:     prepare_started=time.monotonic_ns()
0200:     source = (crop[:, :, ::-1].astype(np.float32)[None] - 127.5) / 128.
0201:     metrics=getattr(runner,'metrics',None)
0202:     if metrics:metrics.elapsed('sface.normalize_input',prepare_started)
0203:     result = (execute or runner.execute)(source)
0204:     if result is None:
0205:         return None
0206:     if len(result) != 1:
0207:         raise ValueError('SFace must have one output')
0208:     validate_started=time.monotonic_ns()
0209:     feature = next(iter(result.values())).reshape(-1)
0210:     if feature.shape != (128,) or not np.isfinite(feature).all() or np.linalg.norm(feature) <= 1e-8:
0211:         raise ValueError('Invalid SFace INT8 feature')
0212:     if metrics:metrics.elapsed('sface.validate_output',validate_started)
0213:     return feature

0008: class DpuVisionSchedule:
0009:     def __init__(self, *, budget_ms=20, guard_ms=5, clock=time.monotonic, diagnostics=None,
0010:                  yunet_recovery=False, yunet_slot_wait_ms=0, sface_slot_wait_ms=0):
0011:         if not 5 <= budget_ms <= 30 or not 1 <= guard_ms <= 20:
0012:             raise ValueError('Invalid DPU budget')
0013:         self.clock, self.budget, self.guard = clock, budget_ms / 1000., guard_ms / 1000.
0014:         self.condition = threading.Condition()
0015:         self.active, self.waiting_primary, self.next_primary = None, 0, 0.
0016:         self.primary_calls = self.primary_overruns = 0
0017:         self.jobs = {}
0018:         self.diagnostics = diagnostics
0019:         if type(yunet_recovery) is not bool:
0020:             raise ValueError('YuNet recovery requires an explicit boolean')
0021:         self.yunet_recovery = None
0022:         self._last_primary = None
0023:         self._native_owner = None
0024:         self._native_completed = False
0025:         if yunet_recovery:
0026:             from board.vision.dpu_recovery_policy import YuNetRecoveryPolicy
0027:             self.yunet_recovery = YuNetRecoveryPolicy()
0028:         if type(yunet_slot_wait_ms) is not int or not 0 <= yunet_slot_wait_ms <= 100:
0029:             raise ValueError('YuNet slot wait must be an integer in 0..100 milliseconds')
0030:         self.yunet_slot_wait_ms = yunet_slot_wait_ms
0031:         self._slot_wait_stats = {'enabled': True, 'maximum_wait_ms': yunet_slot_wait_ms,
0032:             'calls': 0, 'ready': 0, 'timeouts': 0, 'disabled': 0, 'condition_waits': 0,
0033:             'total_wall_ms': 0., 'maximum_wall_ms': 0.}
0034:         if type(sface_slot_wait_ms) is not int or not 0<=sface_slot_wait_ms<=100:
0035:             raise ValueError('SFace slot wait must be an integer in 0..100 milliseconds')

0037:         self._sface_wait_stats=dict(self._slot_wait_stats,maximum_wait_ms=sface_slot_wait_ms)
0038: 
0039:     @contextmanager
0040:     def primary(self, next_deadline):
0041:         with self.condition:
0042:             self.next_primary = next_deadline
0043:             self.waiting_primary += 1
0044:             try:
0045:                 while self.active is not None:
0046:                     self.condition.wait()
0047:                 self.active = 'yolo'
0048:             finally:
0049:                 self.waiting_primary -= 1
0050:         try:
0051:             yield
0052:         finally:
0053:             with self.condition:
0054:                 self.primary_calls += 1
0055:                 primary_late = self.clock() > next_deadline
0056:                 self.primary_overruns += int(primary_late)
0057:                 if self.yunet_recovery is not None:
0058:                     self._last_primary = self.clock()
0059:                     if primary_late and self.yunet_recovery.primary_failed():
0060:                         self.jobs['yunet']['disabled'] = True
0061:                 self.active = None
0062:                 self.condition.notify_all()
0063: 
0064:     def disabled(self, kind):
0065:         with self.condition:
0066:             return self.jobs.get(kind, {}).get('disabled', False)
0067: 
0068:     def _recovery_due(self, kind):
0069:         policy = self.yunet_recovery
0070:         return (kind == 'yunet' and policy is not None and policy.due(self.clock(),
0071:                 primary_calls=self.primary_calls, primary_overruns=self.primary_overruns,
0072:                 last_primary=self._last_primary))
0073: 
0074:     def recovery_ready(self, kind):
0075:         with self.condition:
0076:             return (self.jobs.get(kind, {}).get('disabled', False)
0077:                     and self.active is None and not self.waiting_primary
0078:                     and self._recovery_due(kind))
0079: 
0080:     def native_completed(self, kind):
0081:         if self.yunet_recovery is None or kind != 'yunet':
0082:             return
0083:         with self.condition:
0084:             if self.active != kind or self._native_owner != (kind, threading.get_ident()):
0085:                 raise RuntimeError('Native completion does not own the active slot')
0086:             self._native_completed = True
0087: 
0088:     def wait_for_optional(self, kind, deadline):
0089:         """Wait for a scheduling opportunity; caller still acquires normally.
0090: 
0091:         No slot, image or native job is owned here. Both ordinary admission
0092:         and the pre-execute freshness callback run again after notification.
0093:         """
0094:         if kind not in ('yunet','sface'):raise ValueError('Unknown optional notification kind')
0095:         if type(deadline) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(deadline):
0096:             raise ValueError('Finite monotonic admission deadline required')
0097:         limit=self.yunet_slot_wait_ms if kind=='yunet' else self.sface_slot_wait_ms
0098:         if not limit:return False
0099:         began = self.clock()
0100:         until = min(deadline, began+limit/1000.)
0101:         with self.condition:
0102:             stats = self._slot_wait_stats if kind=='yunet' else self._sface_wait_stats
0103:             stats['calls'] += 1
0104:             try:
0105:                 while True:
0106:                     now = self.clock()
0107:                     if now >= until:
0108:                         stats['timeouts'] += 1; return False
0109:                     job = self.jobs.get(kind, {})
0110:                     if job.get('disabled', False) and not self._recovery_due(kind):
0111:                         stats['disabled'] += 1; return False
0112:                     estimate = max(self.budget, job.get('estimated_budget_ms', self.budget*1000.)/1000.)
0113:                     if (self.active is None and not self.waiting_primary
0114:                             and self.next_primary-now >= estimate+self.guard):
0115:                         stats['ready'] += 1; return True
0116:                     stats['condition_waits'] += 1
0117:                     # Releases the scheduler condition and the GIL. Existing
0118:                     # primary/optional completion notify_all wakes this wait.
0119:                     self.condition.wait(until-now)
0120:             finally:
0121:                 elapsed = max(0., self.clock()-began)*1000.
0122:                 stats['total_wall_ms'] += elapsed
0123:                 stats['maximum_wall_ms'] = max(stats['maximum_wall_ms'], elapsed)
0124: 
0125:     @contextmanager
0126:     def optional(self, kind):
0127:         if kind not in ('yunet', 'sface'):
0128:             raise ValueError('Unknown optional DPU task')
0129:         with self.condition:
0130:             stats = self.jobs.setdefault(kind, {'calls': 0, 'skipped': 0, 'overruns': 0,
0131:                 'budget_exceeded': 0, 'estimated_budget_ms': self.budget * 1000.,
0132:                 'maximum_ms': 0., 'disabled': False})
0133:             estimate = max(self.budget, stats['estimated_budget_ms'] / 1000.)
0134:             recovering = stats['disabled'] and self._recovery_due(kind)
0135:             acquired = ((not stats['disabled'] or recovering) and self.active is None and not self.waiting_primary
0136:                         and self.next_primary - self.clock() >= estimate + self.guard)
0137:             if acquired:
0138:                 self.active, started = kind, self.clock()
0139:                 finish_deadline = self.next_primary - self.guard
0140:                 if self.yunet_recovery is not None and kind == 'yunet':
0141:                     self._native_owner = (kind, threading.get_ident())
0142:                     self._native_completed = False
0143:                     if recovering:
0144:                         self.yunet_recovery.admitted(started)
0145:                         stats['disabled'] = False
0146:             else:
0147:                 stats['skipped'] += 1
0148:             rejection = ('disabled' if stats['disabled'] else 'active' if self.active is not None
0149:                          else 'primary_waiting' if self.waiting_primary else 'deadline')
0150:             diagnostic_deadline = self.next_primary-self.guard
0151:         trace = None
0152:         diagnostics = self.diagnostics
0153:         if diagnostics is not None and diagnostics.enabled:
0154:             try:trace = diagnostics.begin(kind,acquired,rejection,estimate*1000.,diagnostic_deadline)
0155:             except Exception:diagnostics.error()
0156:         body_failed = False
0157:         native_not_submitted = False
0158:         try:
0159:             yield acquired
0160:         except BaseException as exc:
0161:             body_failed = True
0162:             native_not_submitted = getattr(exc, 'native_not_submitted', False) is True
0163:             raise
0164:         finally:
0165:             if acquired:
0166:                 with self.condition:
0167:                     elapsed = self.clock() - started
0168:                     stats['calls'] += 1
0169:                     stats['maximum_ms'] = max(stats['maximum_ms'], elapsed * 1000.)
0170:                     if elapsed > estimate:
0171:                         stats['budget_exceeded'] += 1
0172:                         stats['estimated_budget_ms'] = min(30., elapsed * 1000. + 2.)
0173:                     # A one-off estimate miss within the admitted deadline is
0174:                     # different from blocking the primary workload. Enforce the
0175:                     # actual deadline plus a hard 30 ms optional occupancy cap.
0176:                     hardcap_exceeded = elapsed > .030
0177:                     guard_exceeded = self.clock() > finish_deadline
0178:                     if hardcap_exceeded or guard_exceeded:
0179:                         stats['overruns'] += 1
0180:                         stats['disabled'] = True
0181:                         stats['last_overrun'] = {'elapsed_ms': elapsed * 1000.,
0182:                             'finish_after_guard_ms': (self.clock() - finish_deadline) * 1000.,
0183:                             'waiting_primary': self.waiting_primary}
0184:                     if self.yunet_recovery is not None and kind == 'yunet':
0185:                         if hardcap_exceeded or guard_exceeded or (body_failed and not native_not_submitted):
0186:                             stats['disabled'] = True
0187:                             self.yunet_recovery.fault(self.clock(), completed=self._native_completed,
0188:                                 body_failed=body_failed, hardcap=hardcap_exceeded, guard=guard_exceeded,
0189:                                 primary_calls=self.primary_calls, primary_overruns=self.primary_overruns)
0190:                         elif self._native_completed:
0191:                             self.yunet_recovery.success()
0192:                         self._native_owner = None
0193:                         self._native_completed = False
0194:                     if trace is not None:
0195:                         try:diagnostics.finish(kind,trace,elapsed*1000.,
0196:                             (self.clock()-finish_deadline)*1000.,self.waiting_primary,
0197:                             hardcap_exceeded,guard_exceeded,body_failed)
0198:                         except Exception:diagnostics.error()
0199:                     self.active = None
0200:                     self.condition.notify_all()
0201: 
0202:     def health(self):
0203:         with self.condition:
0204:             result = {'active': self.active, 'primary_calls': self.primary_calls,
0205:                     'primary_period_overruns': self.primary_overruns,

0001: """YuNet DPU adapter with YOLO priority and explicit CPU fallback on overrun."""
0002: from board.vision.dpu_vision_runtime import (PinnedRunner, encode, make_yunet,
0003:     SlotAdmissionDenied, OptionalJobDisabled)
0004: from contextlib import contextmanager
0005: import hashlib
0006: from pathlib import Path
0007: import time
0008: import math
0009: 
0010: 
0011: class DpuYuNet:
0012:     kind = 'DPU_YUNET_INT8'
0013: 
0014:     def __init__(self, config, schedule, owner):
0015:         from board.vision.reference_faces import YuNetCompat
0016:         if schedule is None or owner is None:
0017:             raise ValueError('DPU YuNet requires the camera owner and scheduler')
0018:         for key in ('model', 'manifest'):
0019:             path = Path(config['face_backend'][key])
0020:             if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != config['face_backend'][key + '_sha256']:
0021:                 raise ValueError('Pinned YuNet artifact mismatch: ' + key)
0022:         self.schedule = schedule
0023:         self.runner = PinnedRunner(config['face_backend']['model'], config['face_backend']['manifest'], owner)
0024:         if self.runner.input_fix != 7 or list(self.runner.inputs[0].dims) != [1, 320, 320, 3]:
0025:             self.runner.close()
0026:             raise ValueError('Unexpected YuNet candidate input')
0027:         self.detector = make_yunet(config['face_model'], self.runner,
0028:             threshold=config['minimum_face_score'], execute=self.scheduled)
0029:         self.fallback = YuNetCompat(config['face_model'], score_threshold=config['minimum_face_score'], input_size=320)
0030: 
0031:     def scheduled(self, normalized):
0032:         # CPU input conversion is outside the DPU critical section.
0033:         source = encode(normalized, self.runner.input_fix)
0034:         check = getattr(self, '_admission_check', None)
0035:         if check is not None:
0036:             # An expired round must not seek another slot after preparation.
0037:             check()
0038:             return self.runner.execute_encoded(source,
0039:                 slot=self._guarded_slot(), pre_execute=check)
0040:         return self.runner.execute_encoded(source, slot=self._completed_slot())
0041: 
0042:     @contextmanager
0043:     def _completed_slot(self):
0044:         with self.schedule.optional('yunet') as acquired:
0045:             yield acquired
0046:             if acquired:
0047:                 self._confirm_completed()
0048: 
0049:     def _confirm_completed(self):
0050:         # PinnedRunner exits its slot only after wait returned status 0.
0051:         # Exceptions and pre-execute withdrawals never reach this marker.
0052:         marker = getattr(self.schedule, 'native_completed', None)
0053:         if marker is not None:
0054:             marker('yunet')
0055: 
0056:     @contextmanager
0057:     def _guarded_slot(self):
0058:         # Start the denial window BEFORE scheduler/diagnostic bookkeeping.
0059:         # This is a conservative admission-attempt time, not an exact sample
0060:         # of the scheduler's decision. Slow diagnostics cannot extend it.
0061:         admission_started_at = time.monotonic()
0062:         wait_ms = getattr(self.schedule, 'yunet_slot_wait_ms', 0)
0063:         check = getattr(self, '_admission_check', None)
0064:         until = admission_started_at+wait_ms/1000.
0065:         outer_deadline = getattr(check, 'deadline_monotonic', None)
0066:         if wait_ms and outer_deadline is not None:
0067:             if type(outer_deadline) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(outer_deadline):
0068:                 raise ValueError('Invalid face retry deadline')
0069:             until = min(until, outer_deadline)
0070:         attempted = False
0071:         while True:
0072:             if wait_ms and attempted and time.monotonic() >= until:
0073:                 if check is not None:check()
0074:                 raise SlotAdmissionDenied(admission_started_at)
0075:             with self.schedule.optional('yunet') as acquired:
0076:                 if acquired:
0077:                     yield True
0078:                     self._confirm_completed()
0079:                     return
0080:             attempted = True
0081:             if self.schedule.disabled('yunet'):
0082:                 raise OptionalJobDisabled('YuNet disabled by original DPU protection')
0083:             if not wait_ms:
0084:                 raise SlotAdmissionDenied(admission_started_at)
0085:             if not self.schedule.wait_for_optional('yunet', until):
0086:                 if check is not None:check()
0087:                 if self.schedule.disabled('yunet'):
0088:                     raise OptionalJobDisabled('YuNet disabled by original DPU protection')
0089:                 raise SlotAdmissionDenied(admission_started_at)
0090: 
0091:     def detect_with_admission_guard(self, image, check):
0092:         # FaceTargetService has one worker. Reject accidental nested use;
0093:         # this is not an API for sharing a runner across threads.
0094:         if getattr(self, '_admission_check', None) is not None:
0095:             raise RuntimeError('YuNet admission guard is already active')
0096:         check()
0097:         self._admission_check = check
0098:         try:
0099:             return self.detect(image)
0100:         finally:
0101:             self._admission_check = None
0102: 
0103:     def detect(self, image):
0104:         recovering = getattr(self.schedule, 'recovery_ready', lambda kind: False)
0105:         if self.schedule.disabled('yunet') and not recovering('yunet'):
0106:             self.kind = 'CPU_YUNET_FALLBACK_AFTER_DPU_OVERRUN'
0107:             return self.fallback.detect(image)
0108:         result = self.detector.detect(image)
0109:         self.kind = 'DPU_YUNET_INT8'
0110:         return result
0111: 
0112:     def close(self):
0113:         self.runner.close()

0061:         self.detector_info = detector_info or {'role':'CPU_FACE_PREVIEW','count_kind':'faces',
0062:             'detail':'CPU YuNet 人脸检测 + IoU 基础关联','target_label':'人脸'}
0063: 
0064:     def captured(self,stamp):
0065:         with self.lock:
0066:             self.capture_frames += 1
0067:             self.capture_stamp = stamp
0068:             self.camera_error = ''
0069:             return self.capture_frames
0070: 
0071:     def publish_preview(self,fid,stamp,jpeg):
0072:         with self.lock:
0073:             self.frames[fid] = (stamp,jpeg)
0074:             while len(self.frames)>8:self.frames.popitem(last=False)
0075:             self.preview_frames += 1
0076:             self.preview_error = ''
0077: 
0078:     def observed(self,fid,stamp,observation,inference_ms):
0079:         with self.lock:
0080:             self.processed_stamp = stamp
0081:             self.observation_fid = fid
0082:             self.observation = copy.deepcopy(observation)
0083:             self.inference_ms = inference_ms
0084:             if observation is not None:
0085:                 self.detected_frames += 1
0086:                 self.selected_id = observation['target_id']
0087: 
0088:     def overlay_observation(self):
0089:         with self.lock:
0090:             if (self.processed_stamp is None or not 0<=self.clock()-self.processed_stamp<250
0091:                     or self.detector_error):return None
0092:             return copy.deepcopy(self.observation)
0093: 
0094:     def identity_target(self):
0095:         with self.lock:
0096:             now = self.clock()
0097:             if self.identity and self.identity.face_targets:
0098:                 if self.camera_error or self.capture_stamp is None or not 0<=now-self.capture_stamp<1000:return None
0099:                 return self.identity.face_targets.current_target()
0100:             if (self.requested_target is not None or self.camera_error or self.detector_error
0101:                     or self.capture_stamp is None or not 0 <= now-self.capture_stamp < 1000
0102:                     or self.processed_stamp is None or not 0 <= now-self.processed_stamp < 750
0103:                     or self.observation is None):
0104:                 return None
0105:             track = next((t for t in self.observation['tracks'] if t['track_id'] == self.selected_id
0106:                           and t['visible'] and t['confirmed']), None)
0107:             return None if track is None else {'camera_epoch': self.camera_epoch, 'track_id': track['track_id'],
0108:                                                'track_generation': track['generation']}

0110:     def snapshot(self):
0111:         with self.lock:
0112:             now = self.clock();self.sequence += 1
0113:             fresh = self.capture_stamp is not None and 0<=now-self.capture_stamp<1000 and not self.camera_error
0114:             detected = (fresh and self.processed_stamp is not None and
0115:                         0<=now-self.processed_stamp<750 and self.observation is not None and not self.detector_error)
0116:             modules = {'camera':{'state':'READY' if fresh else 'ERROR' if self.camera_error else 'LOST',
0117:                         'detail':self.camera_error or 'USB 相机 · 640×480 · 镜像预览'},
0118:                        'vision':{'state':'READY' if detected else 'ERROR' if self.detector_error else 'DEGRADED',
0119:                         'detail':self.detector_error or self.detector_info['detail'],
0120:                         'count_kind':self.detector_info['count_kind']},
0121:                        'target':{'state':'NONE','selected':None,'tracks':[]}}
0122:             tracks = []
0123:             if self.raw_preview:modules['camera']['preview_transport']=TRANSPORT
0124:             if self.detector_info['role']=='DPU_YOLOX_PERSON_PREVIEW':
0125:                 modules['npu']={'state':'READY' if detected else 'ERROR' if self.detector_error else 'DEGRADED',
0126:                     'detail':self.detector_error or 'YOLOX-Nano · B4096 · 人体检测'}
0127:             if detected:
0128:                 tracks = [t for t in self.observation['tracks'] if t['visible'] and t['confirmed']][:16]
0129:                 modules['vision']['people_count'] = sum(t['visible'] for t in self.observation['tracks'])
0130:                 modules['target']['tracks'] = [{'key':{'camera_epoch':self.camera_epoch,'track_id':t['track_id'],
0131:                     'track_generation':t['generation']},'label':self.detector_info['target_label']+' #'+str(t['track_id'])} for t in tracks]
0132:                 selected = next((r['key'] for r in modules['target']['tracks'] if r['key']['track_id']==self.selected_id),None)
0133:                 modules['target'].update(state='CONFIRMED' if selected else 'CANDIDATE' if tracks else 'NONE',selected=selected)
0134:             elif self.observation is not None:
0135:                 modules['target']['state'] = 'LOST'
0136:             result = {'protocol_version':PROTOCOL,'board_boot_id':self.boot,'service_epoch':self.epoch,
0137:                 'sequence':self.sequence,'observed_mono_ms':now,'ttl_ms':2000,'source_kind':'actual',
0138:                 'context':{'session_id':self.session,'request_id':'','generation':0},'modules':modules,
0139:                 'capabilities':{a:bool(a=='target_select' and tracks) for a in ACTIONS}}
0140:             if self.identity:
0141:                 modules['identity'], capabilities = self.identity.view()
0142:                 result['capabilities'].update(capabilities)
0143:             elif self.identity_error:
0144:                 modules['identity'] = {'state': 'ERROR', 'detail': self.identity_error}
0145:             if fresh and self.frames:
0146:                 fid = next(reversed(self.frames));stamp,_ = self.frames[fid]
0147:                 if 0<=now-stamp<1000:
0148:                     result['preview'] = {'camera_epoch':self.camera_epoch,'frame_id':fid,'captured_mono_ms':stamp}
0149:             return result
0150: 
0151:     def preview(self,epoch,fid):
0152:         with self.lock:
0153:             value = self.frames.get(fid)
0154:             if (epoch != self.camera_epoch or value is None or not 0<=self.clock()-value[0]<1000
0155:                     or self.camera_error):return None
0156:             return value[1]
0157: 
0158:     def command(self,command):
0159:         with self.lock:
0160:             now = self.clock()
0161:             names = ('protocol_version','board_boot_id','service_epoch','command_id','ui_epoch','ui_generation')
0162:             owner = {k:command.get(k) for k in names}
0163:             try:
0164:                 if set(command)!=set(names)|{'context','snapshot_sequence','issued_mono_ms','deadline_mono_ms','action','params'}:
0165:                     raise ValueError('请求字段不完整或未知')
0166:                 if command.get('protocol_version')!=PROTOCOL or command.get('board_boot_id')!=self.boot or command.get('service_epoch')!=self.epoch:
0167:                     raise ValueError('请求属于其他服务或板卡启动')
0168:                 if (not isinstance(command.get('command_id'),str) or not 0<len(command['command_id'])<=96 or
0169:                         not isinstance(command.get('ui_epoch'),str) or not 0<len(command['ui_epoch'])<=96 or
0170:                         not integer(command.get('ui_generation'))):raise ValueError('请求归属无效')
0171:                 issued,deadline = command.get('issued_mono_ms'),command.get('deadline_mono_ms')
0172:                 if not (integer(issued) and integer(deadline) and issued<=now+100 and now<deadline<=issued+2500):
0173:                     raise ValueError('请求已过期或期限无效')
0174:                 if (command.get('context') != {'session_id':self.session,'request_id':'','generation':0} or
0175:                         not integer(command.get('snapshot_sequence'),1) or command['snapshot_sequence']>self.sequence):
0176:                     raise ValueError('请求状态上下文不匹配')
0177:                 action = command.get('action')
0178:                 if action not in ('target_select', 'identity_enroll', 'identity_delete','identity_sample_start','identity_sample_cancel'):
0179:                     raise ValueError('该服务仅提供视觉目标与身份操作')
0180:                 if command['command_id'] in self.receipts:
0181:                     previous,ack = self.receipts[command['command_id']]
0182:                     if previous!=command:raise ValueError('重复请求的内容发生变化')
0183:                     return copy.deepcopy(ack)
0184:                 snapshot = self.snapshot()
0185:                 params = validate_action(action,command.get('params'),snapshot)
0186:                 if action == 'target_select':
0187:                     self.requested_target = params['target']['track_id']
0188:                     self.requested_target_key = copy.deepcopy(params['target'])
0189:                     self.requested_target_deadline = deadline
0190:                     detail = '已受理目标选择，等待下一帧确认'
0191:                 elif action == 'identity_enroll':
0192:                     detail = self.identity.enroll(params, command['command_id'])
0193:                 elif action == 'identity_sample_start':
0194:                     detail = self.identity.sample_start(params,command['command_id'])
0195:                 elif action == 'identity_sample_cancel':
0196:                     detail = self.identity.sample_cancel(params,command['command_id'])
0197:                 else:
0198:                     detail = self.identity.delete(params, command['command_id'])
0199:                 ack = dict(owner,accepted=True,detail=detail)
0200:                 self.receipts[command['command_id']] = (copy.deepcopy(command),ack)
0201:                 while len(self.receipts)>128:self.receipts.popitem(last=False)
0202:                 return copy.deepcopy(ack)
0203:             except (ValueError,TypeError,sqlite3.Error) as exc:return dict(owner,accepted=False,detail=str(exc)[:160])
0204: 
0205:     def health(self):
0206:         with self.lock:
0207:             now = self.clock();seconds=max((now-self.started)/1000,.001)
0208:             return {'pid':os.getpid(),'board_boot_id':self.boot,'service_epoch':self.epoch,
0209:                 'camera_epoch':self.camera_epoch,'capture_frames':self.capture_frames,'detected_frames':self.detected_frames,
0210:                 'capture_age_ms':None if self.capture_stamp is None else now-self.capture_stamp,
0211:                 'observation_age_ms':None if self.processed_stamp is None else now-self.processed_stamp,
0212:                 'capture_hz':round(self.capture_frames/seconds,2),'detection_hz':round(self.detected_frames/seconds,2),
0213:                 'preview_frames':self.preview_frames,'preview_hz':round(self.preview_frames/seconds,2),
0214:                 'observation_frame_id':self.observation_fid,'preview_error':self.preview_error,
0215:                 'last_inference_ms':self.inference_ms,'preview_buffers':len(self.frames),
0216:                 'camera_error':self.camera_error,'detector_error':self.detector_error,
0217:                 'device':copy.deepcopy(self.device),
0218:                 'role':self.detector_info['role'],'detector':copy.deepcopy(self.detector_info),
0219:                 'people_count':None if self.observation is None or self.processed_stamp is None or
0220:                     not 0<=now-self.processed_stamp<750 or self.detector_error or self.camera_error else
0221:                     sum(t['visible'] for t in self.observation['tracks']),
0222:                 'identity_recognition':bool(self.identity and self.identity.health()['ready']),
0223:                 'identity':self.identity.health() if self.identity else {'ready':False,'error':self.identity_error},
0224:                 'dpu_schedule':self.dpu_schedule.health() if self.dpu_schedule else None,
0225:                 'motion_command_emitted':False,
0226:                 'pipeline_metrics':self.pipeline_metrics.snapshot(),
0227:                 'timestamp_kind':'appsink_dequeue_monotonic','mirror_horizontal':True}

0230: def handler_for(state):
0231:     class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
0232:         def log_message(self,*_):pass
0233:         def respond(self,code,value,mime='application/json',headers=None):
0234:             body = value if isinstance(value,bytes) else json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,allow_nan=False).encode()
0235:             self.send_response(code);self.send_header('Content-Type',mime)
0236:             self.send_header('Content-Length',str(len(body)));self.send_header('Cache-Control','no-store')
0237:             for k,v in (headers or {}).items():self.send_header(k,v)
0238:             self.end_headers()
0239:             try:self.wfile.write(body)
0240:             except (BrokenPipeError,ConnectionResetError):pass
0241:         def do_GET(self):
0242:             path = urlsplit(self.path)
0243:             if path.path=='/v1/ui/status':self.respond(200,state.snapshot())
0244:             elif path.path=='/health':self.respond(200,state.health())
0245:             elif path.path=='/v1/ui/preview.jpg':
0246:                 try:
0247:                     args=parse_qs(path.query);epoch=args['camera_epoch'][0];fid=int(args['frame_id'][0])
0248:                     jpeg=state.preview(epoch,fid)
0249:                     if jpeg is None:raise ValueError()
0250:                     self.respond(200,jpeg,'image/jpeg',{'X-Aura-Boot-Id':state.boot,
0251:                         'X-Aura-Camera-Epoch':epoch,'X-Aura-Frame-Id':str(fid)})
0252:                 except (KeyError,ValueError,IndexError):self.respond(404,{'error':'画面已过期或换代'})
0253:             elif path.path=='/v1/ui/identity-face.png':
0254:                 try:
0255:                     args=parse_qs(path.query)
0256:                     key={'camera_epoch':args['camera_epoch'][0], 'track_id':int(args['track_id'][0]),
0257:                          'track_generation':int(args['track_generation'][0])}
0258:                     fid=int(args['frame_id'][0])
0259:                     crop=state.identity.face_preview(key,fid) if state.identity else None
0260:                     if crop is None:raise ValueError()
0261:                     self.respond(200,crop,'image/png',{'X-Aura-Boot-Id':state.boot,
0262:                         'X-Aura-Service-Epoch':state.epoch,'X-Aura-Camera-Epoch':key['camera_epoch'],
0263:                         'X-Aura-Frame-Id':str(fid)})
0264:                 except (KeyError,ValueError,IndexError):self.respond(404,{'error':'人脸已失效，请重新正对摄像头'})
0265:             else:self.respond(404,{'error':'未知接口'})
0266:         def do_POST(self):
0267:             if self.path!='/v1/ui/commands':self.respond(404,{});return
0268:             try:
0269:                 size=int(self.headers.get('Content-Length','0'))
0270:                 if not 0<size<=8192 or self.headers.get('Content-Type','').split(';')[0]!='application/json':raise ValueError()
0271:                 command=json.loads(self.rfile.read(size))
0272:                 if not isinstance(command,dict):raise ValueError()
0273:                 self.respond(200,state.command(command))
0274:             except (ValueError,TypeError):self.respond(400,{'error':'请求格式无效'})
0275:     from board.identity.calibration_http import wrap_handler
0276:     return wrap_handler(Handler,state)

0279: class PersonPreviewTracks:
0280:     """Reuse the pinned geometry-only IoU baseline; no identity or face output."""
0281:     def __init__(self,epoch,reference):
0282:         self.reference=reference(epoch)
0283:         self.last_visible=set()
0284:         self.generations={}
0285: 
0286:     @property
0287:     def tracks(self):return self.reference.tracks
0288: 
0289:     @property
0290:     def target_id(self):return self.reference.target_id
0291: 
0292:     @target_id.setter
0293:     def target_id(self,value):self.reference.target_id=value
0294: 
0295:     def update(self,people,fid,stamp,width,height):
0296:         # The existing association uses only xywh and score. Padding is private
0297:         # compatibility data; it is never exposed or used as face landmarks.
0298:         rows=[list(d.box)+[0.]*10+[d.score] for d in people]
0299:         observation=self.reference.update(rows,fid,stamp,width,height)
0300:         current=set()
0301:         for track in observation['tracks']:
0302:             tid=track['track_id']
0303:             if track['visible']:
0304:                 if tid not in self.last_visible:self.generations[tid]=self.generations.get(tid,0)+1
0305:                 current.add(tid)
0306:             track['generation']=self.generations.get(tid,1)
0307:             self.reference.tracks[tid]['generation']=track['generation']
0308:         self.last_visible=current
0309:         self.generations={k:v for k,v in self.generations.items() if k in self.reference.tracks}
0310:         observation['association']='PERSON_IOU_BASELINE'
0311:         return observation

0314: def detector_info(config):
0315:     if config.get('detector_backend','cpu_yunet')=='dpu_yolox':
0316:         tracking=config.get('tracking',{}).get('backend')=='bytetrack_numpy'
0317:         return {'role':'DPU_YOLOX_PERSON_PREVIEW','count_kind':'people','target_label':'人体',
0318:             'detail':'DPU YOLOX-Nano 人体检测 + '+('ByteTrack 跟踪' if tracking else 'IoU 基础关联'),'model':'YOLOX-Nano',
0319:             'tracking_backend':'BYTETRACK_PINNED_NUMPY' if tracking else 'PERSON_IOU_BASELINE',
0320:             'model_sha256':config['yolo']['model_sha256'],'arch_fingerprint':config['yolo']['arch_fingerprint']}
0321:     return None

0324: def checked_backend(config):
0325:     import cv2
0326:     import numpy as np
0327:     loaded = {}
0328:     directory = Path(config['backend_dir'])
0329:     for name,expected in config['backend_sha256'].items():
0330:         path=directory/name
0331:         if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=expected:raise ValueError('Backend hash mismatch: '+name)
0332:         spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('_aura_camera_'+path.stem,str(path))
0333:         module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(module);loaded[name]=module
0334:     cv2.setNumThreads(2)
0335:     if config.get('detector_backend','cpu_yunet')=='dpu_yolox':
0336:         from board.vision.yolo_dpu import DpuYoloX, checked_file
0337:         detector=DpuYoloX(config['yolo'])
0338:         try:
0339:             reference_path=checked_file(config['yolo']['reference_image'],config['yolo']['reference_sha256'])
0340:             reference=cv2.imread(str(reference_path))
0341:             if reference is None:raise RuntimeError('YOLO reference image cannot be read')
0342:             count=sum(d.score>=.3 for d in detector.detect_bgr(reference))
0343:             blank=len(detector.detect_bgr(np.zeros((480,640,3),np.uint8)))
0344:             if count<2 or blank:raise RuntimeError('YOLO fixed reference or blank check failed')
0345:             detector.reference_check={'reference_people':count,'blank_people':blank}
0346:         except BaseException:
0347:             detector.close()
0348:             raise
0349:         backend=config.get('tracking',{}).get('backend','iou')
0350:         if backend=='bytetrack_numpy':
0351:             from board.vision.person_bytetrack import PersonByteTracks
0352:             if config['yolo']['score_threshold']!=.1:
0353:                 detector.close();raise ValueError('ByteTrack requires low-score person detections')
0354:             return cv2,np,detector,lambda epoch:PersonByteTracks(epoch,config['tracking'])
0355:         if backend!='iou':
0356:             detector.close();raise ValueError('Unknown tracking backend')
0357:         return cv2,np,detector,lambda epoch:PersonPreviewTracks(epoch,loaded['vision_core.py'].FaceTracks)
0358:     if config.get('detector_backend','cpu_yunet')!='cpu_yunet':raise ValueError('Unknown detector backend')
0359:     detector=loaded['face_compat_fast.py'].YuNetCompat(Path(config['model_root'])/'models/yunet_2022mar.onnx',input_size=192)
0360:     reference=cv2.imread(str(Path(config['model_root'])/'reference/lena.jpg'))
0361:     if reference is None or len(detector.detect(reference))!=1 or len(detector.detect(np.zeros((480,640,3),np.uint8)))!=0:
0362:         raise RuntimeError('Existing detector reference checks failed')
0363:     return cv2,np,detector,loaded['vision_core.py'].FaceTracks

0366: def video_owners(device,proc_root=Path('/proc')):
0367:     device=os.path.realpath(device)
0368:     owners=[]
0369:     for folder in proc_root.iterdir():
0370:         if not folder.name.isdigit() or int(folder.name)==os.getpid():continue
0371:         try:
0372:             for fd in (folder/'fd').iterdir():
0373:                 try:
0374:                     if os.path.realpath(str(fd))==device:
0375:                         owners.append(int(folder.name));break
0376:                 except OSError:pass
0377:         except OSError:pass
0378:     return owners

0381: def run(config):
0382:     # Resolve again on every process restart: USB reconnects can change videoN.
0383:     device=resolve_camera_device(config['device'],identity=CAMERA_IDENTITY if config['device']==PINNED_CAMERA else None)
0384:     import gi
0385:     gi.require_version('Gst','1.0')
0386:     from gi.repository import Gst
0387:     cv2,np,detector,Tracks=checked_backend(config)
0388:     Gst.init(None)
0389:     state=CameraState(Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip(),detector_info=detector_info(config),device=device)
0390:     if config.get('detector_backend') == 'dpu_yolox':
0391:         from board.vision.dpu_vision_schedule import DpuVisionSchedule
0392:         state.dpu_schedule = DpuVisionSchedule(diagnostics=state.pipeline_metrics.optional_diagnostics,
0393:             yunet_recovery=config.get('yunet_dpu_recovery', False),
0394:             yunet_slot_wait_ms=config.get('yunet_slot_wait_ms', 0),
0395:             sface_slot_wait_ms=config.get('sface_slot_wait_ms', 0))
0396:     if detector is not None:detector.pipeline_metrics=state.pipeline_metrics
0397:     stop=threading.Event();condition=threading.Condition();latest=[None]
0398:     capture=None;server=None;thread=None;worker=None;preview_worker=None;raw=None
0399:     lock_path=Path('/run/aura-camera-ui.lock')
0400:     with lock_path.open('a+') as ownership:
0401:         fcntl.flock(ownership,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
0402:         owners=video_owners(device['resolved'])
0403:         if owners:raise RuntimeError('Camera already owned by PID '+str(owners))
0404:         if config.get('identity', {}).get('enabled'):
0405:             try:
0406:                 from board.identity.camera_faces import create_service
0407:                 state.identity = create_service(AURA, config['identity']['config'], state,
0408:                     schedule=state.dpu_schedule, owner=detector)
0409:             except Exception as exc:
0410:                 state.identity_error = ('身份服务不可用：'+str(exc))[:150]
0411:         tracker=Tracks(state.camera_epoch)
0412:         raw=RawFrameStore({'board_boot_id':state.boot,'service_epoch':state.epoch,'camera_epoch':state.camera_epoch})
0413:         state.raw_preview=True
0414:         def sample(sink):
0415:             stage_started=time.monotonic_ns()
0416:             try:
0417:                 item=sink.emit('pull-sample')
0418:                 if item is None:return Gst.FlowReturn.EOS
0419:                 buffer=item.get_buffer();caps=item.get_caps().get_structure(0)
0420:                 w,h=caps.get_value('width'),caps.get_value('height')
0421:                 data=buffer.extract_dup(0,buffer.get_size())
0422:                 if (w,h)!=(640,480) or len(data)!=w*h*3:raise ValueError('Unexpected BGR capture layout')
0423:                 image=np.frombuffer(data,dtype=np.uint8).reshape(h,w,3)
0424:                 stamp=millis();fid=state.captured(stamp)
0425:                 source=cv2.flip(image,1)
0426:                 state.pipeline_metrics.elapsed('capture.callback_prepare',stage_started)
0427:                 with condition:latest[0]=(fid,stamp,source);condition.notify_all()
0428:                 if state.identity and state.identity.face_targets:state.identity.face_targets.offer(source,fid,stamp)
0429:                 return Gst.FlowReturn.OK
0430:             except Exception as exc:
0431:                 state.camera_error=str(exc)[:150];stop.set();return Gst.FlowReturn.ERROR
0432:         def inference():
0433:             nonlocal detector
0434:             previous=0
0435:             while not stop.is_set():
0436:                 started=time.monotonic()
0437:                 with condition:

0485:         def preview():

0487:             while not stop.is_set():

0001: """Experimental process-local interpreter interval around the pinned entrypoint."""
0002: import hashlib
0003: import json
0004: import os
0005: from pathlib import Path
0006: import runpy
0007: import sys
0008: 
0009: CAMERA_SHA='1e0e6f0b8881c2d4d4cf81a2527e79d063e673eeba1d069e33bdde2608303005'
0010: ALLOWED_ROOT=Path('/home/root/aura-dpu-vision-20261021/postpower-retry-live')
0011: 
0012: 
0013: def launch(argv,*,allowed_root=ALLOWED_ROOT,expected_sha=CAMERA_SHA,
0014:            record_directory=None,runner=runpy.run_path):
0015:     selected=os.environ.get('AURA_EXPERIMENT_GIL_MS')
0016:     if selected not in ('1','5'):raise ValueError('Explicit 1ms or 5ms experiment required')
0017:     if not argv:raise ValueError('Pinned camera entrypoint required')
0018:     target=Path(argv[0])
0019:     if (not target.is_absolute() or target.resolve(strict=True)!=target
0020:             or allowed_root.resolve() not in target.parents or target.name!='board_camera_motion.py'
0021:             or hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=expected_sha):
0022:         raise ValueError('Camera entrypoint differs from pinned experiment')
0023:     record_directory=Path(record_directory) if record_directory is not None else Path(__file__).parent
0024:     old_interval=sys.getswitchinterval();old_argv=sys.argv
0025:     try:
0026:         sys.setswitchinterval(int(selected)/1000.)
0027:         actual=sys.getswitchinterval()
0028:         if abs(actual-int(selected)/1000.)>1e-9:raise RuntimeError('Interpreter interval was not applied')
0029:         record={'pid':os.getpid(),'requested_ms':int(selected),'actual_start_seconds':actual,
0030:             'previous_seconds':old_interval,'target':str(target),'target_sha256':expected_sha,
0031:             'scope':'interpreter startup setting in this camera process only; not OS timeslice or hardware deadline'}
0032:         with (record_directory/('process-setting-'+str(os.getpid())+'.json')).open('x') as f:
0033:             json.dump(record,f)
0034:         sys.argv=list(argv)
0035:         return runner(str(target),run_name='__main__')
0036:     finally:
0037:         sys.argv=old_argv;sys.setswitchinterval(old_interval)
0038: 
0039: 
0040: if __name__=='__main__':launch(sys.argv[1:])

0001: """Stable entrypoint for the byte-identical validated process-local GIL wrapper."""
0002: import hashlib
0003: import importlib.util
0004: from pathlib import Path
0005: import sys
0006: import tempfile
0007: import uuid
0008: 
0009: WRAPPER_SHA='0cec741d143a012b5d5aa5808f404e7b818510d2773f3b122dd1fb639222ae02'
0010: 
0011: 
0012: def load_wrapper(path):
0013:     if path.is_symlink() or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=WRAPPER_SHA:
0014:         raise RuntimeError('Validated runtime wrapper differs')
0015:     spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('_validated_camera_wrapper',path)
0016:     module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(module)
0017:     return module
0018: 
0019: 
0020: def launch(argv,*,root=None,boot_id=None,loader=load_wrapper):
0021:     root=Path(root) if root is not None else Path(__file__).resolve().parent
0022:     boot_id=boot_id if boot_id is not None else Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip()
0023:     if str(uuid.UUID(boot_id))!=boot_id:raise ValueError('Canonical boot UUID required')
0024:     if not argv:raise ValueError('Camera entrypoint required')
0025:     if root.resolve()!=root or (root/'process-settings').is_symlink():
0026:         raise ValueError('Runtime record parent redirects outside release')
0027:     directory=root/'process-settings'/boot_id
0028:     directory.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True,mode=0o700)
0029:     if directory.resolve()!=directory:raise ValueError('Runtime record path redirects outside release')
0030:     # PID values can repeat across boots or after PID wrap. Keep every launch
0031:     # in an owned directory; never overwrite an older validation record.
0032:     record=Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='launch-',dir=str(directory)))
0033:     wrapper=loader(root/'gil_camera_launcher.py')
0034:     return wrapper.launch(argv,record_directory=record)
0035: 
0036: 
0037: if __name__=='__main__':launch(sys.argv[1:])

SHA256: 42159f8b5a37c8bd0a86628df035d959cfd8ce8558b3433bf6b7b9fd01cfb621

0001: """Opt-in loopback control wrapper. Never serves images, features or names."""
0002: import ipaddress
0003: import json
0004: import secrets
0005: 
0006: PATH = '/v1/ui/sface-calibration'
0007: 
0008: 
0009: def _unique_object(pairs):
0010:     result = {}
0011:     for key, value in pairs:
0012:         if key in result: raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON field')
0013:         result[key] = value
0014:     return result
0015: 
0016: 
0017: def _invalid_constant(_):
0018:     raise ValueError('Non-finite JSON value')
0019: 
0020: 
0021: def wrap_handler(original, state):
0022:     class CalibrationHandler(original):
0023:         def do_POST(self):
0024:             if self.path != PATH:
0025:                 return super().do_POST()
0026:             try:
0027:                 if not ipaddress.ip_address(self.client_address[0]).is_loopback:
0028:                     self.respond(403, {'error': 'Calibration control is loopback only'})
0029:                     return
0030:             except ValueError:
0031:                 self.respond(403, {'error': 'Calibration control is loopback only'})
0032:                 return
0033:             with state.lock:
0034:                 service = state.identity
0035:                 extractor = getattr(service, 'extractor', None)
0036:                 controller = getattr(extractor, 'calibration', None)
0037:                 config = (getattr(extractor, 'config', {}) or {}).get('dpu_sface_calibration') or {}
0038:                 capability = config.get('control_token', '')
0039:                 if not controller or config.get('enabled') is not True:
0040:                     self.respond(404, {'error': 'Calibration control disabled'})
0041:                     return
0042:             supplied = self.headers.get_all('Authorization') or []
0043:             if (len(supplied) != 1 or not isinstance(capability, str) or len(capability) != 64
0044:                     or not secrets.compare_digest(supplied[0], 'Bearer '+capability)):
0045:                 self.respond(403, {'error': 'Calibration capability required'})
0046:                 return
0047:             try:
0048:                 lengths = self.headers.get_all('Content-Length') or []
0049:                 if (len(lengths) != 1 or self.headers.get('Transfer-Encoding')
0050:                         or self.headers.get('Content-Type', '').split(';')[0] != 'application/json'):
0051:                     raise ValueError('Bounded JSON request required')
0052:                 size = int(lengths[0])
0053:                 if not 0 < size <= 4096: raise ValueError('Request too large or empty')
0054:                 previous_timeout = self.connection.gettimeout()
0055:                 try:
0056:                     self.connection.settimeout(2.)
0057:                     raw = self.rfile.read(size)
0058:                 finally:
0059:                     self.connection.settimeout(previous_timeout)
0060:                 if len(raw) != size: raise ValueError('Incomplete request')
0061:                 command = json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=_unique_object, parse_constant=_invalid_constant)
0062:                 if (not isinstance(command, dict) or set(command) != {'action', 'params'}
0063:                         or not isinstance(command['action'], str)):
0064:                     raise ValueError('Exact action and params required')
0065:                 if command['action'] == 'start':
0066:                     # Target and start are atomic relative to camera state. No
0067:                     # stats calculation or DPU work happens under this lock.
0068:                     with state.lock:
0069:                         if (state.identity is not service or getattr(service, 'extractor', None) is not extractor
0070:                                 or not getattr(getattr(extractor, 'shadow', None), 'ready', False)):
0071:                             raise ValueError('Calibration DPU worker not ready')
0072:                         result = controller.dispatch('start', command['params'], current_target=state.identity_target())
0073:                 else:
0074:                     with state.lock:
0075:                         controller.observe_target(state.identity_target())
0076:                     # Reports take their own bounded RAM snapshot. Camera state
0077:                     # stays unlocked during score aggregation/search.
0078:                     result = controller.dispatch(command['action'], command['params'])
0079:                 self.respond(200, result)
0080:             except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, UnicodeError, RecursionError):
0081:                 self.respond(400, {'error': 'Invalid calibration control or insufficient coverage'})
0082:             except (OSError, TimeoutError):
0083:                 self.respond(400, {'error': 'Incomplete bounded calibration request'})
0084:     return CalibrationHandler

0001: """Natural-motion enrollment: a bounded video window and held-out validation."""
0002: import copy

0005: 
0006:

0008: COLLECTION_MS = 15000
0009: MAX_POOL = 24
0010: FRONT_YAW_MAX = .22
0011: FRONT_ROLL_MAX = 15.
0012: MIN_FRONT_FRAMES = 3
0013: GUIDES = {'collect': '请看着镜头慢慢自然转头，正在自动挑选清晰人脸',
0014:           'validate': '选样完成，请自然看向镜头，正在用新画面验证',
0015:           'ready': '新画面验证通过，可以确认称呼后保存', 'failed': '采样或验证未通过，请重新采样'}
0016: 
0017: 
0018: def pose_quality(face, blur):
0019:     """Five-landmark geometric guide, not a calibrated head-pose model."""
0020:     ex, ey = face[6]-face[4], face[7]-face[5]
0021:     distance = math.hypot(ex, ey)
0022:     if distance < 1:
0023:         return None
0024:     yaw = ((face[8]-(face[4]+face[6])/2)*ex + (face[9]-(face[5]+face[7])/2)*ey)/(distance*distance)
0025:     return {'yaw': yaw, 'roll': math.degrees(math.atan2(ey, ex)),
0026:             'pixels': min(face[2:4]), 'eye_distance': distance, 'blur': blur}
0027: 
0028: 
0029: class EnrollmentSampling:
0030:     policy = 'frontal_medoid_v1'
0031:     def __init__(self, threshold, ambiguity_margin, clock, photo_archive=None):
0032:         self.threshold, self.margin, self.clock = threshold, ambiguity_margin, clock
0033:         self.photo_archive=photo_archive
0034:         self.reset()
0035: 
0036:     def reset(self,outcome='cancelled',revision=None):

0038:             self.photo_archive.finish(self.session_id,outcome,revision)
0039:         self.last_failure = getattr(self,'last_failure',None)

0041:         self.started = self.until = 0; self.last_fid = self.last_stamp = -1

0043:         self.detail = ''; self.validation_scores = []; self.validation_frame_ids=[]; self.missing_since = None
0044:         self.replace_id = ''

0047:                       'validation_passes':0,'score_rejections':0,'margin_rejections':0,'best_validation_run':0,
0048:                       'recent_validation':[],'front_pose_skips':0,'candidate_pose':None}
0049: 
0050:     def start(self, owner, revision, replace_id='', anchor=None):
0051:         self.reset(); self.session_id = uuid.uuid4().hex; self.owner = copy.deepcopy(owner)
0052:         self.last_failure = None

0066:     def check(self, owner, revision):
0067:         if self.stage not in ('idle', 'failed'):
0068:             if revision != self.revision or (owner is not None
0069:                     and owner['camera_epoch'] != self.owner['camera_epoch']):
0070:                 self.fail('对象或身份库已变化，请重新采样')
0071:             elif self.clock() >= self.until:
0072:                 self.fail('采样或确认已超时，请重新采样')
0073:             elif owner != self.owner:
0074:                 if self.missing_since is None:self.missing_since = self.clock()
0075:             else:
0076:                 self.missing_since = None

0078:     def public(self):

0082:                 'total': sum(self.counts.values()), 'total_required': 15,

0087:     def authorizes(self, owner):
0088:         return owner is not None and owner == self.owner and self.missing_since is None
0089: 
0090:     def usable(self, vector, existing_best):
0091:         if self.candidate is None:
0092:             return False
0093:         score = sum(a*b for a,b in zip(vector,self.candidate['embedding']))
0094:         return score >= self.threshold and (existing_best is None or score-existing_best >= self.margin)
0095: 
0096:     @staticmethod
0097:     def frontal(pose):
0098:         # This ratio is a five-landmark hint, not a calibrated angle. Natural
0099:         # side views can be collected, but cannot certify a frontal template.
0100:         return abs(pose['yaw'])<=FRONT_YAW_MAX and abs(pose['roll'])<=FRONT_ROLL_MAX

0102:     def _keep(self,result,quality):
0103:         self.training.append({'embedding':result['embedding'],'fid':result['fid'],
0104:             'stamp':result['stamp'],'face_png':result.get('face_png'),
0105:             'pose':dict(quality),'quality':min(quality['blur'],400.)+min(quality['pixels'],240.)})
0106:         if len(self.training)>MAX_POOL:
0107:             # A long, sharp profile must not evict the earlier usable front.
0108:             self.training.remove(min(self.training,key=lambda row:(self.frontal(row['pose']),row['quality'])))
0109: 
0110:     def _choose_front(self):
0111:         front=[row for row in self.training if self.frontal(row['pose'])]
0112:         if len(front)<MIN_FRONT_FRAMES:return False
0113:         # Limit the representative vote to the most frontal observed faces;
0114:         # time spent turned sideways must not dominate the saved template.
0115:         front=sorted(front,key=lambda row:(abs(row['pose']['yaw']),abs(row['pose']['roll']),-row['quality']))[:5]
0116:         def representative(row):
0117:             return (sum(sum(a*b for a,b in zip(row['embedding'],other['embedding'])) for other in front),
0118:                     row['quality'])
0119:         self.candidate=max(front,key=representative)
0120:         self.metrics['candidate_pose']={k:round(float(v),4) for k,v in self.candidate['pose'].items()}
0121:         if self.photo_archive:self.photo_archive.select(self.session_id,self.candidate)
0122:         self.training=[]
0123:         return True
0124: 
0125:     def _photo(self,result,quality,decision,score=None,gap=None):
0126:         if self.photo_archive:self.photo_archive.sample(self.session_id,result,quality,decision,score,gap)
0127: 
0128:     def offer(self, result, quality, existing_best):
0129:         self.check(result['target'], result['revision'])
0130:         if self.stage not in (*COUNTS,'ready') or quality is None:
0131:             return
0132:         fid, stamp = result['fid'], result['stamp']
0133:         if fid <= self.last_fid or stamp < self.started or stamp-self.last_stamp < 500:
0134:             return
0135:         if (not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in quality.values()) or quality['pixels'] < 72
0136:                 or quality['eye_distance'] < 18 or quality['blur'] < 40 or abs(quality['roll']) > 20
0137:                 or abs(quality['yaw']) > .38):
0138:             self.metrics['quality_skips']+=1
0139:             if result['target']==self.owner:self._photo(result,quality,'quality_rejected')
0140:             self.detail = '这帧已跳过，请慢慢转头、双眼朝向镜头并保持清晰'; return
0141:         if result['target'] != self.owner:
0142:             # Recovered detector tracks are not proof of the same person. Bind
0143:             # one only after a fresh, qualified SFace observation matches the
0144:             # ORIGINAL session anchor, with the unchanged identity threshold
0145:             # and gallery margin. Never chain successively changing anchors.
0146:             score = None if self.anchor is None else sum(a*b for a,b in zip(result['embedding'],self.anchor))
0147:             if score is None or score < self.threshold or (existing_best is not None and score-existing_best < self.margin):
0148:                 self.metrics['recovery_rejections']+=1
0149:                 self.detail = '选样已保留，请看向镜头，正在核对是否同一人'; return
0150:             self.owner = copy.deepcopy(result['target']); self.missing_since = None
0151:         if self.anchor is None:self.anchor = result['embedding']
0152:         if self.stage=='ready':return
0153:         phase = self.stage
0154:         self.last_fid, self.last_stamp = fid, stamp; self.detail = ''
0155:         if phase == 'validate':
0156:             if not self.frontal(quality):
0157:                 self._photo(result,quality,'pose_rejected')
0158:                 self.metrics['front_pose_skips']+=1
0159:                 self.validation_scores=[];self.validation_frame_ids=[];self.counts['validate']=0
0160:                 self.detail='请自然看向镜头稍停，正在等待清晰正脸验证';return
0161:             if self.candidate is None:
0162:                 self._photo(result,quality,'front_selection')
0163:                 # The transition already triggers the existing spoken cue.
0164:                 # If the video window contained only profiles, gather fronts
0165:                 # now, then require FIVE LATER frames; never count selection
0166:                 # frames as their own held-out validation.
0167:                 self._keep(result,quality);self._choose_front()
0168:                 self.detail='正在补充清晰正脸，请继续看向镜头稍停';return
0169:             score=sum(a*b for a,b in zip(result['embedding'],self.candidate['embedding']))
0170:             gap=None if existing_best is None else score-existing_best
0171:             self.metrics['validation_attempts']+=1
0172:             self.metrics['recent_validation'].append({'frame_id':fid,'score':round(score,6),
0173:                 'other_identity_gap':None if gap is None else round(gap,6)})
0174:             self.metrics['recent_validation']=self.metrics['recent_validation'][-24:]
0175:             self._photo(result,quality,'validation_pass' if self.usable(result['embedding'],existing_best) else 'validation_rejected',score,gap)
0176:             if not self.usable(result['embedding'], existing_best):
0177:                 self.metrics['score_rejections']+=int(score<self.threshold)
0178:                 self.metrics['margin_rejections']+=int(gap is not None and gap<self.margin)
0179:                 # Require a new consecutive run; a poor frame never erases the
0180:                 # selection window, lowers the threshold, or counts as a pass.
0181:                 self.validation_scores = [];self.validation_frame_ids=[]; self.counts['validate'] = 0
0182:                 self.detail = '这一帧验证不足，已保留选样；请看向镜头稍停，正在继续验证'; return
0183:             self.validation_scores.append(sum(a*b for a,b in zip(result['embedding'],self.candidate['embedding'])))
0184:             self.validation_frame_ids.append(fid)
0185:             self.metrics['validation_passes']+=1
0186:         else:
0187:             self._photo(result,quality,'collect')
0188:             self._keep(result,quality)
0189:         self.counts[phase] = min(COUNTS[phase], self.counts[phase]+1)
0190:         self.metrics['best_validation_run']=max(self.metrics['best_validation_run'],self.counts['validate'])
0191:         if self.counts[phase] == COUNTS[phase]:
0192:             if phase == 'collect':
0193:                 if stamp-self.started < COLLECTION_MS:
0194:                     self.detail = '已收集清晰人脸，请继续慢慢自然转头，正在完成视频选样'; return
0195:                 self._choose_front()
0196:                 if self.candidate is None:self.training=[row for row in self.training if self.frontal(row['pose'])]
0197:                 self.stage = 'validate'
0198:             else:
0199:                 self.stage = 'ready'; self.until = self.clock()+60000
0200:                 if self.photo_archive:self.photo_archive.validated(self.session_id,self.validation_frame_ids,self.validation_scores)

0027: def calibration_configuration(config):
0028:     value = config.get('dpu_sface_calibration')
0029:     if value is None:
0030:         return None
0031:     if not isinstance(value, dict) or type(value.get('enabled')) is not bool:
0032:         raise ValueError('Invalid calibration configuration')
0033:     if not value['enabled']:
0034:         if set(value) != {'enabled'}:
0035:             raise ValueError('Disabled calibration accepts only enabled=false')
0036:         return None
0037:     shadow = config.get('dpu_sface_shadow') or {}
0038:     if (set(value) != {'enabled', 'mode', 'control_token'}
0039:             or value['mode'] != 'CALIBRATION_ONLY'
0040:             or not isinstance(value['control_token'], str)
0041:             or not HEX64.fullmatch(value['control_token'])
0042:             or shadow.get('enabled') is not True or shadow.get('compare_cosine') is not False):
0043:         raise ValueError('Explicit CALIBRATION_ONLY capability and independent shadow required')
0044:     return dict(value)
0045: 
0046: 
0047: def target_tuple(value):
0048:     if (not isinstance(value, dict)
0049:             or set(value) != {'camera_epoch', 'track_id', 'track_generation'}
0050:             or not isinstance(value['camera_epoch'], str)
0051:             or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}', value['camera_epoch'])
0052:             or any(type(value[k]) is not int or not 1 <= value[k] <= 2**31-1
0053:                    for k in ('track_id', 'track_generation'))):
0054:         raise ValueError('Explicit current target with generation required')
0055:     return value['camera_epoch'], value['track_id'], value['track_generation']
0056: 
0057: 
0058: def normalized(values):
0059:     values = tuple(values)
0060:     if (len(values) != 128 or any(not isinstance(v, Real) or isinstance(v, bool) or not math.isfinite(v)
0061:                                   for v in values)):
0062:         raise ValueError('Invalid calibration embedding')
0063:     # VART postprocessing returns numpy.float32 scalars, not Python floats.
0064:     # Convert to standard scalars without importing numpy in this pure module.
0065:     values = tuple(float(v) for v in values)
0066:     norm = math.sqrt(sum(v*v for v in values))
0067:     if not math.isfinite(norm) or norm <= 1e-12:
0068:         raise ValueError('Zero calibration embedding')
0069:     return tuple(v/norm for v in values)
0070: 
0071: 
0072: def summary(values):
0073:     values = list(values)
0074:     return {'count': len(values), 'mean': sum(values)/len(values) if values else None,
0075:             'min': min(values) if values else None, 'max': max(values) if values else None}

0078: def frontal_pose(value):
0079:     if (not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != set(POSE_FIELDS)
0080:             or any(not isinstance(v, Real) or isinstance(v, bool) or not math.isfinite(v)
0081:                    for v in value.values())):
0082:         raise ValueError('Camera-derived quality metadata required')
0083:     if (abs(value['yaw']) > .22 or abs(value['roll']) > 15.
0084:             or not 72 <= value['pixels'] <= 8192 or not 18 <= value['eye_distance'] <= 8192
0085:             or value['blur'] < 40):
0086:         raise ValueError('Qualified frontal observation required')
0087:     return tuple(float(value[k]) for k in POSE_FIELDS)

0118:     def __init__(self, clock, max_source_age_ms, *, token_factory=None):
0119:         if not callable(clock) or type(max_source_age_ms) is not int or not 100 <= max_source_age_ms <= 1500:
0120:             raise ValueError('Explicit clock and bounded source lifetime required')
0121:         self.clock, self.ttl = clock, max_source_age_ms
0122:         self.lock = threading.RLock()
0123:         self.analysis_lock = threading.Lock()
0124:         self.token_factory = token_factory or (lambda: secrets.token_hex(32))
0125:         self.sessions = []
0126:         self.active = None
0127:         self.generation = self.serial = self.last_consumed = 0
0128:         self.tokens, self.request_ids = set(), set()

0130:         self.accepted = self.rejected = self.observer_errors = 0
0131:         self.quality_rejected = 0

0134:     def _new_token(self):
0135:         for _ in range(8):
0136:             value = self.token_factory()
0137:             if isinstance(value, str) and HEX64.fullmatch(value) and value not in self.tokens:
0138:                 self.tokens.add(value)
0139:                 return value
0140:         raise ValueError('Unable to create a unique session token')

0142:     def _end(self, reason):
0143:         if self.active is not None:
0144:             self.active['end_reason'] = reason
0145:             self.active = None
0146:             self.generation += 1

0148:     def _expiry(self):
0149:         if self.active and not 0 <= self.clock()-self.active['started'] < self.MAX_SESSION_MS:
0150:             self._end('SESSION_EXPIRED')
0151: 
0152:     def observe_target(self, current):
0153:         with self.lock:
0154:             self._expiry()
0155:             if self.active:
0156:                 try: same = target_tuple(current) == self.active['target']
0157:                 except ValueError: same = False
0158:                 if not same:
0159:                     self._end('TARGET_CHANGED')
