0161:     def begin(self, purpose, person, capture_session, target, current_target):
0162:         with self.lock:
0163:             self._expiry()
0164:             if self.closed or self.active is not None:
0165:                 raise ValueError('Calibration is closed or already collecting')
0166:             if (purpose not in PURPOSES or not isinstance(person, str) or not PERSON.fullmatch(person)
0167:                     or not isinstance(capture_session, str) or not CAPTURE.fullmatch(capture_session)):
0168:                 raise ValueError('Strict anonymous person and independent capture session required')
0169:             key = target_tuple(target)
0170:             if key != target_tuple(current_target):
0171:                 raise ValueError('Target changed before session start')
0172:             if len(self.sessions) >= self.MAX_SESSIONS or any(s['capture'] == capture_session for s in self.sessions):
0173:                 raise ValueError('Capture session reused or capacity exhausted')
0174:             if len({s['person'] for s in self.sessions} | {person}) > self.MAX_PEOPLE:
0175:                 raise ValueError('Anonymous person capacity exhausted')
0176:             if bool(self.frozen) != (purpose in VALIDATION):
0177:                 raise ValueError('Calibration only before freeze; validation only after freeze')
0178:             known = {s['person'] for s in self.sessions if s['purpose'] == 'calibration_gallery'}
0179:             unknown = {s['person'] for s in self.sessions if s['purpose'] in ('calibration_unknown', 'validation_unknown')}
0180:             if purpose == 'calibration_gallery' and person in unknown:
0181:                 raise ValueError('Unknown-labelled person cannot become gallery in this experiment')
0182:             if purpose == 'calibration_gallery' and any(s['person'] == person
0183:                     and s['purpose'] == purpose and s['end_reason'] == 'OPERATOR_ENDED'
0184:                     and len(s['samples']) >= self.MIN_SAMPLES for s in self.sessions):
0185:                 raise ValueError('One completed gallery capture per person; reset to recollect')
0186:             if purpose in ('calibration_probe', 'validation_known') and person not in known:
0187:                 raise ValueError('Known probe requires an explicit prior gallery group')
0188:             if purpose in ('calibration_unknown', 'validation_unknown') and person in known:
0189:                 raise ValueError('Unknown probe cannot use a gallery person')
0190:             token = self._new_token()
0191:             self.generation += 1
0192:             item = {'purpose': purpose, 'person': person, 'capture': capture_session,
0193:                     'target': key, 'started': self.clock(), 'samples': [], 'last_fid': 0,
0194:                     'last_stamp': None, 'end_reason': None, 'token': token}
0195:             self.sessions.append(item)
0196:             self.active = item
0197:             return {'mode': 'CALIBRATION_ONLY', 'session_token': token,
0198:                     'capture_session': capture_session, 'purpose': purpose, 'samples': 0}

0200:     def end(self, token, *, discard=False):
0201:         with self.lock:
0202:             self._expiry()
0203:             if (not self.active or not isinstance(token, str)
0204:                     or not secrets.compare_digest(token, self.active['token'])):
0205:                 raise ValueError('Active session token required; replay refused')
0206:             count = len(self.active['samples'])
0207:             if discard:
0208:                 self.active['samples'].clear()
0209:             self._end('DISCARDED' if discard else 'OPERATOR_ENDED')
0210:             return {'ended': True, 'samples_retained': 0 if discard else count}

0212:     def ticket(self, fid, stamp, target, current_target=None, *, quality=None):
0213:         # Called by the primary extractor: a control request must never make
0214:         # that producer wait for calibration state. Busy simply drops capture.
0215:         if not self.lock.acquire(blocking=False):
0216:             return None
0217:         try:
0218:             self._expiry()
0219:             if current_target is not None:
0220:                 self.observe_target(current_target)
0221:             item = self.active
0222:             if (not item or self.closed or type(fid) is not int or fid < 1 or type(stamp) is not int
0223:                     or not 0 <= self.clock()-stamp < self.ttl):
0224:                 return None
0225:             try: key = target_tuple(target)
0226:             except ValueError: return None
0227:             if key != item['target'] or len(item['samples']) >= self.MAX_SAMPLES:
0228:                 return None
0229:             try: pose = frontal_pose(quality)
0230:             except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
0231:                 self.quality_rejected += 1
0232:                 return None
0233:             self.serial += 1
0234:             return Ticket(item['token'], self.generation, self.serial, key, fid, stamp, pose)
0235:         finally:
0236:             self.lock.release()

0238:     def accept(self, ticket, feature, current_target):
0239:         with self.lock:
0240:             self.observe_target(current_target)
0241:             item = self.active
0242:             if (not isinstance(ticket, Ticket) or not item or self.closed
0243:                     or not secrets.compare_digest(ticket.token, item['token'])
0244:                     or ticket.generation != self.generation or ticket.serial <= self.last_consumed
0245:                     or ticket.serial > self.serial or ticket.target != item['target']
0246:                     or not 0 <= self.clock()-ticket.stamp < self.ttl):
0247:                 self.rejected += 1
0248:                 return False
0249:             self.last_consumed = ticket.serial
0250:             if (ticket.fid <= item['last_fid'] or len(item['samples']) >= self.MAX_SAMPLES
0251:                     or item['last_stamp'] is not None and ticket.stamp-item['last_stamp'] < self.MIN_INTERVAL_MS):
0252:                 self.rejected += 1
0253:                 return False
0254:             try: vector = normalized(feature)
0255:             except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
0256:                 self.rejected += 1
0257:                 return False
0258:             item['samples'].append(Sample(vector,ticket.pose,min(ticket.pose[4],400.)+min(ticket.pose[2],240.)))
0259:             item['last_stamp'], item['last_fid'] = ticket.stamp, ticket.fid
0260:             self.accepted += 1
0261:             return True

0263:     def _snapshot(self):
0264:         with self.lock:
0265:             self._expiry()
0266:             # Only RAM copy; never return vectors to caller or HTTP route.
0267:             data = [{'purpose': s['purpose'], 'person': s['person'], 'capture': s['capture'],
0268:                      'samples': tuple(s['samples']), 'ended': s['end_reason'] == 'OPERATOR_ENDED'}
0269:                     for s in self.sessions]
0270:             return data, copy.deepcopy(self.frozen), self.active is not None
0271: 
0272:     @staticmethod
0273:     def _gallery(data):
0274:         groups = {}
0275:         for item in data:
0276:             if (item['purpose'] == 'calibration_gallery' and item['ended']
0277:                     and len(item['samples']) >= CalibrationSession.MIN_SAMPLES):
0278:                 groups.setdefault(item['person'], []).extend(item['samples'])
0279:         result = {}
0280:         for person, rows in groups.items():
0281:             # Same selection core as EnrollmentSampling._choose_front: top five
0282:             # most frontal observations, then observed medoid/quality tie-break.
0283:             front = sorted(rows,key=lambda row:(abs(row.pose[0]),abs(row.pose[1]),-row.quality))[:5]
0284:             if len(front) < CalibrationSession.MIN_SAMPLES: continue
0285:             def representative(row):
0286:                 return (sum(sum(a*b for a,b in zip(row.feature,other.feature)) for other in front),row.quality)
0287:             result[person] = max(front,key=representative).feature
0288:         return result

0290:     @classmethod
0291:     def _coverage(cls, data, validation=False):
0292:         galleries = {s['person'] for s in data if s['purpose'] == 'calibration_gallery'
0293:                      and s['ended'] and len(s['samples']) >= cls.MIN_SAMPLES}
0294:         known_purpose = 'validation_known' if validation else 'calibration_probe'
0295:         unknown_purpose = 'validation_unknown' if validation else 'calibration_unknown'
0296:         known = {s['person'] for s in data if s['purpose'] == known_purpose
0297:                  and s['ended'] and len(s['samples']) >= cls.MIN_SAMPLES}
0298:         unknown = {s['person'] for s in data if s['purpose'] == unknown_purpose
0299:                    and s['ended'] and len(s['samples']) >= cls.MIN_SAMPLES}
0300:         missing = []
0301:         if len(galleries) < 2: missing.append('AT_LEAST_TWO_GALLERY_PEOPLE')
0302:         if not galleries <= known: missing.append('INDEPENDENT_KNOWN_PROBE_PER_GALLERY_PERSON')
0303:         if not unknown: missing.append('INDEPENDENT_UNKNOWN_PERSON')
0304:         return {'sufficient': not missing, 'gallery_people': len(galleries),
0305:                 'known_probe_people': len(known & galleries), 'unknown_probe_people': len(unknown),
0306:                 'missing': missing, 'minimum_samples_per_capture': cls.MIN_SAMPLES,
0307:                 'general_accuracy_proven': False, 'labels_operator_asserted': True}
0308: 
0309:     @classmethod
0310:     def _probes(cls, data, validation=False):
0311:         gallery = cls._gallery(data)
0312:         purposes = VALIDATION if validation else {'calibration_probe', 'calibration_unknown'}
0313:         rows = []
0314:         for item in data:
0315:             if item['purpose'] not in purposes or not item['ended'] or len(item['samples']) < cls.MIN_SAMPLES:
0316:                 continue
0317:             for sample in item['samples']:
0318:                 feature = sample.feature
0319:                 # Same normalized, one-template-per-person semantics as store.match.
0320:                 scores = sorted(((sum(a*b for a,b in zip(feature, template)), person)
0321:                                  for person, template in gallery.items()), reverse=True)
0322:                 if len(scores) >= 2:
0323:                     rows.append((item['purpose'].endswith('unknown'), item['person'],
0324:                                  scores[0][1], scores[0][0], scores[0][0]-scores[1][0]))
0325:         return rows
0326: 
0327:     @staticmethod
0328:     def _metrics(rows, threshold, margin):
0329:         value = {key: 0 for key in ('known_samples', 'unknown_samples', 'correct_accept',
0330:                     'false_reject', 'known_ambiguous', 'misidentification', 'unknown_reject',
0331:                     'unknown_ambiguous', 'false_accept')}
0332:         for unknown, expected, predicted, best, gap in rows:
0333:             value['unknown_samples' if unknown else 'known_samples'] += 1
0334:             if best < threshold: result = 'unknown_reject' if unknown else 'false_reject'
0335:             elif gap < margin: result = 'unknown_ambiguous' if unknown else 'known_ambiguous'
0336:             elif unknown: result = 'false_accept'
0337:             else: result = 'correct_accept' if expected == predicted else 'misidentification'
0338:             value[result] += 1
0339:         value['false_accept_fraction'] = value['false_accept']/value['unknown_samples'] if value['unknown_samples'] else None
0340:         value['correct_accept_fraction'] = value['correct_accept']/value['known_samples'] if value['known_samples'] else None
0341:         return value
0342: 
0343:     def report(self, *, validation=False):
0344:         data, frozen, active = self._snapshot()
0345:         coverage = self._coverage(data, validation)
0346:         rows = self._probes(data, validation)
0347:         pairs_same, pairs_different = [], []
0348:         if not validation:
0349:             # Cross-capture only; a continuous capture never compares with itself.
0350:             eligible = [s for s in data if s['purpose'] in CALIBRATION and s['ended']
0351:                         and len(s['samples']) >= self.MIN_SAMPLES]
0352:             for i, a in enumerate(eligible):
0353:                 for b in eligible[i+1:]:
0354:                     if a['capture'] == b['capture']: continue
0355:                     target = pairs_same if a['person'] == b['person'] else pairs_different
0356:                     for va in a['samples']:
0357:                         for vb in b['samples']:
0358:                             target.append(sum(x*y for x,y in zip(va.feature,vb.feature)))
0359:         result = {'mode': 'CALIBRATION_ONLY', 'model_id': MODEL_ID,
0360:                   'gallery_policy': GALLERY_POLICY, 'quality_source': QUALITY_SOURCE,
0361:                   'deployment_threshold_validated': False,
0362:                   'split': 'VALIDATION' if validation else 'CALIBRATION',
0363:                   'coverage': coverage, 'active_session': active,
0364:                   'independent_capture_sessions': len(data),
0365:                   'candidate': frozen, 'identity_decisions_published': 0,
0366:                   'templates_written': 0, 'images_saved': 0, 'features_saved': 0}
0367:         if not validation:
0368:             result['cross_capture_scores'] = {'same_person': summary(pairs_same), 'different_person': summary(pairs_different)}
0369:         else:
0370:             calibration_people = {s['person'] for s in data if s['purpose'] in CALIBRATION}
0371:             unknown_people = {s['person'] for s in data if s['purpose'] == 'validation_unknown' and s['ended']}
0372:             result['validation_unknown_people_seen_in_calibration'] = len(unknown_people & calibration_people)
0373:             result['independent_subjects_required_or_proven'] = False
0374:         if frozen:
0375:             result['metrics'] = self._metrics(rows, frozen['threshold'], frozen['ambiguity_margin'])
0376:         result['status'] = ('COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT' if not coverage['sufficient'] else
0377:                             'OPERATOR_LABELLED_SMALL_SAMPLE_ONLY')
0378:         return result
0379: 
0380:     def suggest(self):
0381:         data, frozen, active = self._snapshot()
0382:         coverage = self._coverage(data)
0383:         if frozen or active or not coverage['sufficient']:
0384:             return {'status': 'COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT' if not coverage['sufficient'] else 'NOT_SELECTABLE',
0385:                     'coverage': coverage, 'candidate': None}
0386:         rows = self._probes(data)
0387:         # Deterministic finite search on CALIBRATION only. Bound to 21x21 grid;
0388:         # explicit operator freeze remains mandatory, no config is written.
0389:         thresholds = [i/20. for i in range(21)]
0390:         margins = [i/40. for i in range(21)]
0391:         candidates = []
0392:         for threshold in thresholds:
0393:             for margin in margins:
0394:                 metrics = self._metrics(rows, threshold, margin)
0395:                 if not metrics['false_accept'] and not metrics['misidentification']:
0396:                     rank = (metrics['correct_accept'], metrics['unknown_reject'],
0397:                             -metrics['known_ambiguous'], threshold, margin)
0398:                     candidates.append((rank, threshold, margin, metrics))
0399:         if not candidates:
0400:             return {'status': 'NO_ZERO_OBSERVED_ERROR_CANDIDATE', 'coverage': coverage, 'candidate': None}
0401:         _, threshold, margin, metrics = max(candidates)
0402:         if not metrics['correct_accept']:
0403:             return {'status': 'NO_USEFUL_ACCEPTANCE_CANDIDATE', 'coverage': coverage, 'candidate': None}
0404:         return {'status': 'SUGGESTION_REQUIRES_OPERATOR_FREEZE', 'coverage': coverage,
0405:                 'candidate': {'threshold': threshold, 'ambiguity_margin': margin},
0406:                 'calibration_metrics': metrics, 'validation_used': False,
0407:                 'general_accuracy_proven': False}
0408: 
0409:     def freeze(self, threshold, margin):
0410:         if (type(threshold) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(threshold) or not 0 <= threshold <= 1
0411:                 or type(margin) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(margin) or not 0 <= margin <= 1):
0412:             raise ValueError('Explicit finite candidate threshold/margin required')
0413:         with self.lock:
0414:             data, old, active = self._snapshot()
0415:             if old or active or not self._coverage(data)['sufficient']:
0416:                 raise ValueError('Complete independent calibration coverage before freeze')
0417:             if len(self._gallery(data)) < 2:
0418:                 raise ValueError('Qualified observed gallery templates required')
0419:             self.frozen = {'model_id': MODEL_ID, 'threshold': float(threshold),
0420:                            'ambiguity_margin': float(margin), 'frozen': True,
0421:                            'validation_used_for_selection': False,
0422:                            'field_threshold_validated': False,
0423:                            'deployment_threshold_validated': False,
0424:                            'gallery_policy': GALLERY_POLICY,
0425:                            'general_accuracy_proven': False,
0426:                            'scope': 'OPERATOR_LABELLED_FIELD_CANDIDATE_ONLY'}
0427:             return copy.deepcopy(self.frozen)
0428: 
0429:     def health(self):
0430:         with self.lock:
0431:             self._expiry()
0432:             return {'enabled': True, 'mode': 'CALIBRATION_ONLY', 'model_id': MODEL_ID,
0433:                     'gallery_policy': GALLERY_POLICY, 'deployment_threshold_validated': False,
0434:                     'closed': self.closed, 'active_session': self.active is not None,
0435:                     'sessions': len(self.sessions), 'samples_in_ram': sum(len(s['samples']) for s in self.sessions),
0436:                     'max_sessions': self.MAX_SESSIONS, 'max_samples_per_session': self.MAX_SAMPLES,
0437:                     'max_embedding_scalar_values': self.MAX_SESSIONS*self.MAX_SAMPLES*128,
0438:                     'accepted': self.accepted, 'rejected': self.rejected,
0439:                     'quality_rejected': self.quality_rejected,
0440:                     'candidate_frozen': self.frozen is not None,
0441:                     'last_session_end_reason': self.sessions[-1]['end_reason'] if self.sessions else None,
0442:                     'mutation_slots_remaining': self.MAX_MUTATIONS-len(self.request_ids),
0443:                     'identity_decisions_published': 0, 'templates_written': 0,
0444:                     'images_saved': 0, 'features_saved': 0}
0445: 
0446:     def dispatch(self, action, params, *, current_target=None):
0447:         """Narrow scalar-only control surface; no embedding input/output endpoint."""
0448:         if not isinstance(params, dict): raise ValueError('Object params required')
0449:         read = {'health', 'report', 'suggest', 'validation_report'}
0450:         if action in read:
0451:             if params: raise ValueError('Read controls accept no params')
0452:             if action == 'health': return self.health()
0453:             if not self.analysis_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
0454:                 raise ValueError('Analytical report already running')
0455:             try:
0456:                 if action == 'suggest': return self.suggest()
0457:                 return self.report(validation=action == 'validation_report')
0458:             finally:
0459:                 self.analysis_lock.release()
0460:         with self.lock:
0461:             if self.closed: raise ValueError('Calibration closed')
0462:             request = params.get('request_id')
0463:             if (not isinstance(request, str) or not HEX32.fullmatch(request)
0464:                     or request in self.request_ids or len(self.request_ids) >= self.MAX_MUTATIONS):
0465:                 raise ValueError('Unique request_id required; replay/capacity refused')
0466:             self.request_ids.add(request)  # Failed operations are consumed too.
0467:             fields = set(params) - {'request_id'}
0468:             if action == 'start' and fields == {'purpose', 'person', 'capture_session', 'target'}:
0469:                 return self.begin(params['purpose'], params['person'], params['capture_session'],
0470:                                   params['target'], current_target)
0471:             if action in ('end', 'discard') and fields == {'session_token'}:
0472:                 return self.end(params['session_token'], discard=action == 'discard')
0473:             if action == 'freeze' and fields == {'threshold', 'ambiguity_margin'}:
0474:                 return self.freeze(params['threshold'], params['ambiguity_margin'])
0475:             if action == 'reset' and not fields:
0476:                 self._end('RESET')
0477:                 self.sessions.clear()
0478:                 self.frozen = None
0479:                 # Retain used-token and request ledgers: reset cannot enable replay.
0480:                 return {'reset': True, 'samples_in_ram': 0, 'candidate_frozen': False}
0481:             raise ValueError('Unknown action or invalid exact params')
0482: 
0483:     def close(self):
0484:         with self.lock:
0485:             self._end('CLOSED')
0486:             self.sessions.clear()
0487:             self.frozen = None
0488:             self.closed = True

0001: """Opt-in RAM-only crop preparation independent of synchronous Windows I/O.
0002: 
0003: The camera capture contract supplies an owned frame that consumers never mutate.
0004: Only its reference is queued; one preparing frame and one latest pending frame.
0005: No device, runner, identity storage, PNG encoding or network ownership here.
0006: """
0007: import math
0008: import threading
0009: import time
0010: 
0011: from board.interaction.ui_contract import target_key
0012: 
0013: 
0014: def producer_configuration(config):
0015:     value = config.get('dpu_sface_producer')
0016:     if value is None:
0017:         return None
0018:     if not isinstance(value, dict) or type(value.get('enabled')) is not bool:
0019:         raise ValueError('Invalid SFace producer configuration')
0020:     if not value['enabled']:
0021:         if set(value) != {'enabled'}:
0022:             raise ValueError('Disabled producer must contain only enabled=false')
0023:         return None
0024:     from board.identity.sface_shadow import shadow_configuration
0025:     shadow = shadow_configuration(config)
0026:     if (set(value) != {'enabled', 'interval_ms'} or type(value['interval_ms']) is not int
0027:             or not 500 <= value['interval_ms'] <= 5000 or shadow is None
0028:             or shadow['compare_cosine']):
0029:         raise ValueError('Independent producer requires compute-only shadow and bounded preparation interval')
0030:     return dict(value)
0031: 
0032: 
0033: class SFaceProducer:
0034:     def __init__(self, config, shadow, current_target, *, clock, guard_lock, align_crop, cv2):
0035:         self.config = producer_configuration(config)
0036:         if self.config is None or shadow is None or not callable(current_target) or guard_lock is None:
0037:             raise ValueError('Producer requires explicit configuration and shared shadow/target guard')
0038:         self.limits, self.shadow, self.current_target = config, shadow, current_target
0039:         self.clock, self.guard_lock, self.align_crop, self.cv2 = clock, guard_lock, align_crop, cv2
0040:         self.condition = threading.Condition()
0041:         self.pending = None
0042:         self.thread = None
0043:         self.stopped = threading.Event()
0044:         self.closed = False
0045:         self.inflight = 0
0046:         self.offered = self.enqueued = self.replaced = self.dropped = 0
0047:         self.prepared = self.submitted = self.stale = self.failures = self.unready = 0
0048:         self.last_error = ''
0049:         self.generation = 0
0050:         self.last_target = None
0051: 
0052:     def start(self):
0053:         with self.condition:
0054:             if self.thread is not None or self.closed:
0055:                 raise RuntimeError('Producer cannot start twice')
0056:             self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.work, name='sface-crop-prepare', daemon=True)
0057:             self.thread.start()
0058: 
0059:     def offer_face(self, image, face, fid, stamp, key):
0060:         """One caller: FaceTargetService's explicit fresh single-target callback.
0061: 
0062:         No array copy, alignment, quality calculation or waiting on this path.
0063:         None face/key explicitly cancels a previously queued target.
0064:         """
0065:         self.offered += 1
0066:         try:
0067:             import numpy as np
0068:             key = target_key(key) if key is not None else None
0069:             if key != self.last_target or face is None:
0070:                 self.generation += 1
0071:                 self.last_target = key if face is not None else None
0072:             generation = self.generation
0073:             if not self.condition.acquire(blocking=False):
0074:                 self.dropped += 1
0075:                 return False
0076:             try:
0077:                 if (self.closed or self.stopped.is_set() or key is None or face is None
0078:                         or type(fid) is not int or fid < 1 or type(stamp) is not int
0079:                         or not 0 <= self.clock()-stamp < self.shadow.config['max_source_age_ms']
0080:                         or not isinstance(image, np.ndarray) or image.dtype != np.uint8
0081:                         or image.shape != (480, 640, 3) or len(face) != 15
0082:                         or not all(math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in face)
0083:                         or min(face[2:4]) < self.limits['minimum_face_pixels']
0084:                         or face[14] < self.limits['minimum_face_score']):
0085:                     self.pending = None
0086:                     self.dropped += 1
0087:                     return False
0088:                 if not self.shadow.ready or self.shadow.closed or self.shadow.stopped.is_set():
0089:                     self.unready += 1
0090:                     return False
0091:                 self.replaced += int(self.pending is not None)
0092:                 self.pending = (image, tuple(face), fid, stamp, key, generation)
0093:                 self.enqueued += 1
0094:                 self.condition.notify()
0095:                 return True
0096:             finally:
0097:                 self.condition.release()
0098:         except Exception as exc:
0099:             self.failures += 1
0100:             self.last_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]
0101:             return False
0102: 
0103:     def _valid(self, packet):
0104:         return (not self.closed and packet[5] == self.generation
0105:                 and packet[4] == self.current_target()
0106:                 and 0 <= self.clock()-packet[3] < self.shadow.config['max_source_age_ms'])
0107: 
0108:     def _prepare(self, packet):
0109:         with self.guard_lock:
0110:             if not self._valid(packet):
0111:                 self.stale += 1
0112:                 return
0113:         image, face, fid, stamp, key, _ = packet
0114:         crop = self.align_crop(image, face)
0115:         variance = float(self.cv2.Laplacian(self.cv2.cvtColor(crop, self.cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY),
0116:                                           self.cv2.CV_64F).var())
0117:         if not math.isfinite(variance) or variance < self.limits['minimum_blur_variance']:
0118:             self.dropped += 1
0119:             return
0120:         from board.identity.sampling import pose_quality
0121:         quality = pose_quality(face, variance)
0122:         self.prepared += 1
0123:         # Do not copy crop / acquire calibration ticket while holding state.lock.
0124:         # Shadow's own before/after execution checks remain authoritative.
0125:         with self.guard_lock:
0126:             if not self._valid(packet):
0127:                 self.stale += 1
0128:                 return
0129:         if self.shadow.offer(crop, fid, stamp, key, None, quality=quality):
0130:             self.submitted += 1
0131: 
0132:     def work(self):
0133:         try:
0134:             next_at = 0.
0135:             while True:
0136:                 with self.condition:
0137:                     self.condition.wait_for(lambda: self.closed or self.pending is not None)
0138:                     if self.closed:
0139:                         return
0140:                     delay = next_at-time.monotonic()
0141:                     if delay > 0:
0142:                         self.condition.wait(delay)
0143:                         continue
0144:                     packet, self.pending = self.pending, None
0145:                     self.inflight = 1
0146:                 try:
0147:                     self._prepare(packet)
0148:                 except Exception as exc:
0149:                     self.failures += 1
0150:                     self.last_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]
0151:                 finally:
0152:                     packet = None
0153:                     with self.condition:
0154:                         self.inflight = 0
0155:                 next_at = time.monotonic()+self.config['interval_ms']/1000.
0156:         finally:
0157:             with self.condition:
0158:                 self.pending = None
0159:                 self.inflight = 0
0160:             self.stopped.set()
0161: 
0162:     def close(self, timeout=2):
0163:         with self.condition:
0164:             self.closed = True
0165:             self.pending = None
0166:             self.condition.notify_all()
0167:         if self.thread is None:
0168:             self.stopped.set()
0169:         else:
0170:             self.thread.join(timeout)
0171:         return self.stopped.is_set()
0172: 
0173:     def health(self):
0174:         with self.condition:
0175:             return {'enabled': True, 'mode': 'INDEPENDENT_CROP_PREPARATION',
0176:                     'ready': not self.closed and self.shadow.ready and not self.shadow.closed,
0177:                     'closed': self.stopped.is_set(), 'stop_requested': self.closed,
0178:                     'pending_frames': int(self.pending is not None), 'inflight_frames': self.inflight,
0179:                     'interval_ms': self.config['interval_ms'],
0180:                     'offered': self.offered, 'enqueued': self.enqueued, 'replaced': self.replaced,
0181:                     'dropped': self.dropped, 'unready': self.unready, 'prepared': self.prepared,
0182:                     'submitted': self.submitted, 'stale': self.stale, 'failures': self.failures,
0183:                     'last_error': self.last_error}

0001: """Bounded optional DPU shadow; Windows alone supplies identity decisions.
0002: 
0003: One immutable crop/reference in flight and one latest pending item, RAM only.
0004: No database, identity result, enrollment, camera, service, or device ownership.
0005: """
0006: from contextlib import contextmanager
0007: import math
0008: from pathlib import Path
0009: import threading
0010: import time
0011: 
0012: from board.identity.deferred import DeferredIdentity

0014: from board.interaction.ui_contract import millis, target_key
0015: 
0016: 
0017: def shadow_configuration(config):
0018:     """Pure validation, default off; never opens artifacts, devices or stores."""
0019:     value = config.get('dpu_sface_shadow')
0020:     if value is None:
0021:         return None
0022:     if not isinstance(value, dict) or type(value.get('enabled')) is not bool:
0023:         raise ValueError('Invalid DPU SFace shadow configuration')
0024:     if not value['enabled']:
0025:         if set(value) != {'enabled'}:
0026:             raise ValueError('Disabled shadow configuration must contain only enabled=false')
0027:         return None
0028:     required = {'enabled','model','manifest','model_sha256','manifest_sha256',
0029:                 'max_source_age_ms','interval_ms','compare_cosine'}
0030:     primary = config.get('feature_backend') or {}
0031:     if (set(value) not in (required,required|{'slot_retry'}) or primary.get('kind') != 'windows_sface'
0032:             or config.get('model_id') != WINDOWS_MODEL_ID):
0033:         raise ValueError('SFace shadow requires the original Windows FP32 identity backend')
0034:     if (value['model_sha256'] != MODEL_SHA256 or type(value['interval_ms']) is not int
0035:             or not 500 <= value['interval_ms'] <= 5000 or type(value['max_source_age_ms']) is not int
0036:             or not 100 <= value['max_source_age_ms'] <= min(1500,config['result_ttl_ms'])
0037:             or type(value['compare_cosine']) is not bool):
0038:         raise ValueError('Invalid SFace shadow model, source lifetime or rate')
0039:     for key in ('model','manifest'):
0040:         if not isinstance(value[key], str) or not Path(value[key]).is_absolute():
0041:             raise ValueError('Shadow model/manifest require explicit absolute paths')
0042:     digest = value['manifest_sha256']
0043:     if not isinstance(digest,str) or len(digest) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in digest):
0044:         raise ValueError('Shadow requires an explicit compiler manifest SHA256')
0045:     retry=value.get('slot_retry')
0046:     if 'slot_retry' in value:
0047:         if (not isinstance(retry,dict) or set(retry)!={'backoff_ms','max_retries'}
0048:                 or type(retry['backoff_ms']) is not int or not 25<=retry['backoff_ms']<=100
0049:                 or type(retry['max_retries']) is not int or not 1<=retry['max_retries']<=4
0050:                 or retry['backoff_ms']*retry['max_retries']>200 or value['compare_cosine']):
0051:             raise ValueError('Slot retry requires compute-only shadow and at most 200ms bounded backoff')
0052:     return dict(value)

0116:     def offer(self, crop, fid, stamp, key, reference, *, quality=None):
0117:         """Never waits for shadow state or inference; primary keeps its vector."""
0118:         self.offered += 1  # The extractor is the sole producer.
0119:         try:
0120:             import numpy as np
0121:             if (self.closed or self.stopped.is_set() or type(fid) is not int or fid < 1
0122:                     or type(stamp) is not int or not 0 <= self.clock()-stamp < self.config['max_source_age_ms']
0123:                     or not isinstance(crop,np.ndarray) or crop.dtype != np.uint8 or crop.shape != (112,112,3)):
0124:                 self.producer_dropped += 1
0125:                 return False
0126:             target = target_key(key)
0127:             original = None
0128:             if self.config['compare_cosine']:
0129:                 values = tuple(float(v) for v in reference)
0130:                 norm = math.sqrt(sum(v*v for v in values))
0131:                 if len(values) != 128 or not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in values) or norm <= 1e-12:
0132:                     self.producer_dropped += 1
0133:                     return False
0134:                 original = tuple(v/norm for v in values)
0135:             # One bounded 37,632-byte private copy; producer/camera buffers can
0136:             # be released or reused immediately after this nonblocking offer.
0137:             image = np.array(crop, dtype=np.uint8, order='C', copy=True)
0138:             image.flags.writeable = False
0139:             packet = {'crop':image,'fid':fid,'stamp':stamp,'target':target,'reference':original}
0140:             if self.observer is not None:
0141:                 try:packet['calibration_ticket'] = self.observer.ticket(fid,stamp,target,quality=quality)
0142:                 except Exception:
0143:                     self.observer_errors += 1
0144:                     packet['calibration_ticket'] = None
0145:             if not self.condition.acquire(blocking=False):
0146:                 self.producer_dropped += 1
0147:                 return False
0148:             try:
0149:                 if self.closed or self.stopped.is_set():
0150:                     self.producer_dropped += 1
0151:                     return False
0152:                 self.replaced += int(self.pending is not None)
0153:                 self.pending = packet
0154:                 self.enqueued += 1
0155:                 self.condition.notify()
0156:                 return True
0157:             finally:
0158:                 self.condition.release()
0159:         except Exception:
0160:             # Shadow input/copy/validation failure cannot escape into primary.
0161:             self.producer_dropped += 1
0162:             return False
0163: 
0164:     @staticmethod
0165:     def _cosine(feature, reference):
0166:         values = tuple(float(v) for v in feature)
0167:         norm = math.sqrt(sum(v*v for v in values))
0168:         if len(values) != 128 or not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in values) or norm <= 1e-12:
0169:             raise ValueError('Invalid shadow embedding')
0170:         return max(-1., min(1., sum(a*b for a,b in zip(values,reference))/norm))
0171: 
0172:     def _process(self, packet):
0173:         with self._guard():
0174:             valid = self._valid(packet)
0175:         if not valid:
0176:             with self.condition:self.stale += 1
0177:             return
0178:         started = time.monotonic()
0179:         try:
0180:             feature = self.embedder.feature(packet['crop'], source_mono_ms=packet['stamp'])
0181:             cosine = self._cosine(feature,packet['reference']) if self.config['compare_cosine'] else None
0182:             elapsed = (time.monotonic()-started)*1000
0183:             # Publication lock order matches the parent health path: camera
0184:             # state lock then shadow condition. Never hold either during DPU.
0185:             with self._guard():
0186:                 valid = self._valid(packet)
0187:                 with self.condition:
0188:                     self.processed += 1
0189:                     if not valid:
0190:                         self.stale += 1
0191:                         return
0192:                     self.accepted += 1
0193:                     self.latency_sum += elapsed
0194:                     self.latency_min = elapsed if self.accepted == 1 else min(self.latency_min,elapsed)
0195:                     self.latency_max = max(self.latency_max,elapsed)
0196:                     if cosine is not None:
0197:                         self.cosine_count += 1
0198:                         self.cosine_sum += cosine
0199:                         self.cosine_min = cosine if self.cosine_count == 1 else min(self.cosine_min,cosine)
0200:                         self.cosine_max = cosine if self.cosine_count == 1 else max(self.cosine_max,cosine)
0201:                     if self.observer is not None and packet.get('calibration_ticket') is not None:
0202:                         try:self.observer.accept(packet['calibration_ticket'],feature,self.current_target())
0203:                         except Exception:self.observer_errors += 1
0204:         except DeferredIdentity as exc:
0205:             with self.condition:self.deferred += 1
0206:             return getattr(exc,'retryable',False) is True
0207:         except Exception as exc:
0208:             with self.condition:
0209:                 self.failures += 1
0210:                 self.last_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]
0211: 
0212:     def work(self):
0213:         try:
0214:             embedder = self.factory()
0215:             with self.condition:
0216:                 self.embedder, self.ready = embedder, True
0217:             next_at = 0.;retries=0
0218:             while True:
0219:                 with self.condition:
0220:                     self.condition.wait_for(lambda:self.closed or self.pending is not None)
0221:                     if self.closed:return
0222:                     delay = next_at-time.monotonic()
0223:                     if delay > 0:
0224:                         self.condition.wait(delay)
0225:                         continue
0226:                     packet, self.pending = self.pending, None
0227:                     self.inflight = 1
0228:                 retryable=self._process(packet)
0229:                 retry=self.config.get('slot_retry')
0230:                 with self.condition:
0231:                     self.inflight = 0
0232:                     if not self.closed and retryable and retry and retries<retry['max_retries']:
0233:                         retries+=1;self.retry_scheduled+=1
0234:                         if self.pending is None:self.pending=packet
0235:                         else:self.retry_superseded+=1  # Preserve the newer crop.
0236:                         next_at=time.monotonic()+retry['backoff_ms']/1000.
0237:                     else:
0238:                         if retryable and retry and retries>=retry['max_retries']:self.retry_exhausted+=1
0239:                         retries=0
0240:                         next_at=time.monotonic()+self.config['interval_ms']/1000.
0241:                 # Only the bounded latest pending slot may retain the crop.
0242:                 packet = None
0243:         except Exception as exc:
0244:             with self.condition:
0245:                 self.failures += 1
0246:                 self.last_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]
0247:         finally:
0248:             if self.embedder is not None:
0249:                 try:self.embedder.close()
0250:                 except Exception as exc:
0251:                     with self.condition:
0252:                         self.failures += 1
0253:                         self.last_error = type(exc).__name__[:64]
0254:             with self.condition:
0255:                 self.ready = False
0256:                 self.pending = None
0257:                 self.inflight = 0
0258:             if self.observer is not None:
0259:                 try:self.observer.close()
0260:                 except Exception:self.observer_errors += 1
0261:             self.stopped.set()
0262: 
0263:     def health(self):
0264:         with self.condition:
0265:             try:
0266:                 runtime = self.embedder.health() if self.embedder is not None and callable(getattr(self.embedder,'health',None)) else None
0267:             except Exception as exc:
0268:                 runtime = {'error':type(exc).__name__[:64]}
0269:             return {'enabled':True,'mode':'SHADOW_ONLY_WINDOWS_IDENTITY','model_id':MODEL_ID,
0270:                 'ready':self.ready,'closed':self.stopped.is_set(),'stop_requested':self.closed,'stopped':self.stopped.is_set(),
0271:                 'pending_frames':int(self.pending is not None),'inflight_frames':self.inflight,
0272:                 'offered':self.offered,'enqueued':self.enqueued,'replaced':self.replaced,
0273:                 'producer_dropped':self.producer_dropped,'processed':self.processed,'accepted':self.accepted,
0274:                 'stale':self.stale,'deferred':self.deferred,'failures':self.failures,'last_error':self.last_error,
0275:                 'accepted_latency_ms':{'count':self.accepted,'mean':self.latency_sum/self.accepted if self.accepted else None,
0276:                     'min':self.latency_min if self.accepted else None,'max':self.latency_max if self.accepted else None},
0277:                 'same_crop_cosine':{'count':self.cosine_count,'mean':self.cosine_sum/self.cosine_count if self.cosine_count else None,
0278:                     'min':self.cosine_min if self.cosine_count else None,'max':self.cosine_max if self.cosine_count else None},
0279:                 'execution':runtime,'identity_decisions_from_shadow':0,'templates_written':0,
0280:                     'calibration_observer_errors':self.observer_errors,
0281:                     'slot_retry':{'enabled':'slot_retry' in self.config,
0282:                         'backoff_ms':(self.config.get('slot_retry') or {}).get('backoff_ms'),
0283:                         'max_retries':(self.config.get('slot_retry') or {}).get('max_retries'),
0284:                         'scheduled':self.retry_scheduled,'exhausted':self.retry_exhausted,
0285:                         'superseded_by_newer_crop':self.retry_superseded}}
0286: 
0287:     def close(self, timeout=5):
0288:         with self.condition:
0289:             self.closed = True
0290:             self.pending = None
0291:             self.condition.notify_all()
0292:         if self.thread is None:
0293:             if self.observer is not None:self.observer.close()
0294:             self.stopped.set()
0295:             return True
0296:         self.thread.join(timeout)
0297:         # The worker owns its runner and closes it after the in-flight job.
0298:         # Do not close an executing runner or the shared owner from this thread.
0299:         return self.stopped.is_set()

FILE: release_pinned_source/aura/board/vision/reference_faces.py

0001: # Input-size extension: https://github.com/opencv/opencv/blob/4.6.0/modules/objdetect/src/face_detect.cpp
0002: """Pinned YuNet 2022mar / SFace 2021dec CPU backend for OpenCV 4.5.2.
0003: 
0004: Postprocessing follows OpenCV 4.6.0 objdetect (Apache-2.0), including its
0005: legacy asymmetric box variance. This is a software reference, not a DPU model.
0006: Instances own mutable DNN state and must be used by one inference worker.
0007: """
0008: from pathlib import Path
0009: import hashlib
0010: import cv2
0011: import numpy as np
0012: 
0013: YUNET_SHA256 = '50ef07f702a31741ca46a4c0d947773b64143b9362780237bf0d427d6c79bab7'
0014: SFACE_SHA256 = '0ba9fbfa01b5270c96627c4ef784da859931e02f04419c829e83484087c34e79'
0015: 
0016: def checked_net(path, expected):
0017:     path = Path(path)
0018:     h = hashlib.sha256()
0019:     with path.open('rb') as f:
0020:         for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
0021:             h.update(chunk)
0022:     if h.hexdigest() != expected:
0023:         raise ValueError('Model hash mismatch: ' + str(path))
0024:     net = cv2.dnn.readNetFromONNX(str(path))
0025:     net.setPreferableBackend(cv2.dnn.DNN_BACKEND_OPENCV)
0026:     net.setPreferableTarget(cv2.dnn.DNN_TARGET_CPU)
0027:     return net
0028: 
0029: def bgr_image(image):
0030:     if not isinstance(image, np.ndarray) or image.dtype != np.uint8 or image.ndim != 3 or image.shape[2] != 3 or not image.size:
0031:         raise ValueError('Expected a nonempty uint8 BGR image')
0032: 
0033: class YuNetCompat:
0034:     def __init__(self, model, score_threshold=0.9, nms_threshold=0.3, top_k=5000, input_size=320):
0035:         if not 0 <= score_threshold <= 1 or not 0 < nms_threshold <= 1 or top_k < 1:
0036:             raise ValueError('Invalid detector thresholds')
0037:         if type(input_size) is not int or input_size not in (128,192,256,320):
0038:             raise ValueError("Expected validated input size divisible by 64")
0039:         self.net = checked_net(model, YUNET_SHA256)
0040:         self.score_threshold, self.nms_threshold, self.top_k = score_threshold, nms_threshold, top_k
0041:         self.size = input_size
0042:         priors = []
0043:         # OpenCV 4.6.0 generatePriors; supported sizes have exact /8,/16,/32,/64 grids.
0044:         for step, sizes in [(8,[10,16,24]), (16,[32,48]), (32,[64,96]), (64,[128,192,256])]:
0045:             cells = self.size // step
0046:             for y in range(cells):
0047:                 for x in range(cells):
0048:                     for s in sizes:
0049:                         priors.append([(x + .5) * step / self.size, (y + .5) * step / self.size, s / self.size, s / self.size])
0050:         self.priors = np.asarray(priors, dtype=np.float32)
0051: 
0052:     def detect(self, image):
0053:         bgr_image(image)
0054:         h, w = image.shape[:2]
0055:         resized = cv2.resize(image, (self.size, self.size), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
0056:         self.net.setInput(cv2.dnn.blobFromImage(resized))
0057:         loc, conf, iou = self.net.forward(['loc', 'conf', 'iou'])
0058:         loc, conf, iou = loc.reshape(-1,14), conf.reshape(-1,2), iou.reshape(-1)
0059:         if not (len(loc) == len(conf) == len(iou) == len(self.priors)):
0060:             raise RuntimeError('Unexpected legacy YuNet output layout')
0061:         score = np.sqrt(conf[:,1] * np.clip(iou,0,1))
0062:         good = np.isfinite(score) & (score > self.score_threshold) & np.isfinite(loc).all(axis=1)
0063:         p, l, s = self.priors[good], loc[good], score[good]
0064:         if not len(p):
0065:             return np.empty((0,15), dtype=np.float32)
0066:         center = (p[:,:2] + l[:,:2] * np.float32(.1) * p[:,2:]) * self.size
0067:         size = p[:,2:] * np.exp(l[:,2:4] * np.array([.1,.2],np.float32)) * self.size
0068:         xy = center - size / 2
0069:         landmarks = (p[:,None,:2] + l[:,4:].reshape(-1,5,2) * np.float32(.1) * p[:,None,2:]) * self.size
0070:         faces = np.concatenate((xy,size,landmarks.reshape(-1,10),s[:,None]),axis=1).astype(np.float32)
0071:         boxes = faces[:,:4].astype(np.int32).tolist()
0072:         keep = cv2.dnn.NMSBoxes(boxes,s.tolist(),self.score_threshold,self.nms_threshold,eta=1.,top_k=self.top_k)
0073:         keep = np.asarray(keep,dtype=np.int32).reshape(-1)
0074:         faces = faces[keep].copy()
0075:         faces[:,[0,2,4,6,8,10,12]] *= w / self.size
0076:         faces[:,[1,3,5,7,9,11,13]] *= h / self.size
0077:         return faces
0078: 
0079: class SFaceCompat:
0080:     def __init__(self, model):
0081:         self.net = checked_net(model, SFACE_SHA256)
0082: 
0083:     @staticmethod
0084:     def align_crop(image, face):
0085:         bgr_image(image)
0086:         face = np.asarray(face,dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1)
0087:         if len(face) != 15 or not np.isfinite(face).all():
0088:             raise ValueError('Expected one finite YuNet face row with five landmarks')
0089:         src = face[4:14].reshape(5,2)
0090:         dst = np.array([[38.2946,51.6963],[73.5318,51.5014],[56.0252,71.7366],[41.5493,92.3655],[70.7299,92.2041]],np.float32)
0091:         src_mean = src.mean(axis=0)
0092:         dst_mean = np.array([56.0262,71.9008],np.float32)
0093:         src_d, dst_d = src - src_mean, dst - dst_mean
0094:         # Use float32 products as the C++ implementation does, then double SVD.
0095:         a = (dst_d[:,:,None] * src_d[:,None,:]).sum(axis=0,dtype=np.float64) / 5
0096:         u,s,vt = np.linalg.svd(a)
0097:         d = np.array([1., -1. if np.linalg.det(a) < 0 else 1.])
0098:         if s[1] <= s[0] * 2 * np.finfo(np.float32).tiny:
0099:             d[1] = 1. if np.linalg.det(u) * np.linalg.det(vt) > 0 else -1.
0100:         variance = (src_d * src_d).sum(dtype=np.float64) / 5
0101:         if variance <= 1e-10:
0102:             raise ValueError('Degenerate face landmarks')
0103:         scale = np.dot(s,d) / variance
0104:         rotation = u @ np.diag(d) @ vt
0105:         matrix = np.column_stack((scale * rotation, dst_mean - scale * (rotation @ src_mean)))
0106:         return cv2.warpAffine(image,matrix,(112,112),flags=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
0107: 
0108:     def feature(self, crop):
0109:         bgr_image(crop)
0110:         if crop.shape[:2] != (112,112):
0111:             raise ValueError('Expected an aligned 112x112 crop')
0112:         self.net.setInput(cv2.dnn.blobFromImage(crop,1.,(112,112),(0,0,0),True,False))
0113:         feature = self.net.forward().reshape(-1).astype(np.float32)
0114:         if feature.shape != (128,) or not np.isfinite(feature).all() or np.linalg.norm(feature) <= 1e-8:
0115:             raise RuntimeError('Invalid SFace embedding')
0116:         return feature
0117: 
0118:     @staticmethod
0119:     def match(feature1, feature2):
0120:         a,b = np.asarray(feature1,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1),np.asarray(feature2,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
0121:         if a.shape != (128,) or b.shape != (128,) or not np.isfinite(a).all() or not np.isfinite(b).all():
0122:             raise ValueError('Expected two finite 128-dimensional embeddings')
0123:         den = np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b)
0124:         if den <= 1e-12:
0125:             raise ValueError('Zero embedding')
0126:         return float(np.dot(a,b) / den)

FILE: supporting_source/aura/board/interaction/ui_contract.py

0001: """Validated display/operation adapter for I03/I04/I05/I07, not a model protocol.
0002: 
0003: All times here are milliseconds from THIS board's monotonic clock. No device,
0004: camera, database or register is opened by this module.
0005: """
0006: import copy
0007: import math
0008: import time
0009: 
0010: PROTOCOL = 'aura-ui-1'
0011: MODULE_STATES = {
0012:     'interaction': ('UNKNOWN', 'IDLE', 'LISTENING', 'PROCESSING', 'SPEAKING', 'CANCELLED', 'ERROR'),
0013:     'camera': ('UNKNOWN', 'READY', 'LOST', 'ERROR'),
0014:     'vision': ('UNKNOWN', 'READY', 'DEGRADED', 'ERROR'),
0015:     'npu': ('UNKNOWN', 'READY', 'DEGRADED', 'ERROR'),
0016:     'pl': ('UNKNOWN', 'READY', 'DEGRADED', 'FAULT'),
0017:     'target': ('UNKNOWN', 'NONE', 'CANDIDATE', 'CONFIRMED', 'LOST', 'AMBIGUOUS'),
0018:     'identity': ('UNKNOWN', 'KNOWN', 'ENROLLING', 'DELETED', 'ERROR'),
0019:     'motion': ('UNKNOWN', 'DISARMED', 'NEEDS_REFERENCE', 'ARMED', 'TRACKING', 'AT_LIMIT', 'FAULT'),
0020:     'host': ('UNKNOWN', 'OFFLINE', 'CONNECTING', 'READY', 'DEGRADED', 'FAILED'),
0021:     'audio': ('UNKNOWN', 'READY', 'RECORDING', 'PLAYING', 'UNAVAILABLE', 'ERROR'),
0022: }
0023: ACTIONS = ('record_start', 'record_stop', 'cancel', 'ask_frame', 'target_select',
0024:            'identity_enroll', 'identity_delete', 'motion_disarm', 'identity_sample_start', 'identity_sample_cancel')
0025: STATE_TEXT = {
0026:     'UNKNOWN': '状态未知', 'IDLE': '待机', 'LISTENING': '正在聆听',
0027:     'PROCESSING': '正在思考', 'SPEAKING': '正在播报', 'CANCELLED': '已取消',
0028:     'ERROR': '异常', 'READY': '就绪', 'LOST': '已失联', 'DEGRADED': '部分可用',
0029:     'NONE': '未选择', 'CANDIDATE': '候选对象', 'CONFIRMED': '已锁定',
0030:     'AMBIGUOUS': '存在歧义', 'KNOWN': '已识别', 'ENROLLING': '正在登记',
0031:     'DELETED': '已删除', 'DISARMED': '已禁能', 'NEEDS_REFERENCE': '等待参考确认',
0032:     'ARMED': '已使能', 'TRACKING': '正在随动', 'AT_LIMIT': '到达指令边界',
0033:     'FAULT': '故障', 'OFFLINE': '未连接', 'CONNECTING': '正在连接',
0034:     'FAILED': '不可用', 'RECORDING': '正在录音', 'PLAYING': '正在播放',
0035:     'UNAVAILABLE': '不可用',
0036: }
0037: 
0038: 
0039: def display_state(module, state):
0040:     if state is None:
0041:         return '待接入'
0042:     if module == 'identity' and state == 'UNKNOWN':
0043:         return '未识别'
0044:     return STATE_TEXT.get(state, '状态未知')
0045: 
0046: 
0047: def millis():
0048:     return int(time.monotonic() * 1000)
0049: 
0050: 
0051: def integer(value, minimum=0, maximum=2**53-1):
0052:     return type(value) is int and minimum <= value <= maximum
0053: 
0054: 
0055: def text(value, limit=160, empty=True):
0056:     return (isinstance(value, str) and len(value) <= limit
0057:             and (empty or bool(value.strip())) and '\x00' not in value)
0058: 
0059: 
0060: def require(condition, message):
0061:     if not condition:
0062:         raise ValueError(message)
0063: 
0064: 
0065: def target_key(value):
0066:     require(isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == {'camera_epoch', 'track_id', 'track_generation'},
0067:             '目标必须携带相机与轨迹代次')
0068:     require(text(value['camera_epoch'], 96, False) and integer(value['track_id'], 1)
0069:             and integer(value['track_generation'], 1), '目标生命周期无效')
0070:     return copy.deepcopy(value)
0071: 
0072: 
0073: def validate_snapshot(value, boot_id, now_ms):
0074:     require(isinstance(value, dict), '服务状态必须为对象')
0075:     required = {'protocol_version', 'board_boot_id', 'service_epoch', 'sequence',
0076:                 'observed_mono_ms', 'ttl_ms', 'source_kind', 'context', 'modules', 'capabilities'}
0077:     require(required <= set(value) and not set(value)-required-{'preview'}, '服务状态字段不完整或未知')
0078:     require(value['protocol_version'] == PROTOCOL, '界面接口版本不匹配')
0079:     require(value['board_boot_id'] == boot_id, '状态属于另一次板卡启动')
0080:     require(text(value['service_epoch'], 96, False), '缺少服务代次')
0081:     require(integer(value['sequence'], 1), '状态序号无效')
0082:     stamp, ttl = value['observed_mono_ms'], value['ttl_ms']
0083:     require(integer(stamp) and integer(ttl, 100, 5000), '状态时间或有效期无效')
0084:     require(stamp <= now_ms + 100 and now_ms < stamp + ttl, '服务状态已过期或不属于板端时钟')
0085:     require(value['source_kind'] in ('actual', 'fixture'), '必须注明真实或测试数据')
0086:     context = value['context']
0087:     require(isinstance(context, dict) and set(context) == {'session_id', 'request_id', 'generation'},
0088:             '交互归属字段不完整')
0089:     require(text(context['session_id'], 96, False) and text(context['request_id'], 96)
0090:             and integer(context['generation']), '交互归属无效')
0091:     caps = value['capabilities']
0092:     require(isinstance(caps, dict) and not set(caps)-set(ACTIONS)
0093:             and all(type(v) is bool for v in caps.values()), '能力列表无效')
0094:     modules = value['modules']
0095:     require(isinstance(modules, dict) and not set(modules)-set(MODULE_STATES), '未知状态模块')
0096:     for name, module in modules.items():
0097:         require(isinstance(module, dict) and module.get('state') in MODULE_STATES[name]
0098:                 and text(module.get('detail', '')), '模块状态无效：'+name)
0099:     interaction = modules.get('interaction', {})
0100:     transport = modules.get('camera',{}).get('preview_transport')
0101:     require(transport in (None,'aura-local-bgr-1'), '未知本机预览传输版本')
0102:     require(text(interaction.get('transcript', ''), 2000) and text(interaction.get('reply', ''), 8000),
0103:             '字幕长度无效')
0104:     timings = interaction.get('timings_ms', {})
0105:     require(isinstance(timings, dict) and not set(timings)-{'asr', 'vlm', 'tts', 'first_audio'}
0106:             and all(type(v) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(v) and 0 <= v <= 3600000

0254: class SnapshotGate:
0255:     """Retain latest state, reject rollback/replayed epochs, expire by source time."""
0256:     def __init__(self, boot_id, clock=millis):

0258:         self.value = None

0262:     def ingest(self, value):
0263:         value = validate_snapshot(value, self.boot_id, self.clock())
0264:         require(value['service_epoch'] not in self.retired, '旧服务代次不可恢复')
0265:         if self.value:
0266:             old = self.value
0267:             if old['service_epoch'] == value['service_epoch']:
0268:                 if value['sequence'] == old['sequence'] and value == old:
0269:                     return False
0270:                 require(value['sequence'] > old['sequence']
0271:                         and value['observed_mono_ms'] >= old['observed_mono_ms'], '状态序号或时间倒退')
0272:             else:
0273:                 require(len(self.retired) < 128, '服务换代过多，请重新连接')
0274:                 self.retired.add(old['service_epoch'])
0275:         self.value = value
0276:         return True
0277: 
0278:     def read(self):
0279:         value = self.value
0280:         if value is None or self.clock() >= value['observed_mono_ms']+value['ttl_ms']:
0281:             return None
0282:         result = copy.deepcopy(value)
0283:         if self.cancelled_context == (value['service_epoch'], value['context']):
0284:             module = result['modules'].setdefault('interaction', {})
0285:             module.update(state='CANCELLED', transcript='', reply='', detail='本轮已取消')
0286:             result['interaction_suppressed'] = True
0287:         return result
0288: 
0289:     def cancel(self):
0290:         if self.value:
0291:             self.cancelled_context = (self.value['service_epoch'], copy.deepcopy(self.value['context']))

0001: """Atomic RAM-backed latest BGR frame; explicit owner, size and source deadline.
0002: 
0003: Optional fast path for this board's camera adapter. No device is opened here.
0004: JPEG/HTTP remains available for other clients and explicit frame requests.
0005: """
0006: import json
0007: import os
0008: from pathlib import Path
0009: import re
0010: import stat
0011: import struct
0012: 
0013: MAGIC = b'AURABGR1'
0014: PREFIX = struct.Struct('!8sH')
0015: PIXELS = 640*480*3
0016: MAX_HEADER = 1024
0017: TRANSPORT = 'aura-local-bgr-1'
0018: 
0019: 
0020: def frame_path(owner, directory='/run'):
0021:     epoch = owner['camera_epoch']
0022:     if not isinstance(epoch,str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{32}',epoch):
0023:         raise ValueError('Invalid local camera epoch')
0024:     return Path(directory)/('aura-camera-ui-'+epoch+'.frame')
0025: 
0026: 
0027: def unpack(data, owner, now_ms):
0028:     if len(data)<PREFIX.size:raise ValueError('Truncated frame prefix')
0029:     magic,size = PREFIX.unpack_from(data)
0030:     if magic!=MAGIC or not 0<size<=MAX_HEADER or len(data)!=PREFIX.size+size+PIXELS:
0031:         raise ValueError('Invalid raw frame size/format')
0032:     header=json.loads(data[PREFIX.size:PREFIX.size+size])
0033:     if (not isinstance(header,dict) or set(header)!=set(owner)|{'frame_id','captured_mono_ms'}
0034:             or any(header.get(k)!=v for k,v in owner.items())
0035:             or type(header['frame_id']) is not int or not 0<header['frame_id']<2**53
0036:             or type(header['captured_mono_ms']) is not int
0037:             or not -100<=now_ms-header['captured_mono_ms']<250):
0038:         raise ValueError('Raw frame owner, sequence or deadline mismatch')
0039:     return header,data[PREFIX.size+size:]
0040: 
0041: 
0042: class RawFrameStore:
0043:     def __init__(self, owner, directory='/run'):
0044:         self.owner=dict(owner)
0045:         self.path=frame_path(owner,directory)
0046:         if self.path.exists() or self.path.is_symlink():raise FileExistsError(str(self.path))
0047:         self.inode=None
0048: 
0049:     def publish(self, fid, stamp, pixels):
0050:         if len(pixels)!=PIXELS:raise ValueError('Expected contiguous 640x480 BGR')
0051:         header=json.dumps(dict(self.owner,frame_id=fid,captured_mono_ms=stamp),separators=(',',':')).encode()
0052:         if len(header)>MAX_HEADER:raise ValueError('Raw frame header too large')
0053:         temp=self.path.with_suffix('.part')
0054:         descriptor=os.open(str(temp),os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600)
0055:         try:
0056:             with os.fdopen(descriptor,'wb') as stream:
0057:                 stream.write(PREFIX.pack(MAGIC,len(header)));stream.write(header);stream.write(pixels)
0058:             os.replace(str(temp),str(self.path))
0059:             self.inode=self.path.stat().st_ino
0060:         finally:
0061:             if temp.exists():temp.unlink()  # This call created this private temporary file.
0062: 
0063:     def close(self):
0064:         if self.inode is not None:
0065:             try:
0066:                 if self.path.lstat().st_ino==self.inode:self.path.unlink()
0067:             except FileNotFoundError:pass
0068: 
0069: 
0070: class RawFrameReader:
0071:     def __init__(self, owner, directory='/run'):
0072:         self.owner=dict(owner)
0073:         self.path=frame_path(owner,directory)
0074:         self.inode=None
0075:         self.previous=0
0076: 
0077:     def read(self, now_ms):
0078:         fd=os.open(str(self.path),os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NOFOLLOW)
0079:         with os.fdopen(fd,'rb') as stream:
0080:             info=os.fstat(stream.fileno())
0081:             if (not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid!=os.geteuid() or info.st_mode&0o077
0082:                     or not PREFIX.size<info.st_size<=PREFIX.size+MAX_HEADER+PIXELS):
0083:                 raise ValueError('Invalid local frame file')
0084:             version=(info.st_dev,info.st_ino,info.st_mtime_ns,info.st_size)
0085:             if version==self.inode:return None
0086:             data=stream.read(PREFIX.size+MAX_HEADER+PIXELS+1)
0087:         header,pixels=unpack(data,self.owner,now_ms)
0088:         if header['frame_id']<=self.previous:raise ValueError('Raw frame sequence rolled back')
0089:         self.inode,self.previous=version,header['frame_id']
0090:         return header,pixels

0051:     def update(self, people, fid, stamp, width, height):

0100:             velocity = (center-old['center']) / (stamp-old['stamp_ms']/1000.) if consecutive else 0.
0101:             row = {'track_id': tid, 'generation': generation, 'box': [x,y,w,h], 'score': float(t.score),
0102:                 'visible': True, 'confirmed': seen >= 2, 'frame_id': fid, 'captured_at': stamp,
0103:                 'velocity': [velocity, 0.], 'state': 'CONFIRMED' if seen >= 2 else 'NEW'}
0104:             current[tid] = row
0105:             self.history[tid] = {'generation': generation, 'seen': seen, 'index': self.update_index,
0106:                 'stamp_ms': now_ms, 'center': center}
0107:             if not consecutive:
0108:                 self.events.append({'event': 'REACQUIRED' if old else 'ENTERED', 'track_id': tid, 'generation': generation})
0109:         for tid in set(self.tracks) - set(current):
0110:             self.events.append({'event': 'LOST', 'track_id': tid, 'generation': self.tracks[tid]['generation']})
0111:         self.tracks = current
0112:         # Keep only the most recent 32 lost tracks. With at most 16 detections
0113:         # this also bounds the next exact-assignment matrix and memory.
0114:         lost=sorted(self.tracker.lost_stracks,key=lambda t:self.history.get(t.track_id,{}).get('stamp_ms',-1),reverse=True)
0115:         for t in lost[32:]:
0116:             t.mark_removed()
0117:             self.events.append({'event':'ENDED','track_id':int(t.track_id),'reason':'capacity'})
0118:         self.tracker.lost_stracks=lost[:32]
0119:         if self.selected_key:
0120:             tid, generation = self.selected_key
0121:             t = current.get(tid)
0122:             if not t or not t['confirmed'] or t['generation'] != generation:
0123:                 self.selected_key = None
0124:         confirmed = [t for t in current.values() if t['confirmed']]
0125:         if not self.manual_mode:
0126:             self.selected_key = ((confirmed[0]['track_id'], confirmed[0]['generation']) if len(confirmed) == 1 else None)
0127:         retained = {t.track_id for t in self.tracker.tracked_stracks + self.tracker.lost_stracks} | set(current)
0128:         self.history = {k:v for k,v in self.history.items() if k in retained}
0129:         if len(retained) > 48:
0130:             raise RuntimeError('ByteTrack retained capacity exceeded')
0131:         self.last_time, self.last_fid = stamp, fid
0132:         return {'tracks': list(current.values()), 'target_id': self.target_id,
0133:                 'association': self.kind, 'events': list(self.events)}

0001: """Calling-thread scheduler counters, opt-in and read-only. No device owners.
0002: 
0003: Linux 5.15 proc schedstat exposes sum_exec_runtime, sched_info.run_delay,
0004: sched_info.pcount; all-zero output means sched_info may be disabled. Counters
0005: bracket execute with small observer boundaries, not the pure hardware interval.
0006: """
0007: import threading
0008: import time
0009: import os
0010: try:
0011:     import resource
0012: except ImportError:
0013:     resource=None
0014: 
0015: 
0016: SAMPLE_FIELDS=('tid','runtime_ns','runqueue_ns','timeslices','voluntary','involuntary',
0017:                'sched_available','usage_available','read_wall_ns','slow_read')
0018: 
0019: 
0020: def missing_sample():
0021:     return {name:0 if name in ('sched_available','usage_available','slow_read') else -1
0022:             for name in SAMPLE_FIELDS}
0023: 
0024: 
0025: def read_schedstat():
0026:     with open('/proc/thread-self/schedstat','rb',buffering=0) as stream:raw=stream.read(257)
0027:     return parse_schedstat(raw)
0028: 
0029: 
0030: def parse_schedstat(raw):
0031:     if len(raw)>256:raise ValueError('Oversized calling-thread schedstat')
0032:     fields=raw.split()
0033:     if len(fields)!=3 or not all(value.isdigit() for value in fields):
0034:         raise ValueError('Invalid calling-thread schedstat')
0035:     values=tuple(int(value) for value in fields)
0036:     if any(value>=2**64 for value in values):raise ValueError('Invalid scheduler counter range')
0037:     return values
0038: 
0039: 
0040: class CallingThreadSchedReader:
0041:     """At most two read-only descriptors, each bound to its creating thread."""
0042:     def __init__(self):
0043:         self.lock=threading.Lock();self.fds={};self.closed=False
0044: 
0045:     def __call__(self):
0046:         tid=threading.get_native_id();key=(os.getpid(),threading.get_ident(),tid)
0047:         with self.lock:
0048:             if self.closed:raise RuntimeError('Thread counter reader closed')
0049:             if key not in self.fds:
0050:                 if len(self.fds)>=2:raise RuntimeError('Only the two optional task threads may be observed')
0051:                 self.fds[key]=os.open('/proc/thread-self/schedstat',os.O_RDONLY|os.O_CLOEXEC)
0052:             raw=os.pread(self.fds[key],257,0)
0053:         return parse_schedstat(raw)
0054: 
0055:     def close(self):
0056:         with self.lock:
0057:             self.closed=True
0058:             for fd in self.fds.values():os.close(fd)
0059:             self.fds.clear()
0060: 
0061: 
0062: def read_switches():
0063:     if resource is None:raise RuntimeError('Calling-thread resource counters unavailable')
0064:     usage=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_THREAD)
0065:     return usage.ru_nvcsw,usage.ru_nivcsw
0066: 
0067: 
0068: class ThreadSchedProbe:
0069:     def __init__(self,*,reader=None,usage_reader=read_switches,
0070:                  clock_ns=time.monotonic_ns,tid_reader=None,max_read_ns=2_000_000):
0071:         if ((reader is not None and not callable(reader)) or not callable(usage_reader) or not callable(clock_ns)
0072:                 or type(max_read_ns) is not int or not 100_000<=max_read_ns<=5_000_000):
0073:             raise ValueError('Invalid calling-thread observer')
0074:         self.owns_reader=reader is None
0075:         self.reader=CallingThreadSchedReader() if reader is None else reader
0076:         self.usage_reader,self.clock=usage_reader,clock_ns
0077:         self.tid_reader=tid_reader or getattr(threading,'get_native_id',lambda:-1)
0078:         self.max_read_ns=max_read_ns;self.lock=threading.Lock()
0079:         self.samples=self.sched_failures=self.usage_failures=self.slow_reads=0
0080:         self.disabled=self.closed=False;self.maximum_read_ns=0
0081: 
0082:     def sample(self):
0083:         with self.lock:
0084:             if self.disabled or self.closed:return missing_sample()
0085:         started=self.clock();value=missing_sample();sched_failed=usage_failed=0
0086:         try:
0087:             tid=self.tid_reader()
0088:             if type(tid) is int and tid>0:value['tid']=tid
0089:         except Exception:pass
0090:         try:
0091:             counters=self.reader()
0092:             if (len(counters)!=3 or any(type(v) is not int or not 0<=v<2**64 for v in counters)):
0093:                 raise ValueError('Invalid scheduler counters')
0094:             value.update(zip(('runtime_ns','runqueue_ns','timeslices'),counters))
0095:             # Do not interpret Linux's disabled all-zero output as zero delay.
0096:             value['sched_available']=int(any(counters))
0097:         except Exception:sched_failed=1
0098:         try:
0099:             counters=self.usage_reader()
0100:             if len(counters)!=2 or any(type(v) is not int or v<0 for v in counters):
0101:                 raise ValueError('Invalid switch counters')
0102:             value.update(zip(('voluntary','involuntary'),counters));value['usage_available']=1
0103:         except Exception:usage_failed=1
0104:         elapsed=self.clock()-started
0105:         value['read_wall_ns']=elapsed if type(elapsed) is int and elapsed>=0 else -1
0106:         value['slow_read']=int(elapsed>self.max_read_ns)
0107:         with self.lock:
0108:             self.samples+=1;self.sched_failures+=sched_failed;self.usage_failures+=usage_failed
0109:             self.maximum_read_ns=max(self.maximum_read_ns,value['read_wall_ns'])
0110:             self.slow_reads+=value['slow_read']
0111:             # One anomalously slow observation disables future reads. Its
0112:             # full cost remains in the original scheduler hard-cap interval.
0113:             if value['slow_read']:self.disabled=True
0114:         if value['slow_read'] and self.owns_reader:self.reader.close()
0115:         return value
0116: 
0117:     def close(self):
0118:         with self.lock:self.closed=True
0119:         if self.owns_reader:self.reader.close()
0120: 
0121:     @staticmethod
0122:     def delta(before,after):
0123:         same=(type(before.get('tid')) is int and before['tid']>0 and before['tid']==after.get('tid'))
0124:         result={'probe_same_thread':int(same),
0125:             'probe_read_wall_ns_total':sum(v['read_wall_ns'] for v in (before,after))
0126:                 if all(v['read_wall_ns']>=0 for v in (before,after)) else -1,
0127:             'probe_slow_read':int(any(v.get('slow_read') for v in (before,after)))}
0128:         for key,available in (('runtime_ns','sched_available'),('runqueue_ns','sched_available'),
0129:                 ('timeslices','sched_available'),('voluntary','usage_available'),('involuntary','usage_available')):
0130:             a,b=before.get(key,-1),after.get(key,-1)
0131:             result['probe_execute_'+key+'_delta']=(b-a if same
0132:                 and before.get(available)==after.get(available)==1
0133:                 and type(a) is int and type(b) is int and b>=a>=0 else -1)
0134:         return result
0135: 
0136:     def snapshot(self):
0137:         with self.lock:
0138:             return {'enabled':True,'disabled_after_slow_read':self.disabled,'closed':self.closed,'samples':self.samples,
0139:                 'schedstat_read_failures':self.sched_failures,'usage_read_failures':self.usage_failures,
0140:                 'slow_reads':self.slow_reads,'maximum_read_ns':self.maximum_read_ns,
0141:                 'slow_read_limit_ns':self.max_read_ns,'missing_value':-1,
0142:                 'scope':'calling thread; scheduler counter interval surrounds execute wall interval; not DPU busy time',
0143:                 'zero_counter_scope':'all-zero schedstat is unavailable, not proof of no scheduling delay',
0144:                 'observer_cost_scope':'before/after reads remain inside original optional hard cap; never subtracted'}
0145: 
0146: 
0147: class ThreadSwitchProbe:
0148:     """Small getrusage-only fallback. It makes no runqueue-time claim."""
0149:     def __init__(self,*,usage_reader=read_switches,clock_ns=time.monotonic_ns,
0150:                  tid_reader=None,max_read_ns=2_000_000,thread_clock_ns=getattr(time,'thread_time_ns',None),
0151:                  max_cpu_read_ns=500_000):
0152:         self.usage_reader,self.clock=usage_reader,clock_ns
0153:         self.tid_reader=tid_reader or getattr(threading,'get_native_id',lambda:-1)
0154:         self.max_read_ns=max_read_ns;self.samples=self.failures=self.maximum_read_ns=0
0155:         self.thread_clock,self.max_cpu_read_ns=thread_clock_ns,max_cpu_read_ns
0156:         self.maximum_cpu_read_ns=self.wall_late_samples=0
0157:         self.disabled=self.closed=False;self.lock=threading.Lock()
0158: 
0159:     def sample(self):
0160:         with self.lock:
0161:             if self.closed or self.disabled:
0162:                 return {'tid':-1,'voluntary':-1,'involuntary':-1,'read_wall_ns':-1,'read_cpu_ns':-1,'slow_read':0}
0163:         started=self.clock();tid=-1;voluntary=involuntary=-1;failed=0
0164:         try:cpu_started=self.thread_clock() if callable(self.thread_clock) else -1
0165:         except Exception:cpu_started=-1
0166:         try:
0167:             tid=self.tid_reader();values=self.usage_reader()
0168:             if type(tid) is not int or tid<1 or len(values)!=2 or any(type(v) is not int or v<0 for v in values):
0169:                 raise ValueError('Invalid calling-thread switch counters')
0170:             voluntary,involuntary=values
0171:         except Exception:tid=-1;failed=1
0172:         elapsed=self.clock()-started
0173:         try:cpu_end=self.thread_clock() if callable(self.thread_clock) else -1
0174:         except Exception:cpu_end=-1
0175:         cpu_elapsed=cpu_end-cpu_started if cpu_end>=cpu_started>=0 else -1
0176:         slow=int(elapsed>self.max_read_ns)
0177:         with self.lock:
0178:             self.samples+=1;self.failures+=failed;self.maximum_read_ns=max(self.maximum_read_ns,elapsed)
0179:             self.maximum_cpu_read_ns=max(self.maximum_cpu_read_ns,cpu_elapsed)
0180:             self.wall_late_samples+=slow
0181:             # A low-CPU wall pause is retained as an observation, not silently
0182:             # counted as observer computation. All wall time still contributes
0183:             # to the unchanged optional hard cap; late samples remain flagged.
0184:             if cpu_elapsed>self.max_cpu_read_ns or (slow and cpu_elapsed<0):self.disabled=True
0185:         return {'tid':tid,'voluntary':voluntary,'involuntary':involuntary,
0186:             'read_wall_ns':elapsed,'read_cpu_ns':cpu_elapsed,'slow_read':slow}
0187: 
0188:     @staticmethod
0189:     def delta(before,after):
0190:         same=type(before.get('tid')) is int and before['tid']>0 and before['tid']==after.get('tid')
0191:         result={'probe_same_thread':int(same),'probe_slow_read':int(bool(before.get('slow_read') or after.get('slow_read')))}
0192:         for key in ('voluntary','involuntary'):
0193:             a,b=before.get(key,-1),after.get(key,-1)
0194:             result['probe_execute_'+key+'_delta']=b-a if same and type(a) is int and type(b) is int and b>=a>=0 else -1
0195:         a,b=before.get('read_wall_ns',-1),after.get('read_wall_ns',-1)
0196:         result['probe_read_wall_ns_total']=a+b if a>=0 and b>=0 else -1
0197:         a,b=before.get('read_cpu_ns',-1),after.get('read_cpu_ns',-1)
0198:         result['probe_read_cpu_ns_total']=a+b if a>=0 and b>=0 else -1
0199:         return result
0200: 
0201:     def close(self):
0202:         with self.lock:self.closed=True
0203: 
0204:     def snapshot(self):
0205:         with self.lock:
0206:             return {'enabled':True,'mode':'CALLING_THREAD_CONTEXT_SWITCHES_ONLY','runqueue_time_collected':False,
0207:                 'samples':self.samples,'read_failures':self.failures,'maximum_read_ns':self.maximum_read_ns,
0208:                 'maximum_cpu_read_ns':self.maximum_cpu_read_ns,'wall_late_samples':self.wall_late_samples,
0209:                 'disabled_after_slow_read':self.disabled,'closed':self.closed,'slow_read_limit_ns':self.max_read_ns,
0210:                 'cpu_read_limit_ns':self.max_cpu_read_ns,
0211:                 'stop_rule':'CPU read >0.5ms; wall read >2ms if CPU clock missing; low-CPU late wall reads stay flagged',
0212:                 'scope':'calling thread switch counts only; cannot time CPU runqueue or identify GIL/native root cause',
0213:                 'observer_cost_scope':'original optional hard cap includes all observation work; never subtracted'}
