0001: """Pinned Vitis AI 3.0 YOLOX-Nano person detector for the current KV260 DPU.
0002: 
0003: The native call releases Python's GIL. The service supplies its latest BGR
0004: frame; this module never opens a camera or changes the FPGA configuration.
0005: """
0006: import ctypes
0007: import fcntl
0008: import hashlib
0009: import json
0010: import math
0011: from pathlib import Path
0012: import subprocess
0013: import threading

0015: from common.contracts import Detection

0017: 
0018: def checked_file(path, expected):
0019:     path = Path(path)
0020:     if not isinstance(expected, str) or len(expected) != 64:
0021:         raise ValueError('A SHA256 is required for every model component')
0022:     if not path.is_file() or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != expected:
0023:         raise RuntimeError('YOLO component hash mismatch: ' + str(path))
0024:     return path.resolve()
0025: 
0026: 
0027: def verify_architecture(model, expected):
0028:     import xir
0029:     graph = xir.Graph.deserialize(str(model))
0030:     subgraphs = [s for s in graph.get_root_subgraph().toposort_child_subgraph()
0031:                  if s.has_attr('device') and s.get_attr('device') == 'DPU']
0032:     if len(subgraphs) != 1:
0033:         raise RuntimeError('Expected exactly one YOLO DPU subgraph')
0034:     subgraph = subgraphs[0]
0035:     fingerprint = hex(int(subgraph.get_attr('dpu_fingerprint')))
0036:     if fingerprint != expected or subgraph.get_attr('dpu_name') != 'DPUCZDX8G_ISA1_B4096':
0037:         raise RuntimeError('YOLO model architecture mismatch')
0038:     inputs, outputs = list(subgraph.get_input_tensors()), list(subgraph.get_output_tensors())
0039:     if (len(inputs) != 1 or list(inputs[0].dims) != [1, 416, 416, 3]
0040:             or inputs[0].get_attr('fix_point') != -2
0041:             or sorted(tuple(t.dims) for t in outputs) != [(1, 13, 13, 85), (1, 26, 26, 85), (1, 52, 52, 85)]
0042:             or any(str(t.dtype) != 'xint8' for t in inputs + outputs)):
0043:         raise RuntimeError('Unexpected YOLOX-Nano tensor layout')
0044:     response = subprocess.run(['xdputil', 'query'], stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
0045:                               stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, timeout=15, check=True)
0046:     kernels = json.loads(response.stdout).get('kernels', [])
0047:     if len(kernels) != 1 or kernels[0].get('fingerprint') != expected:
0048:         raise RuntimeError('Live DPU fingerprint does not match the YOLO model')
0049:     return fingerprint

0051: 
0052: def original_person_boxes(rows, width, height, threshold=.3, nms_iou=.45):
0053:     """Validate, clip and bound the native original-image xyxy results."""
0054:     detections = []
0055:     for row in rows:
0056:         if len(row) != 6 or not all(math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in row):
0057:             raise RuntimeError('Non-finite or malformed native YOLO result')
0058:         x1, y1, x2, y2, score, label = map(float, row)
0059:         if label != 0 or not 0 <= score <= 1:
0060:             raise RuntimeError('Unexpected native YOLO class or score')
0061:         if score < threshold:
0062:             continue
0063:         x1, y1 = max(0., min(width, x1)), max(0., min(height, y1))
0064:         x2, y2 = max(0., min(width, x2)), max(0., min(height, y2))
0065:         if x2 > x1 and y2 > y1:
0066:             detections.append(Detection((x1, y1, x2 - x1, y2 - y1), score, 'person'))
0067:     # The supplied 0.65 NMS can retain nested boxes on a single person. Use an
0068:     # explicit, stricter person-only NMS for the UI count and association.
0069:     retained = []
0070:     for candidate in sorted(detections, key=lambda d: d.score, reverse=True):
0071:         x, y, w, h = candidate.box
0072:         duplicate = False
0073:         for previous in retained:
0074:             px, py, pw, ph = previous.box
0075:             overlap = max(0., min(x+w, px+pw)-max(x, px)) * max(0., min(y+h, py+ph)-max(y, py))
0076:             if overlap / max(w*h+pw*ph-overlap, 1e-9) > nms_iou:
0077:                 duplicate = True
0078:                 break
0079:         if not duplicate:
0080:             retained.append(candidate)
0081:             if len(retained) == 16:break
0082:     return retained

0085: class DpuYoloX:
0086:     backend_kind = 'DPU'
0087:     kind = 'DPU_YOLOX_NANO_PERSON'
0088: 
0089:     def __init__(self, config):
0090:         self.config = dict(config)
0091:         self.handle = None
0092:         self.ownership = None
0093:         self.lock = threading.Lock()
0094:         model = checked_file(config['model'], config['model_sha256'])
0095:         checked_file(model.with_suffix('.prototxt'), config['model_config_sha256'])
0096:         library = checked_file(config['native_library'], config['native_sha256'])
0097:         if (config.get('classes') != ['person'] or config.get('score_threshold') not in (.1, .3)
0098:                 or config.get('person_nms_iou', .45) != .45):
0099:             raise ValueError('Only reviewed person detection/tracking thresholds are supported')
0100:         self.fingerprint = verify_architecture(model, config['arch_fingerprint'])
0101:         # Also coordinate the project's generic DPU adapter and UI adapter.
0102:         self.ownership = Path('/run/aura-person-dpu.lock').open('a+')
0103:         try:
0104:             fcntl.flock(self.ownership, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
0105:             native = ctypes.CDLL(str(library))
0106:             native.aura_yolox_abi.restype = ctypes.c_int
0107:             if native.aura_yolox_abi() != 1:
0108:                 raise RuntimeError('YOLO native ABI mismatch')
0109:             native.aura_yolox_error.restype = ctypes.c_char_p
0110:             native.aura_yolox_create.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p]
0111:             native.aura_yolox_create.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
0112:             native.aura_yolox_destroy.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p]
0113:             native.aura_yolox_destroy.restype = None
0114:             native.aura_yolox_detect.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p,
0115:                 ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_float),
0116:                 ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int)]
0117:             native.aura_yolox_detect.restype = ctypes.c_int
0118:             self.native = native
0119:             self.handle = native.aura_yolox_create(str(model).encode())
0120:             if not self.handle:
0121:                 raise RuntimeError(self._error())
0122:         except BaseException:
0123:             self.close()
0124:             raise
0125: 
0126:     def _error(self):
0127:         return (self.native.aura_yolox_error() or b'Native YOLO failure').decode(errors='replace')
0128: 
0129:     def verify_runtime(self):
0130:         return self.fingerprint
0131: 
0132:     def detect_bgr(self, image):
0133:         import numpy as np
0134:         if image.dtype != np.uint8 or image.ndim != 3 or image.shape[2] != 3:
0135:             raise ValueError('YOLO expects uint8 BGR image data')
0136:         height, width = image.shape[:2]
0137:         if not 0 < width <= 4096 or not 0 < height <= 4096:
0138:             raise ValueError('YOLO image size out of range')
0139:         image = np.ascontiguousarray(image)
0140:         output = (ctypes.c_float * (16 * 6))()
0141:         count = ctypes.c_int()
0142:         with self.lock:
0143:             if not self.handle:
0144:                 raise RuntimeError('YOLO detector is closed')
0145:             status = self.native.aura_yolox_detect(self.handle, image.ctypes.data,
0146:                 width, height, image.strides[0], output, 16, 0, ctypes.byref(count))
0147:             if status != 0:
0148:                 raise RuntimeError(self._error())
0149:             if not 0 <= count.value <= 16:
0150:                 raise RuntimeError('Native YOLO output exceeds capacity')
0151:         rows = [list(output[i * 6:i * 6 + 6]) for i in range(count.value)]
0152:         return original_person_boxes(rows, width, height, self.config['score_threshold'], self.config.get('person_nms_iou', .45))
0153: 
0154:     def detect(self, frame):
0155:         if frame.ref.timestamp_semantics == 'synthetic':
0156:             raise ValueError('DPU adapter requires a real captured frame')
0157:         return self.detect_bgr(frame.image)
0158: 
0159:     def close(self):
0160:         with self.lock:
0161:             if self.handle:
0162:                 self.native.aura_yolox_destroy(self.handle)
0163:                 self.handle = None
0164:             if self.ownership:
0165:                 self.ownership.close()
0166:                 self.ownership = None
0167: 
0168: 
0169: def create(config):
0170:     return DpuYoloX(config)

0017: class Embedder:
0018:     def __init__(self,denials=2,hook=None):
0019:         self.denials=denials;self.hook=hook;self.calls=0;self.pixels=[];self.closed=False
0020:     def feature(self,crop,**kw):
0021:         self.calls+=1;self.pixels.append(int(crop[0,0,0]))
0022:         if self.hook:self.hook(self.calls)
0023:         if self.calls<=self.denials:raise DeferredIdentity('synthetic slot denial',retryable=True)
0024:         return vector()
0025:     def close(self):self.closed=True
0026:     def health(self):return {'calls':self.calls}

0017: 
0018:     def test_counter_delta_matches_calling_thread_and_stays_numeric(self):
0019:         values=iter([(100,20,3),(400,90,5)]);switches=iter([(4,5),(5,8)])
0020:         p=self.probe(reader=lambda:next(values),usage=lambda:next(switches))

0069:     def test_state_lock_contention_after_notification_does_not_cancel_fresh_native(self):
0070:         h=self.make();h.deny();locked=threading.Event();release=threading.Event()
0071:         def hold_state():
0072:             with h.service.lock:locked.set();release.wait(1.)
0073:         thread=threading.Thread(target=hold_state)
0074:         original=h.runner._diagnostic_mark
0075:         def marker(phase,status=None):
0076:             original(phase,status)
0077:             if phase=='execute_start':thread.start();self.assertTrue(locked.wait(.5))
0078:             elif phase=='wait_end':release.set()
0079:         h.runner._diagnostic_mark=marker
0080:         h.schedule.condition.wait=lambda timeout:(h.clock.advance(.020),h.allow())
0081:         try:h.run(1)
0082:         finally:
0083:             release.set()
0084:             if thread.ident is not None:thread.join(.5)
0085:         self.assertEqual(h.native.completed,1)
0086:         self.assertEqual(h.service.retry_health()['busy'],0)
0087:         self.assertEqual(h.service.retry_health()['withdrawn_after_slot'],0)
0088: 
0089:     def test_close_token_is_visible_even_while_other_thread_holds_state_lock(self):
0090:         h=self.make();locked=threading.Event();release=threading.Event();closed=threading.Event()
0091:         def hold_state():
0092:             with h.service.lock:locked.set();release.wait(1.)
0093:         holder=threading.Thread(target=hold_state)
0094:         closer=threading.Thread(target=lambda:(h.service.close(),closed.set()))
0095:         original=h.runner._diagnostic_mark
0096:         def marker(phase,status=None):
0097:             original(phase,status)
0098:             if phase=='execute_start':
0099:                 holder.start();self.assertTrue(locked.wait(.5));closer.start()
0100:                 self.assertTrue(h.service._admission_cancelled.wait(.5))
0101:             elif phase=='exception':release.set()
0102:         h.runner._diagnostic_mark=marker
0103:         try:h.run(1)
0104:         finally:
0105:             release.set()
0106:             for thread in (holder,closer):
0107:                 if thread.ident is not None:thread.join(.5)
0108:         self.assertTrue(closed.is_set())
0109:         self.assertEqual(h.native.submitted,0)
0110:         self.assertEqual(h.service.retry_health()['closed'],1)
0111:         self.assertFalse(h.schedule.disabled('yunet'))

0117:         with self.assertRaises(ValueError):FaceTargetService(None,None,'x',independent_admission=True)
0118:         service=FaceTargetService(None,None,'x',slot_retry=True)
0119:         self.assertFalse(service.independent_admission)
